Calm Help Guide

Calm’s own help documentation, gathered into one document

2026-08-02

Calm Help Guide

This guide gathers Calm’s own help documentation, as it stood on 2 August 2026.

How this was gathered. Calm’s rendered help pages refuse automated reading; its own listing answered. All 231 articles arrived carrying their full text.

The arrangement is Calm’s own, one level down. Calm files its articles under seven categories, but two of those hold seventy-six and sixty-five articles apiece, which is a shelf rather than a category. Calm also divides them into forty sections, and those are used here.

This is not medical advice. Calm is a meditation and sleep app, and some of what follows touches on sleep difficulty, anxiety and mood. Every word here is Calm’s own description of how its product works. None of it is clinical guidance, none of it is a substitute for talking to a doctor, and nothing here should be read as treatment for anything.

The guide covers the Calm app, Calm Sleep, Calm Health, and Calm provided through an employer or insurer, along with subscriptions and account management.

This document holds 29 categories, 231 articles and about 73,228 words.

Calm - Who We Are

Calm’s mission is to support everyone on every step of their mental health journey.  

Calm is a mental health company with the #1 app to help you sleep more, stress less and live mindfully, available in both the App Store and Google Play store.

Launched in 2012 by Alex Tew and Michael Acton Smith, Calm was named as Apple’s  2017 iPhone App of the Year; by Google Play as an “Editors’ Choice” in 2018; and found by a major study of 200,000 iPhone users to be “the world’s elated app” – the single app that left users feeling happiest from spending time on it.

Calm offers a large library of content and tools to help people get better sleep, manage stress or anxious feelings, practice gratitude, build a mindfulness practice, and so much more. . One of the most popular offerings on  Calm is ‘The Daily Calm’, a 10-minute meditation which explores a fresh mindful theme and inspiring concept each day. Calm is also the creator of the Sleep Story (audio bedtime stories for all ages) and has more than 500+ Sleep Stories today, in addition to a large library of meditations, mindfulness exercises, soundscapes, music, and more delivered by experts and beloved voices.

Calm is free to download, but to unlock the entire Calm library, you’ll need a Calm Premium subscription. 

The heart of Calm is digital, but Calm is also expanding offline in various ways to support people on their mental health and sleep journeys. To learn more, visit calm.com and follow us on all channels @calm.

Calm in Different Languages

Which languages are available?

The Calm app currently offers full text and audio support for:

  • English
  • Spanish (Español)
  • German (Deutsch)
  • French (Français)
  • Korean (한국어)
  • Portuguese (Português)
  • Japanese (日本語)
  • Italian (Italiano)
  • Mandarin Chinese (繁體中文)
  • Polish (Polski)

How to Access Audio in Other Languages

You must change your app’s language setting to listen to translated content.

  • If your app is set to English, you will only see and hear English content.
  • To listen to Spanish (or any other language) Meditations and Sleep Stories, you must follow the steps below to switch the app’s language.

How to Change Your App Language

  1. Open the Calmapp.
  2. Tap Profile > Settings (Gear Icon).
  3. Tap ChangeLanguage.
  4. Select your language from the list.
  5. The app will reload and the audio library will update.

Contact Calm Support: Submit a Ticket or Chat with Us

We do not offer phone support. To ensure your request reaches the right team (Consumer, Health, or Business), please use one of the two methods below.

Method 1: The Help Widget (Fastest) The quickest way to find answers or contact a human agent is via our Help Widget.

  1. Look for the “Support” or “Chat” icon in the bottom right corner of this screen.

  2. Type your question (e.g., “Refund” or “Login Issue”).

  3. The AI assistant will offer immediate solutions.

  4. If the AI assistant is unable to assist, it will create a ticket and route your request to our support team.

Method 2: The “Submit a Request” Form If you prefer to fill out a detailed form:

  1. Click “Submit a request” at the bottom of this page.

  2. Select your issue: Use the dropdown menu to choose your topic (e.g., Billing, Technical Trouble, Calm Health, Business Account).

  3. Fill in details: Provide as much information as possible and screenshots (if applicable)

  4. Submit: Our team will review your ticket and respond via email.

How to Verify We Received Your Request

  • The Auto-Reply: Within minutes of submitting your form or chat message, you must receive an automated email confirmation with a Ticket Number.

  • No Email? If you do not see this confirmation, we did not receive your message.

  • Troubleshooting: Check your Spam/Junk folder. If it is missing, please resubmit your request and double-check that your email address is spelled correctly.

How to Contact Calm Support

At Calm, we know your time is valuable. To provide you with the fastest, most secure, and most accurate assistance possible, our dedicated Support team operates entirely via email.

While we do not offer a customer service phone number, our digital-first approach allows us to deliver a superior support experience. Here is why we have chosen to assist you via email:

The Benefits of Email AssistanceClear, Clickable Instructions: Phone agents can only describe where to click, which can be confusing. Through email, our team can provide direct, clickable links that take you straight to the exact account settings, billing portals, or specific meditations you need.

Accurate Technical Troubleshooting: It is often difficult to describe a technical bug out loud. Through email, you can send us screenshots or screen recordings of exactly what is happening on your device, allowing our tech team to identify and resolve the issue much faster.

A Written Record: You will always have a complete written copy of our troubleshooting steps and solutions right in your inbox, allowing you to refer back to them anytime you need.

Enhanced Privacy & Security: Handling account details, subscription receipts, and billing matters in writing ensures a higher level of privacy and security compared to reading sensitive information out loud over the phone.

How to Contact Us

We are here to help! To ensure your request reaches the right team quickly, please use one of the two methods below:

  • Method 1: The Help Widget (Fastest) The quickest way to find answers or escalate your request is via the Help Widget located in the bottom right corner of this screen.

  • Method 2: Submit a Request Form If you prefer to fill out a detailed form directly, you can submit an email ticket to our team here. Please be sure to include the email address associated with your Calm account and any relevant screenshots so we can assist you right away!

Pro Tip: Check Your Spam Folder!

If you have submitted a ticket but haven’t seen a reply from our team yet, please be sure to check your Spam or Junk email folders. Sometimes our responses get accidentally filtered there by your email provider!

How to Join the Daily Calm Community Facebook Group

The Daily Calm Community is a welcoming Facebook group dedicated to discussing mindfulness practices and exploring the themes of our daily meditations. Our goal is to make this group one of the kindest and most inspiring places on the internet!

Who Can Join?

Everyone is welcome! While our core members are Calm subscribers who listen to the Daily Calm, you do not need an active subscription to participate. Even if you haven’t started using the Calm app yet, the community is a wonderful place to learn more about mindfulness and connect with others.

What to Expect in the Group

The Daily Calm Community is designed to help you deepen your awareness and equanimity. It is a supportive space to:

  • Discuss the topics featured in the daily meditations.

  • Cultivate a sense of connection based on mindfulness.

  • Nurture a calm place within and celebrate the beauty that surrounds us.

How to Join

To become a member of the community, simply click the link below to submit your request!

  • Join the Daily Calm Community

Note: Because this group is hosted on Facebook, you will need an active Facebook account to submit a join request and participate in the discussions.

How to Submit Feedback or Suggestions to Calm

We are always looking for ways to improve, and your input is essential to us! If you have an idea or suggestion for the app, we would love to hear it.

What kind of feedback can I submit?

We welcome all types of suggestions, including:

  • Ideas for new app features or tools

  • Suggestions for future Sleep Story narrators

  • Requests for new meditation topics or music genres

  • General feedback about your experience using Calm

How to Share Your Ideas

To share your thoughts with our team, please submit them through our Feedback and Suggestions Form and select I want to request content or suggest feedback.

Our product and content teams review these submissions regularly. We truly appreciate your support and suggestions as we work to make the app even better!

Note: If you are experiencing a technical issue or need help with your subscription, please do not use the feedback form. Instead, contact our support team directly here and select Something’s not working right so we can assist you right away.

Is Calm Backed by Science? Research & Clinical Studies

Calm is an evidence-based leader in the mobile app, health, and fitness industry, that leverages science to improve mental fitness and help people live a happier and healthier lifestyle.

Calm Science is integrated across Calm’s offerings, using evidence-based strategies to inform our product. Calm’s ongoing research continues to reinforce and strengthen Calm as an innovative and scientifically grounded product that improves mental and physical health.

Learn more about our Calm Science team and rigorous approach to scientific research on our Calm Science page here.

Meet the Calm Instructors (Meditation Teachers & Guest Experts)

At Calm, we are proud to offer a wide variety of voices, teaching styles, and mindfulness techniques to support your practice. From our daily guides to our specialized guest experts, here is a look at the creators behind our meditation library:

Our Primary Guides

  • Tamara Levitt: As the Head of Mindfulness at Calm, Tamara has written and recorded most of the core meditation sessions available in the Calm app, including The Daily Calm. She is a long-time Mindfulness Instructor with over 20 years of experience in the field. You can learn more about her on the Calm blog here.

  • John Armstrong: If you prefer a different voice, you can switch the narrator of our 7-Day Meditation programs (and a few select series) to John Armstrong for a new experience. For detailed information on how to change narrators during a session, please click here here. (Note: This feature is only available on the Calm mobile app).

The Daily Programs

In addition to Tamara Levitt’s Daily Calm, we offer daily mindfulness sessions from world-renowned guides:

  • Jay Shetty: Former monk, #1 New York Times best-selling author, and life & purpose coach. In The Daily Jay, Shetty brings together meditation, storytelling, wisdom, and actionable insights to help you live a less anxious, more meaningful life.

  • Jeff Warren: Meditation instructor and journalist celebrated for his dynamic and accessible style of teaching. Jeff leads The Daily Trip (quirky, accessible daily sessions) as well as the popular How to Meditate 30-day program.

How to Find Guest Instructors in the App

We also offer a curated collection of meditations recorded by specialized guest instructors. To browse their content:

  1. Tap Discover from the Calm home screen.

  2. Select the Meditation button.

  3. Scroll through the category tabs at the top of the page and select Guest Instructors.

Meet Our Guest Experts

  • LeBron James: The three-time basketball champion and MVP opens up his playbook for mental fitness. In Train your Mind, LeBron describes what it means to be mentally fit, how to manage emotions under pressure, and the importance of sleep.

  • Tara Brach: Author and meditation instructor who teaches self-compassion. Her programs include Saying Yes to Life, Letting Go into Sleep, and Relaxed Open-Hearted Presence.

  • Oren Jay Sofer: Author and communication trainer. His programs include Rhythm of the Train, Deep Rest, and 7 Days of Soothing Pain.

  • Shinzen Young: Meditation teacher and neuroscience research consultant. His programs include Deconstructing Performance Anxiety, Untangling Physical Pain, and Mindfulness in Daily Life.

  • Dr. Elisha Goldstein: Co-founder of The Center for Mindful Living. His programs include Anxiety Release, Restful Sleep, and Easing Depression.

  • Mallika Chopra: Author of mindfulness books for youth. She created Mindfulness with Mallika, a series of exercises specifically for kids and teens.

Curious why we decided to expand our library and offer programs from multiple meditation instructors? Watch a message from Tamara on our Facebook page here.

A Warning About Phishing Scams

Please be advised that we have received reports of fraudulent emails, which appear to come from Calm and/or Calm Health, but are actually generated by malicious actors seeking to gather personal information.

These phishing scams are requesting individuals to click on an attachment that is fraudulently disguised as an invoice and/or asking to call a fake phone number.

To check whether or not an email is from Calm, please take a look at the sender’s email. Calm sends emails from the following:

  • no-reply@sending.mailapps.org

We Are Not Affiliated with Calm Radio, Calm Supplement, My Calm Blanket, or Calm Box

Please note: The Calm app and our Help Center will not connect you with Calm Radio, Calm supplements, My Calm Blanket, or Calm Box. Calm.com is not associated with these other companies in any way.  If you have questions about Calm Radio or any other companies like the ones mentioned above, please reach out to them directly.

About Calm Health

Calm Health FAQs

Find answers to frequently asked questions about Calm Health, how it works, and how to get the most out of your experience.

What is Calm Health?

Calm Health is an invitation-only app available through your health insurance plan, physician/care team, or employer. It gives you access to digital mental health programs created by psychologists to support specific conditions and mental health needs, along with a curated selection of popular Calm content. 

Calm Health’s programs are based on therapeutic approaches including cognitive behavioral therapy (CBT), acceptance and commitment therapy (ACT), and dialectical behavior therapy (DBT).

How to get started:

  1. Take a quick mental health screening based on how you are feeling.
  2. Based on your screening results and self-reported input, follow a personalized Plan to support your condition and mental health needs.
  3. See how your mental health changes over time.

Please note: Some features may not be available in your region or through your sponsor.

 

What is the mental health screening?

The mental health screening is a short survey that personalizes your Calm Health Plan based on how you are feeling. It asks about your thoughts and feelings over the past two weeks. The screening is based on two clinically validated, industry-standard tools: the PHQ-9 and GAD-7. For more information about the screening and these tools, visit our Calm Health Product FAQs.

 

Why should I fill out the screening?

The mental health screening helps personalize your Plan and keeps your recommendations relevant as your needs change. We recommend retaking the screening every four weeks to track your progress over time. You will also receive a reminder in your account when it’s time to retake it.

 

How is Calm Health different from the Calm app? The Calm app is open to everyone and is focused on sleep, relaxation, and daily mindfulness, with a vast library of content including soothing soundscapes, guided meditations, and celebrity-narrated Sleep Stories.

Calm Health, available through select health insurance plans, physician/care teams, and employers, is designed to support a variety of health and life experiences. Calm Health’s personalized Plans guide you to evidence-based programs and resources most relevant to your needs. Calm Health also includes the majority of content from the Calm app, such as Sleep Stories, meditations, and more.

Calm Health is built to comply with HITRUST and HIPAA privacy and security standards and can be integrated into care programs.  

I’m a paid Calm subscriber. Does that give me access to Calm Health?

No, Calm Health is only available through a sponsorship from a health insurance plan, physician/care team, or employer. A paid Calm Premium subscription does not include access to Calm Health.

 

If I already have a Calm account, do I need a separate username and password to access Calm Health?

No. You can use the same Calm username and password to access both Calm and Calm Health.

 

I’m working with a therapist currently. Can I still use Calm Health?

Yes. Calm Health is designed to supplement other forms of support and is not intended as a substitute for care by a physician, therapist, or other mental health care provider.  

How do I share my feedback or suggest a feature?

We would love to hear from you. Reach out to our Support team with eligibility questions, feature enhancements, or app feedback:

  • Calm Health members: Click here
  • Optum/UHC members: Click here
