Account Management

Calm Health Access: What Happens When Your Sponsorship Ends

If your Calm Health sponsorship through your healthcare provider or employer ends, you can still access your Calm Health account and data.

When you log in to the Calm Health app or website, you’ll see a screen notifying you that your Calm Health sponsorship has expired. From this screen, you can:

  • Update your sponsorship: Add new sponsor details, if applicable.
  • Log out: Safely exit your account. Your data stays intact, and you can log back in at any time to choose one of the other options below.
  • Delete your account: Permanently delete your account and data.
  • Convert your account: Switch to an unsponsored experience of Calm Health.

How do I update my sponsorship?

If you have a new healthcare provider offering a Calm Health sponsorship, select Have a new sponsor? from the home screen in the app or website and follow the prompts. For detailed instructions, see How to Change Your Sponsor for Calm Health.

What does it mean to convert my account?

Converting your account switches you from an expired sponsorship to an unsponsored experience of Calm Health. You’ll keep access to your account and data, along with partial access to any clinical programs you’re currently enrolled in. Some in-app features will be limited under the unsponsored experience.

How do I convert my account?

Select Convert Account at the bottom of the expired sponsorship screen. You’ll be shown an authorization and consent screen; confirm your consent to proceed. Once conversion is complete, you’ll see a success screen.

What happens if I delete my Calm Health account?

Deleting your account is irreversible. You’ll lose access to your Calm Health data and records permanently. For more information, see Deleting Your Calm Health Account.

How to Change the Name or Email Address on Your Calm Account

You can update the name and email address associated with your Calm account at any time. You can make these changes using either the Calm website or the Calm mobile app.

Method 1: Update via the Calm Website (Desktop/Browser)

  1. Go to the Calm website and log in.

  2. Navigate to your Profile page .

  3. Locate your account details to make your changes:

    • Option A: If your fields are directly editable on this page, simply type in your new name or email.

    • Option B: If you see an ‘Account Settings’ tab instead, click the ‘Go to Settings’ button. This will take you to the page where you can update your name or email address.

  4. Save your changes.

Method 2: Update via the Calm Mobile App (iOS & Android)

  1. Open the Calm app and tap Profile on the home screen.

  2. Tap the Settings (Gear icon) in the upper left corner.

  3. Tap Account Info.

  4. Change your name or email address in the fields provided.

  5. Important: You must tap ‘Update’ at the bottom of the screen to ensure your new information saves.

Troubleshooting: “Email Already in Use” Error

If you try to update your email address and receive an “Email already in use” error, it means another Calm account is already registered under that email.

To fix this, check if that old account has an active paid subscription and follow the correct steps below:

  • If the old account does NOT have an active subscription: 1. Sign out of your current account. 2. Sign in to the older account using the email address you are trying to switch to. 3. Delete that older account to free up the email address. 4. Once deleted, log back into your primary account and update your email address.

  • If the old account DOES have an active subscription: * Do not delete the account! Deleting it could cause you to lose your Premium access. Instead, please contact our support team for assistance with your accounts.

Related Helpful Resources

If you currently log into Calm using a third-party service (like Apple or Google) and want to change your login method, please see our guide: How to Switch From 3rd Party Sign-In to Email Address and Password Combination

How to Change Your Calm Health Sponsor

If your healthcare provider or employer changes, you may be invited to continue your Calm Health sponsorship with a new provider. You can update your sponsor details in the Profile settings of the Calm Health app or website.

You’ll need a Calm Health invitation link or access code from your new provider to complete this process. If you’re unsure how to get this, please contact your HR or benefits administrator.

Change via the Calm Health app or website

  1. Launch the Calm Health app and log in. Tap Profile in the bottom right corner of your screen.
    • Website users: Click the Profile tab in the top navigation bar.
  2. From the Profile screen, tap Your Calm Health Sponsor, located just below your personal details, to view your current sponsorship.
  3. At the bottom of this screen, tap Update Sponsor.
  4. Enter the access code provided by your new sponsor.
  5. If prompted with an authorization or consent screen, tap I Accept to continue.
  6. Enter your eligibility details from your new provider. This may include your work email, member ID, employee ID, or date of birth. If you’re unsure what to enter, contact your benefits administrator for clarification.
  7. Once the update is successful, you’ll see a confirmation message before being redirected to your Profile Sponsorship screen, where your updated sponsorship details will appear.

Note: If your sponsorship has already ended and you don’t yet have a new sponsor, see Calm Health Access: What Happens When Your Sponsorship Ends.

How to Switch from Apple, Google, or Facebook Login to an Email & Password

If you originally signed up for Calm using a 3rd-party login (like Apple, Google, or Facebook), you can easily add a standard email and password combination to your account. This will allow you the flexibility to log in using either your new password OR your original 3rd-party sign-in option.

How to Add an Email & Password to Your Account

To set up a new password for your account, follow these steps:

  1. Log in to your Calm account using your current 3rd-party sign-in button (Apple, Google, or Facebook).

  2. Ensure your Calm account has an email address attached to it: Tap Profile > Settings (gear icon) > Account Info. If an email is not listed, add your preferred email address here.

  3. Next, open a web browser and go to www.calm.com/login.

  4. Enter the email address associated with your account and click “Forgot password.”

  5. Check your email inbox for a unique link to create a new password for your Calm account. (Note: If you don’t see the password reset email, please check your spam or junk folder).

How to Unlink or Remove a 3rd-Party Login

Please note: Calm cannot manually remove or unlink 3rd-party logins from your account on our end.

If you want to completely disconnect your Apple, Google, or Facebook account from Calm so it can no longer be used to log in, you must do so directly through your provider’s security settings.

Please follow the official guides below to manage and remove your linked apps:

  • Google: Follow these instructions to manage your linked accounts here 

  • Facebook: Follow these instructions to remove apps and websites here 

  • Apple: Follow these instructions to manage apps using ‘Sign in with Apple’ here

How to Transfer Your Calm Account & Subscription to a New Phone

Because your Calm subscription, listening history, and mindfulness streak are all tied to your Calm account email address (and not your physical device), moving to a new phone is easy!

To ensure a seamless transition to your new device, please follow the steps below based on what type of phone you are upgrading to.

Step 1: Verify Your Account (Before You Switch!)

Before getting rid of your old phone, open the Calm app and navigate to Profile > Settings (gear icon) > Account Info. Verify the exact email address you used to create your Calm account. As long as you know this email address and your password, all of your saved data will safely sync to your new device!

  • (Forgot your password? Learn how to easily reset it here: Reset Calm Password: Apple, Google & Email Login Guide).

Scenario A: Upgrading to the Same Type of Phone (e.g., iPhone to iPhone, or Android to Android)

If you are sticking with the same operating system, transferring is effortless:

  1. Download the Calm app on your new phone.

  2. Tap Log In and enter your Calm email address and password.

  3. Your Premium subscription, history, and streaks will immediately appear! Your billing will continue to auto-renew as normal through your Apple or Google Play account.

Scenario B: Switching to a Different Platform (e.g., iPhone to Android, or Android to iPhone)

If you are switching operating systems, your data and streak will still transfer when you log in, but your billing will not. Apple and Google do not share billing information with each other.

Because your new phone will not have your old app store on it, you must cancel your old auto-renewal to avoid being charged for a subscription you can no longer manage.

  • If switching from Android to iOS: You must cancel your subscription via the Google Play Store. To do this, see Google’s support article here: Cancel, pause, or change a subscription on Google Play

  • If switching from iOS to Android: You must cancel your subscription via your Apple ID. You can do this on an older Apple device, or by contacting Apple directly at 0.

Note: Canceling auto-renewal does not end your Premium access immediately! You will continue to have Premium access until your current billing cycle expires. Once it expires, you can simply open the Calm app on your new phone and purchase a new subscription through your new app store.

Pro Tip: Re-download Your Offline Content!

While your listening history and streak will automatically sync via the cloud, any content you previously downloaded for offline listening (like Sleep Stories or Music) was saved locally to your old phone’s hard drive. When you log in on your new device, your Downloads tab will be empty. Be sure to re-download your favorite offline tracks before your next flight or commute!

How to Unsubscribe from Calm Emails (Marketing vs. Transactional)

You can manage your email preferences and opt out of promotional messages directly from your email inbox.

How to Unsubscribe from Marketing Emails

To stop receiving promotional offers, newsletters, and marketing emails from Calm, follow these steps:

  1. Open a recent marketing email from Calm in your inbox.

  2. Scroll to the very bottom of the email.

  3. Click the link that says “Unsubscribe.”Important Tip for Gmail Users: Sometimes, long emails get “clipped” by your email provider. If you scroll to the bottom and do not see the Unsubscribe link, look for a button that says “View entire message” or “Message clipped.” Click that first, then scroll to the bottom again to find the Unsubscribe link.

Troubleshooting: Still Receiving Emails?

  • Did you recently change your account email? If you previously updated the email address associated with your Calm account, but you are still receiving marketing emails sent to your old email address, please contact our support team here so we can manually remove your old address from our mailing list.

  • Having trouble with the link? If the unsubscribe button is broken or you are still receiving marketing emails after opting out, send us a message here and our support team will manually unsubscribe you.

Transactional Emails

Even if you unsubscribe from all marketing emails, Calm will occasionally send you “transactional emails.”

  • What are they? These are mandatory emails about important account updates, such as changes to our Terms of Service, Privacy Policy, or billing receipts.

  • Can I unsubscribe from these? No. We make every effort to keep these emails infrequent, but as long as you maintain an active Calm account, you cannot unsubscribe from legally required account updates. If you wish to completely stop transactional emails, you must permanently delete your Calm account. You can read about full account deletion here: Deleting or Accessing your Personal Information

How to Update Your Calm Health Account Email, Name, or Password

You can update your Calm Health account email address, name, or password at any time from your Profile settings.

Looking to update your phone number? See How to Update Your Calm Health Phone Number (and Manage MFA) instead.

How to get to the Account Management screen

Using the app:
  1. Log in to the Calm Health app and tap the Profile icon in the bottom right corner.
  2. Tap Settings ⚙️ in the top right corner, then tap Manage Calm Account.
Using the website:
  1. Log in to the Calm Health website and click the Profile tab.
  2. Click Settings, then click Manage Calm Account.

You’ll be prompted to verify your identity using Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA). Follow the on-screen instructions using your registered phone number.

What you can update

Once verified, you’ll land on the Account Management screen, where you can:

  • View the email address or social login used to create your account
  • View and edit your first and last name
  • Reset your password (email and password users only)
  • View the phone number registered for MFA

How to Update Your Calm Health Phone Number (and Manage MFA)

We take your account security seriously. Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA) adds a vital layer of protection by requiring two forms of identification to access your account: your password and a unique, one-time verification code.

You can secure your account using text messages (SMS), voice calls, or third-party authenticator apps.

Need to update the phone number on file? Jump to How to Update or Change Your Phone Number below.

Option 1: Phone (SMS or Voice Call)

This method sends a one-time code directly to your mobile device via a text message or an automated call.

  1. Select Method: Choose Phone from the “Keep Your Account Safe” screen.

  2. Enter Details: Enter your mobile phone number and select your country code.

  3. Choose Delivery: Select Text message or Voice call.

  4. Verify: Enter the code sent to your phone and click Continue.

Important Note: If you enroll in both methods, you can always select “Try another method” to switch to your authenticator app and vice versa.

Option 2: Authenticator App

Using an authenticator app (like Google Authenticator or Okta Verify) is highly secure and works even without a cellular signal or internet connection.

  1. Download an App: Install a trusted authenticator app from your device’s app store. We recommend Google Authenticator or Okta Verify.

  2. Select Method: When prompted to “Secure Your Account,” tap Try another method and select Google Authenticator or similar.

  3. Link your Calm Account:

    • On a Computer: Use your authenticator app to scan the QR code displayed on your screen.
    • On a Mobile Device: The app will default to showing a manual setup code. Tap Copy code and paste it into your authenticator app’s “Enter a setup key” field.

  4. Verify: Your app will generate a code. Enter this code where it says “Enter your one-time code” and click Continue.

  5. Please note: If you don’t already have an authenticator app installed on your mobile device, follow these steps for Step 3 above:

    • Go to either your Play Store (Android) or App Store (iPhone) to download the Google Authenticator app.
    • Once downloaded, copy the code shown as “xxxx…” on the Calm Health app.
    • Open the Google Authenticator app.
      • Tap Add a code (or the plus sign).
      • Select Enter a setup key.
      • In the Code name field, enter a name to identify this as Calm Health (e.g., “Calm Health App”).
      • In the Your key field, enter the code you copied from Calm Health.
      • Leave Type of key as Time-based.
      • Tap Add.
      • You’ll see the time-based code displayed for Calm Health. Copy or write it down.
    • Go back to the Calm Health app and enter the code where it says “Enter your one-time code.”
    • Tap Continue in Calm Health to proceed with your onboarding.

Troubleshooting & FAQ

I am not receiving my SMS code.

If you’ve waited a while or clicked “Resend” multiple times with no success:

  • Tap Try another method to use your authenticator app.
  • Check that your phone number is correct and you have a steady signal.
  • Reach out to our support team.
I see a “Blocked Country” error.

Some regions don’t support SMS/Voice MFA. If you see this, use the Authenticator App method instead.

My authenticator code is being rejected.
  • Check Timing: Codes usually refresh every 30 seconds; enter the most recent one.
  • Sync Time: Make sure your device’s date and time are set to Automatic. Authenticator apps rely on precise time sync to work.
  • External Guides: For app-specific errors, see the Google Authenticator or Okta Verify help pages.
I see an error (e.g., “Something went wrong”).

Close and relaunch the Calm Health app. If that doesn’t work, restart your phone and try again. If the error persists, submit a request with a screenshot of the error and the steps you took before seeing it.

How to Update or Change Your Phone Number

If you’ve lost your mobile device, purchased a new phone, need to switch to a different MFA method, or simply need to update the number on file, you’ll need to reset your MFA enrollment.

For security reasons, this must be handled by the Calm Support team.

To request a phone number update:

  1. Submit a ticket: Visit our contact page and select Submit a Request.
  2. Provide account details: Use the email address associated with your Calm account so we can verify your identity quickly.
  3. Specify your request: State that you need to update your phone number (or MFA method), and include your new number if applicable.
  4. Choose your method: Let us know which method you’d like to enroll in next:
    • Phone (text message or voice call)
    • Authenticator App (Google Authenticator, Okta Verify, etc.)

For your protection, a support agent will verify your account details before clearing your current MFA settings. Once processed, you’ll receive an email with a new enrollment link to set up your chosen method.

For other account changes, like your name, email, or password, see How to Update Your Calm Health Account Email, Name, or Password.

Reset Calm Password: Apple, Google & Email Login Guide

If you’re having trouble logging in to your Calm account, you can reset your password.

How to reset your password:

  1. Open the Calm app or go to calm.com/login.

  2. Tap Log In.

  3. Enter the exact email address associated with your Calm account/subscription purchase.

  4. Tap Forgot Password?

  5. Check your inbox for a link from support@calm.com.

  6. Click the link to create your new password.

    • If you have not received the email after 10 minutes, please add support@calm.com and hello@breathe.calm.com to your email contact list and try resetting your password again.
    • For security reasons, your password reset link must be accessed within 20 minutes. After 20 minutes, the link will expire, but you can always request a new link by following these steps again.  

If you are currently logged in to your Calm account: On the Mobile App:

  1. Tap Profile > Settings.

  2. Tap Change Password.

  3. Enter your Old Password and then your New Password.

  4. Tap Update.

On the Website:

  1. Log in at calm.com/login.

  2. Go to Profile > Account.

  3. Click Change Password.

Troubleshooting

  • Don’t remember your current password? Log out of your Calm account and follow the steps at the beginning of this article.
  • If you originally created your Calm account using Facebook, Apple, or Google 3rd party sign-in options and you would like to use your email address and password to log in to Calm, please see How to Switch From 3rd Party Sign-In to Email Address and Password Combination for more information.

Updating Profile Name in the Calm Sleep app

To update your name in your Calm Sleep account:

  1. Tap My Library located on the lower navigation bar of the app
  2. Tap the Settings ⚙️ icon in the top right corner
  3. Next, tap Manage Account located under Account
  4. Tap ChangeAccountDetails. A web page will open prompting you to log in to your Calm Sleep account
  5. Log in using the same method you used to create your Calm Sleep account
  6. A 6-digit verification code will be sent to the email address associated with your account
  7. Enter the access code from the email and tap Continue
  8. Tap Edit next to your name
  9. Enter your updated name and tap Save to apply the changes

Updating your Email Address in the Calm Sleep App

To update your email address associated with your Calm Sleep account:

  1. Tap My Library located on the lower navigation bar of the app
  2. Tap the Settings ⚙️ icon in the top right corner
  3. Next, tap Manage Account located under Account
  4. Tap ChangeAccountDetails. A web page will open prompting you to log in to your Calm Sleep account
  5. Log in using the same method you used to create your Calm Sleep account
  6. A 6-digit verification code will be sent to the email address associated with your account
  7. Enter the access code from the email and tap Continue.
  8. Tap Logged In With Email to update your email address
  9. Tap Update to save your changes.

Updating your Password in the Calm Sleep App

To update your password associated with your Calm Sleep account:

  1. Tap My Library located on the lower navigation bar of the app
  2. Tap the Settings ⚙️ icon in the top right corner
  3. Next, tap Manage Account located under Account
  4. Tap ChangeAccountDetails. A web page will open prompting you to log in to your Calm Sleep account
  5. Log in using the same method you used to create your Calm Sleep account
  6. A 6-digit verification code will be sent to the email address associated with your account
  7. Enter the access code from the email and tap Continue
  8. Tap Reset Password. This will send you an email with further instructions
  9. In the email, tap Change Password
  10. Enter a new password, and tap Reset Password to save your new credentials

Please note: You can also use the ‘Forgot Password’ option on the login screen in Step 5 above if you have forgotten your current password.

Billing and Refunds

Calm App Pricing Update

Calm Premium Pricing Update & Renewal Check

At Calm, we’re committed to providing you with the best tools to support your mental health journey. 

To continue investing in expert-led programs, new features, and product innovation, we’re updating the price of our yearly subscription. While we recognize that changes in pricing can be challenging, this update ensures we can keep bringing you the best tools, content, and support.

  • Current Pricing: To view the most up-to-date subscription plans and pricing for your region, please View Current Plans & Pricing Here.

  • Legacy Pricing: If you subscribed years ago at a lower rate, your price may have increased to the current standard upon your next renewal.

How to Check Your Exact Renewal Price

If you are already a subscriber, your specific renewal price is listed in your account settings right now.

Option A: Apple (iPhone/iPad)

  1. Open iPhone Settings.

  2. Tap your Name > Subscriptions.

  3. Tap Calm.

  4. Look for “Renews on [Date] for [Price]”.

Option B: Google Play (Android)

  1. Open the Play Store app.

  2. Tap your Profile Icon > Payments & subscriptions.

  3. Tap Subscriptions > Calm.

  4. The renewal amount is listed next to the date.

Option C: Website (Direct Billing)

  1. Log in at calm.com/profile.

  2. Go to Settings > Manage Subscription.

  3. The next billing amount is displayed clearly.

Alternatives to Canceling

Consider these options:

  • Lifetime Membership: Pay once and never worry about price increases again.

  • Family Plan: Share one subscription with up to 5 friends/family members to split the cost.

  • Employer Access: Check if your company offers Calm for free via Calm Business.

  • Partnership Offers: Check if you are eligible for a deal through other apps or services you use. Companies such as Lincoln or MyFitnessPal sometimes offer special discounts or extended trials for Calm.

If you do not wish to continue with your subscription, you can always cancel your auto-renewal by following the instructions here: How to Cancel Your Subscription or Free Trial

Calm App Receipt & Invoice: Download Proof of Purchase

How to Get a Receipt or Invoice for ReimbursementWhich company charged you? The method to find your receipt depends on who processed your payment (Apple, Google, or Calm directly).

Option A: Website Purchases (Calm.com)

If you paid directly on our website via Credit Card:

  1. Log in to www.calm.com/login.

  2. Go to Profile > Settings > Manage Subscription.

  3. Look for the “Billing History” or “Receipts” section.

  4. Click the date of the charge to download a PDF receipt.

Option B: Apple App Store (iPhone/iPad)

Apple processes these payments, see How to see your purchase history on your device for more information.

  1. Open your iPhone Settings.

  2. Tap your Name (at the top) > Media & Purchases.

  3. Tap View Account > Purchase History.

  4. Select the Calm subscription transaction.

  5. Tap Resend Receipt (to email) or screenshot the details for your records.

Option C: Google Play Store (Android)

If you paid through Google Play, see Review your order history for more information.

  1. Go to payments.google.com.

  2. Sign in with your Google account.

  3. Click Activity.

  4. Select the Calm transaction to view and print the detailed receipt.

Option D: PayPal

If you paid on our website using Paypal, see How do I view and download statements and reports? for more information.

  1. Log in to your PayPal account.

  2. Go to Activity.

  3. Find the charge from “Calm” or “Calm.com”.

  4. Click the transaction to print the details.

Calm App Refund Policy: How to Get Your Money Back

We understand that sometimes things don’t work out as planned. Refunds are available within 30 days of your Calm.com purchase. The steps below explain how to request a refund depending on where you bought your Calm subscription.

Step 1: Identify where you purchased your Calm subscription

Refunds are processed differently depending on your purchase platform:

  • Where You Purchased: Who Handles the RefundWhat to Do

  • Calm.com: Calm Support

    Request a refund (login required)

     

  • Apple App Store / iTunes: Apple Support

    Request a refund from Apple

     

  • Google Play Store: Calm Support

    Contact Calm Support

     

Refunds for Calm.com purchases

If you purchased your subscription directly from Calm.com, you can request a refund within 30 days of purchase.

  1. Go to our Refund Request Form
  2. Log in to your Calm account
  3. Complete and submit the form

Once submitted, your refund is typically processed within 5–10 business days, depending on your payment provider.

Refunds for Apple (iTunes / App Store) purchases

If your subscription was purchased through Apple, Calm isn’t able to process the refund directly. Please request a refund from Apple using their portal:

  • Request a refund through Apple Support

You can also open the Apple Support app and select Billing & Subscriptions → Request a Refund.

Apple reviews and processes refunds independently of Calm. You can expect updates directly from Apple once your request is submitted.

Refunds for Google Play Store purchases

If you subscribed through Google Play, our Calm Support team can assist you.

To request a refund:

  1. Visit our Contact Support page
  2. Select Managing my Subscription → Google Play Store
  3. Fill out the short form and submit your request

We’ll respond in the order tickets are received. Refunds are typically processed within 5–10 business days once approved.

What to expect after submitting your refund request

  • You’ll receive a confirmation email once your refund is processed.
  • Refunds usually appear on your original payment method within 5–10 business days.
  • You’ll continue to have Calm Premium access until your refund is finalized.

Frequently Asked QuestionsHow do I know where I purchased my subscription?

Check your purchase confirmation email or your device’s purchase history. If you subscribed on a mobile device and see Apple or Google listed, follow their instructions above.

Can I request a partial refund?

At this time, Calm offers full refunds only for eligible Calm.com purchases within 30 days.

I canceled my subscription — do I still need to request a refund?

Yes. Canceling stops future renewals, but it doesn’t automatically issue a refund. Please follow the refund steps above.

What if I don’t see my refund after 10 days?

Check with your payment provider first. If it’s been more than 10 business days, reply to your original refund confirmation email for further assistance.

Full Terms & Conditions for Calm subscription purchases can be found here.
  • Lifetime memberships, gift cards of any length via Calm.com/Apple/Google Play, Calm Premium Family Plans, and Enterprise Plans are not eligible for a refund.

For further assistance:

Please reach out to us here to contact our support team.

Does Calm Support Apple Family Sharing?

No. At this time, the Calm app is not eligible for Apple’s native “Family Sharing” feature or the “Google Play Family Library.”

If you purchase a standard individual subscription to Calm through the Apple App Store or Google Play Store, that subscription is tied specifically to your personal account and cannot be shared with your family members through your device settings.

How to Share Calm: The Calm Premium Family Plan

We recognize how important it is to share mindfulness with your loved ones! While we do not support native Apple or Google app sharing, we offer our own dedicated Calm Premium Family Plan.

With the Calm Premium Family Plan, you can share full Premium access with up to 5 friends or family members (6 accounts total). Everyone gets their own separate, private account so your histories, streaks, and favorites remain completely independent.

Ready to upgrade to a Family Plan?

You can purchase the family plan directly through our website. Learn all about the cost, eligibility rules, and how to invite your loved ones here: Calm Family Plan: Price, Invites & How to Share Premium

⚠️ Important Note for Current Subscribers

If you already have an active individual subscription through Apple or Google and decide to purchase the Family Plan on our website, your original individual subscription will not cancel automatically. You will need to manually cancel your existing App Store or Google Play subscription to avoid being double-billed.

(Need help canceling your old plan? Check out our guide here: How to Cancel Your Subscription or Free Trial

How Calm Subscriptions Are Billed & Renewed

When you subscribe to Calm Premium, your subscription is tied to the payment method provided at the time of purchase. Here is a breakdown of how our different subscription plans are billed.

⚠️  Subscriptions Auto-Renew

Unless you actively cancel your subscription beforehand, all monthly and annual plans are set to automatically renew at the end of each billing period.

Billing by Plan Type

  • Annual Subscriptions: You are billed one upfront payment (plus applicable taxes) for the entire year at the time of purchase.

    • Free Trials: If you sign up for an annual plan with a 7-day free trial, your payment method will be charged the upfront annual fee at the very end of the trial period.

  • Calm Premium Family Plans: Similar to our standard annual plans, the Family Plan is billed as one upfront payment (plus applicable taxes) for the full year.

  • Monthly Subscriptions: You are billed once per month on the calendar date you initially subscribed.

  • Lifetime Memberships: You are charged a single, one-time payment (plus applicable taxes) at the time of purchase. Lifetime memberships never expire and do not auto-renew.

Where Will I Be Charged?

The platform you used to purchase your subscription determines how your billing is managed and which payment method is charged:

  • Apple App Store (iPhone/iPad): Billed to the primary payment method connected to your Apple ID.

  • Google Play Store (Android): Billed to the primary payment method connected to your Google Play account.

  • Calm Website (Direct): Billed directly to the credit card, debit card, or PayPal account you entered during checkout on calm.com.

Need to Update Your Payment Method?

If your card has expired or you want to change how you pay for your upcoming renewal, please see our guide here: Update Credit Card & Billing Info for Calm Subscription

Need to Cancel Your Renewal?

Don’t want to be charged again? You can stop your automatic renewal at any time. Learn how here: How to Cancel Your Subscription or Free Trial

How to Fix Calm Website Checkout Errors (“There was an error charging your card”)

If you are trying to purchase a subscription on the Calm website and receive a message saying “There was an error charging your card,” your payment has not gone through.

Why did my payment fail?

There are a few common reasons your payment may have been declined:

  • Fraud protection: Some banks and credit card providers erroneously flag Calm charge attempts as fraud. When this happens, your bank automatically blocks the transaction to protect your account.
  • Insufficient funds: Your card may not have enough available balance or credit to cover the charge.
  • Incorrect card details: Your card number, expiration date, CVC, or billing zip code may not match what your bank has on file. 
  • Recurring charge blocks: Some banks block recurring or subscription charges by default as a security measure, even if one-time purchases go through fine. 
  • International charges: Calm.com is based in the USA, so your bank may flag or block the charge if it doesn’t recognize or allow payments from outside your country.

Steps to Fix the Checkout Error

If your credit card is being declined on the Calm website, try the following troubleshooting steps to complete your subscription purchase:

  1. Check your details: Confirm that you are entering your card details correctly (including the card number, expiration date, and security code).

  2. Contact your bank: Reach out directly to your bank or credit card provider to confirm there is nothing blocking charges on your account. Ask them to remove any fraud flags they have in place that might prevent a Calm charge from going through.

  3. Try an alternate card or payment method: If possible, try using a different credit card or payment method to complete your purchase.

  4. Wait and try again: If the steps above do not work, close the page, wait 48 hours, and then try the purchase again.

How to Redeem Calm Promotional Offers & Discounts

This article explains how to redeem promotional discounts for Calm Premium and answers common questions about eligibility.

What to Know Before You Begin: Promotions vs. Partnerships

  • Calm Promotional Offers are Direct Campaigns: These are special discounts sent directly from Calm (for example, via our marketing emails). They are not partnerships.

  • Partner Offers are Separate: If you are trying to redeem an offer through a third-party partner (such as a health insurance provider, employer, or brand), that is a separate process. You may check out Promotional Offers through Calm Partners instead.

How to Redeem an Email Discount

If you received an email offering a discount, click or tap the button labeled “View Offer”. You should be directed to a purchase page displaying the discount price.

Important: Website Only (The “App Trap”)

This discount is only available through the Calm website. If you don’t see the offer, your device is likely opening the link inside the Calm app by mistake. To ensure you stay on the website, please try the following options:

  • Open the link on a desktop computer using a web browser (we recommend Google Chrome).

  • On your phone, tap and hold the link until a menu appears, then select “Open in New Tab”.

  • Open the email through a dedicated email application (such as the Gmail app) and tap the link.

Account Requirements & Exclusions

  • Do I need a new account? No. You do not need to create a new account. Simply sign in to your existing Calm account (make sure this account does not currently have an active subscription).

  • Family Plans: We are not able to apply a subscription discount to the purchase of a Calm Premium Family Plan at this time.

  • Gift Cards: We are not able to apply a subscription discount to gift cards at this time.

  • Can I get a discount right now? If you’re interested in a discount, we recommend creating a free Calm account to be added to our mailing list so you receive special offers. (You can also view our selection of completely free content here).

Rules for Existing Subscribers & Free Trials

“I currently have a 7-day free trial. Can I use the discount?”

  1. First, you must cancel your free trial to avoid being charged full price at the end of the trial period. (Learn how to cancel your free trial here).

  2. Wait until the trial period has ended and the free access has expired.

  3. Once expired, you can subscribe using the promotional discount link you received.

“I already have a paid subscription. Can I use the discount to extend my yearly plan?”

No. Promotional discounts are designed for Calm users who do not already have an active subscription.

  • Using this offer will not extend your current expiration date by 1 year. Instead, it activates a new subscription starting on the date of purchase (expiring 1 year from that date). We are unable to change the expiration date.

  • The Solution: You can cancel your current subscription and use the discount to make a new subscription purchase once your current billing period ends. (Learn how to cancel a subscription here).

  • Note: We do not offer any rebates or refunds on subscription purchases beyond 30 days of purchase.

TroubleshootingWhen I click the link, I am logged in as someone else. 

This happens if you are following a link that was emailed to a different Calm user, and then forwarded to you. Please only use discount links sent directly to the email address on your Calm account, otherwise, the discount will not be correctly applied.

I followed the link, but I am not seeing the discounted price. 

Many of our discount offers are available for a limited time and are subject to the terms of the original offer. Our yearly and lifetime subscription discounts are not available all year long, so your link may have expired.

I just purchased using the discount, but my Premium content is still locked. 

Please ensure that you are logged into the Calm app using the exact same credentials you just used to purchase the subscription on the website. (Visit this page for more information on how to log in).

I am getting a credit card error. 

If your credit card is valid but you are getting an error at checkout, please click here to read our troubleshooting guide for credit card purchase errors.

I was incorrectly charged. 

If you attempted to redeem the offer and were charged the wrong amount or received a system error, please contact our support team here. Please note, not all users are eligible for discount offers.

Requesting a Refund from Apple After Being Charged

How to Request a Refund for an Apple/iPhone Subscription

Who has your money?

  • Apple is the retailer. When you subscribe via the App Store, you pay Apple directly. Calm never receives your credit card or billing details.

  • The Result: Calm Support cannot process refunds for these charges. We physically do not have the ability to reverse the transaction. You must request it from Apple.

Step-by-Step Refund Instructions

Apple handles these requests entirely online.

  1. Go to the Portal: Visit reportaproblem.apple.com.

  2. Log In: Sign in with your Apple ID and password.

  3. Find the Charge: Look for “Calm” in the list of recent purchases.

  4. Select Option: Tap the drop-down menu “I’d like to…” and select “Request a refund”.

  5. Choose Reason: Select the reason (e.g., “I didn’t mean to buy this” or “Right of Withdrawal”).

  6. Submit: Apple will review your request. See this article for additional information.

Troubleshooting Common Issues

  • “The charge is ‘Pending’”:

    • If the charge says Pending on your bank statement or in Apple’s portal, you cannot request a refund yet.

    • The Fix: You must wait (usually 24 hours) for the charge to “Post” or “Clear” before the refund option becomes available.

  • “I don’t see the charge”:

    • You might be logged into a different Apple ID. Check your email receipt to see which email address was billed.

  • “Apple denied my refund”:

    • Since Apple controls the decision, Calm cannot overturn their ruling. However, you can try appealing the decision through the same “Report a Problem” portal.

Update Credit Card & Billing Info for Calm Subscription

Which app are you using?

  • Calm: Follow the instructions below to update your billing info.
  • CalmSleep/ Calm Health: These standalone apps use separate billing systems. Please check your account settings within those specific apps.

The method for updating your credit card or PayPal info depends on where you originally purchased your subscription (Apple, Google, or Calm.com).

Step 1: Identify Where You Subscribed If you aren’t sure where you paid:

  1. Open the Calm app.
  2. Tap Profile > Settings (Gear Icon).
  3. Tap ManageSubscription.
  • If it says “Manage on iTunes/App Store,” follow the Apple steps below.
  • If it says “Manage on Google Play,” follow the Android steps below.
  • If it lists a credit card or “Calm,” follow the Website steps below.

iTunes / App Store (iPhone & iPad) Calm cannot update this for you. You must do it in your iPhone settings.

  1. Open your iPhone’s Settings app.
  2. Tap your Name/Apple ID at the top.
  3. Tap Payment & Shipping.
  4. Tap Add Payment Method or tap an existing card to edit details.

Google Play (Android)Calm cannot update this for you. You must do it in your Google Play settings.

  1. Open the Google Play Store app.
  2. Tap your profile icon (top right).
  3. Tap Payments& subscriptions > Payment methods.
  4. Tap Update on the card you wish to fix.

Calm Website (Direct Purchase) If you subscribed directly via calm.com:

  1. Log in to your account at www.calm.com/login.
  2. Go to Profile > Settings > ManageSubscription.
  3. Look for the Update Payment Method link.
  4. Enter your new credit card details and save.

PayPalIf you paid via PayPal, you must update your funding source inside your PayPal account, not on Calm.

  1. Log in to PayPal.
  2. Go to Settings > Payments > ManageAutomaticPayments.
  3. Select Calm and change the funding source.

Which Payment Methods Does Calm Accept?

The payment methods you can use to purchase a Calm Premium subscription depend entirely on where you are making the purchase. We accept a variety of payment options across our website, the Apple App Store, and the Google Play Store.

Method 1: Purchasing on the Calm Website (calm.com)

When you buy a subscription directly through our website, your billing is managed directly by Calm. We currently accept:

  • Most major Credit Cards (Visa, Mastercard, American Express, Discover)

  • Debit Cards (Must be tied to a major credit network like Visa or Mastercard)

  • PayPal

  • Google Pay

  • Apple Pay (Available if browsing on a supported Apple device)

Method 2: Purchasing via the Apple App Store (iPhone/iPad)

If you upgrade your subscription inside the Calm app on an iOS device, your payment is processed entirely by Apple. You can use any payment method linked to your Apple ID, which typically includes:

  • Apple Pay

  • Credit or Debit Cards

  • PayPal

Method 3: Purchasing via the Google Play Store (Android)

If you upgrade inside the Calm app on an Android device, your payment is processed entirely by Google Play. You can use any payment method linked to your Google account, which typically includes:

  • Google Pay

  • Credit or Debit Cards

  • PayPal

🎁 A Note on Calm Gift Cards

If you received a Calm Gift Card, it cannot be entered as a standard “payment method” during checkout. Instead, it acts as a prepaid code that you redeem to activate your subscription. (Learn how here: Redeem Calm Gift Card: Where to Enter Code & Activation Steps). If you are looking to buy a Calm Gift Card for someone else at calm.com/gift, please note that we only accept Credit Cards and PayPal for these specific purchases.

Need to Change Your Current Payment Method?

If your current card has expired or you want to switch your payment type before your next renewal, please see our guide here: Update Credit Card & Billing Info for Calm Subscription

Calm App Integrations

Devices Compatible with Calm

Calm is available on a wide range of devices, including smartphones, tablets, computers, and select smart devices. This article outlines which platforms are fully supported and which may offer limited functionality.

Mobile devices

Calm is compatible with:

  • iOS devices on iOS 17 and above, such as iPhone, iPad, and Mac
  • Android devices on Android OS 8.1 and above, such as smartphones and tablets with a web browser

The Calm app is available on the Apple App Store and Google Play Store. If you’re in a region without access to these stores, you can still use Calm in a mobile browser at calm.com.

For step-by-step instructions, see How to Download the Calm App.

 

Computers and Web Browsers

You can access Calm on a computer by visiting calm.com using a supported web browser, such as Google Chrome or Safari. Calm is not compatible with Internet Explorer.

 

Smart TVs and Smart Home Devices

Calm is available on certain smart devices and third-party platforms. However, features may be limited, and login or syncing is not always supported.

  • Apple TV: A limited selection of Calm content is available through the Apple TV App Store. You do not need to log in to use the app on Apple TV. Login is not supported, so syncing with your Calm account is unavailable. Learn more about Calm on Apple TV.
  • Apple HomePod: Some Calm content can be streamed to Apple HomePod using Apple’s AirPlay feature. Login and syncing are not supported, and the Calm app is not directly integrated with Siri. For setup help, see Apple’s AirPlay instructions.
  • Google Home: Calm is no longer supported through Google Assistant voice commands. However, you can still stream Calm content by casting audio from your mobile device to your Google speaker using the Google Home app.
  • GoogleChromecast: Calm is compatible with Google Chromecast. You can cast your favorite meditations, Sleep Stories, and music directly from the Calm mobile app to your Chromecast-enabled TV or display.
  • Spotify: A selection of Calm content is available on Spotify. You can log in with your Spotify account, but Calm login and syncing are not supported. See Calm through Spotify.

 

Smartwatches

  • Apple Watch: Calm is available on Apple Watch OS6+ with limited functionality. You can play daily content, track mindful minutes, and view recent sessions. Login and syncing are not supported. Learn more about Calm on Apple Watch.
  • Wear OS: Calm is available on Wear OS with limited functionality. You can complete mood check-ins, use the Breathe Bubble, and access a selection of meditations and Sleep Stories. Login and syncing are not supported. See how to use Calm with Wear OS.

 

Additional Information

  • Older Devices: Devices running iOS versions below 17 or Android OS versions below 8.1 may experience issues running Calm due to limited internal memory and reduced compatibility.
  • Not supported: Calm is no longer supported on Sonos, Samsung TV, Alexa-enabled devices, or Roku. Calm also does not support Gabb devices running Gabb OS.

How to Install and Use the Calm App on WearOS Smartwatches

The Calm app is compatible with WearOS! If you are a subscribed Calm Premium member, you can use the Calm WearOS app to access a curated, limited selection of Premium content while on the go.

(Note: The Calm app is currently temporarily unavailable on some WearOS devices. We are actively working to restore the app for all watch models as quickly as possible.)

How to Install Calm on Your WearOS Watch

To download the app directly to your smartwatch, follow these steps:

  1. Open the Play Store directly on your WearOS watch.

  2. Search for Calm.

  3. Tap Install to add the app to your device.

Alternative Installation Method:

If your watch does not have the Play Store available directly on the screen, you can install it via your mobile phone:

  1. Download the Calm app to your Android phone.

  2. Open the WearOS application on your phone.

  3. Tap Get Apps to sync Calm to your connected watch.

What Content is Available on WearOS?

To optimize the experience for your smartwatch, the WearOS app features a limited, offline-friendly selection of Calm’s most popular tools and content:

  • Tools: Mood Check-in and the Breathe Bubble.

  • Meditations: The Big Idea, Basics of Mindfulness, Understanding Sleep, and Unlocking Happiness.

  • Sleep Stories: Blue Gold, The Grand Plan to Rise and Shine, and ShiShi and the Tree of Wonder.

Troubleshooting: Missing Session History

Please note that Mood Check-ins and session history from the Calm WearOS app will not sync with the Calm app on your phone or other devices. If your watch sessions are not showing up in your main profile’s history, this is expected behavior.

Further Reading

For more general information on how to manage apps on your smartwatch, you can read more about using Android Wear here: Android Wear Support Guide.

How to Remove Daily Calm Events from Your Personal Calendar

If you previously synced Daily Calm reminders to your personal calendar and no longer want to see them, you can remove them directly through the calendar settings on your mobile device.

Because these events are saved locally to your device’s personal calendar app, they are not managed inside the Calm app itself. Therefore, you will need to delete or unsubscribe from them using either Apple Calendar or Google Calendar.

Note: The Calm Support team does not have access to your personal device settings and cannot remotely delete these events from your calendar on your behalf.

How to Remove Events from Apple Calendar (iPhone/iCal)

If you are using an iPhone or Apple Calendar, you can delete the synced events or unsubscribe from the Daily Calm calendar by adjusting your iOS settings.

  • Please follow Apple’s official step-by-step instructions found here.

How to Remove Events from Google Calendar

If you are using Google Calendar (on either Android or iOS), you can hide or completely remove the Daily Calm calendar from your view.

  • Please follow Google’s official step-by-step instructions found here.

How to Set Calm Content as an Alarm (Android Devices Only)

If you use an Android device, you can start your day peacefully by setting Calm content as your morning alarm! Instead of standard alarm tones, you can choose to wake up to relaxing soundscapes, nature melodies, or even the Daily Calm.

(Please note: This feature is currently only available for Android devices and requires the Google Clock app.)

How to Set a Calm Alarm

  1. Open the default Clock app on your Android device.

  2. Tap the Alarm tab at the bottom of the screen.

  3. Find the specific alarm you want to change and tap the Down Arrow ⬇️ to expand the settings for that alarm.

  4. Tap on the current alarm sound (usually a bell icon).

  5. Select Calm from the options at the top of the screen.

Available Alarm Content

Once you select Calm as your audio source, you can choose from the following categories to set as your wake-up sound:

  • Daily Calm

  • Soundscapes

  • Nature Melodies

  • Relax

Check Your Alarm Volume

Because nature sounds and relaxing soundscapes are often softer and more gradual than traditional, jarring alarm tones, we highly recommend double-checking your Android device’s alarm volume settings before you go to sleep to ensure you wake up on time!

How to Set Up & Use Siri Voice Shortcuts with Calm

You can use Apple’s Siri to navigate the Calm app completely hands-free! With over a dozen custom voice commands, you can ask Siri to do everything from playing your Daily program to launching an Emergency Calm meditation when you need it most.

How to Enable Siri Shortcuts (iOS 16 & Newer)

If your Apple device is running iOS 16 or newer, you will manage your Siri settings through Apple’s native Shortcuts app.

  1. Ensure Siri is enabled in your device’s main Settings.

  2. Open the Shortcuts app on your iPhone or iPad.

  3. Scroll down to find Calm, or type “Calm” into the search bar.

  4. Tap the ‘See All’ button to reveal all available Calm Siri commands.

  5. You will see a banner labeled Talk to Calm. Tap the option to Turn on Calm Shortcuts with Siri. (Alternatively, you can tap the (i) symbol in the top right corner and ensure that both ‘Spotlight’ and ‘Siri’ are toggled on).

What You Can Say (Calm Siri Commands)

Once enabled, you can trigger your favorite content by saying “Hey Siri…” followed by any of these commands:

Daily Programs

  • “Play the Daily Calm” * “Play the Daily [Jay, Trip, or Move] on Calm” (This will play today’s specific program from your requested instructor).

Sleep Content

  • “Play a Sleep Story from Calm” (This will automatically play our featured Sleep Story. You can also specifically say “Play a Kids Sleep Story from Calm” or “Play a favorite Sleep Story from Calm”.)

Meditations & Breathing

  • “Start a meditation with Calm” (Siri will prompt you to select a 5, 10, 20, or 30-minute session).

  • “Start a breathing exercise on Calm” (Siri will prompt you to select from Relax, Balance, Restore, Focus, Energize, or Unwind).

  • “Play the Emergency Calm” * “Start a mood check-in on Calm”Music, Sounds & Kids

  • “Play a Soundscape on Calm” (This plays your most recently played soundscape or a favorited soundscape. If none exist, it will play a random one).

  • “Play music on Calm”

  • “Play a Kids meditation on Calm”

How to Sync the Calm App with Apple Health (Track Mindfulness Minutes)

You can connect the Calm app to your iPhone or iPad’s Apple Health app to automatically track your listening sessions as Mindfulness Minutes.

Important Note Before You Begin: These instructions are specifically for the primary Calm app. If you are looking to sync the standalone Calm Sleep app, please see our other guide here: Connecting Health Data to Calm Sleep

How to Enable Apple Health Syncing

To connect the Calm app to Apple Health, follow these steps:

  1. Open the Calm app and tap Profile in the bottom right corner.

  2. Tap the Settings (Gear icon ⚙️) in the upper left corner.

  3. Locate the ‘Apple Health’ setting and toggle it to the ON position.

  4. Follow the on-screen Apple Health prompts to allow Calm to write Mindfulness data.

Once connected, your completed sessions will appear under the Mindfulness category inside your Apple Health app.

What Content Counts as Mindfulness Minutes?

Please note that background Scenes do not count as Mindfulness minutes in Apple Health. Only active, guided sessions (such as meditations and Sleep Stories) are recorded to your Health app.

Using Calm with an Apple Watch

If you use the Calm app on your Apple Watch, your sessions can also be tracked and synced. For more information on how to set up and use Calm on your wearable device, please see our Apple Watch guide here: How to Use the Calm App on Apple Watch

Troubleshooting & Frequently Asked QuestionsWhy did the Apple Health toggle disappear from my Calm settings?

This is normal! Once Calm is successfully connected to the Health app, the ‘Apple Health’ toggle will be removed from your Calm settings menu. If you need to manage your permissions or disconnect Calm in the future, you must do so directly inside your Apple Health app (Open Apple Health > Tap your Profile picture > Select Apps > Select Calm).”

Is Calm’s Apple Health integration supported on iPad?

Yes! Apple Health syncing is supported on iPad running iPadOS 17 or later.

Need more help with Apple Health? 

You can read more about how to manage the Health app on Apple’s official support page here: Use the Health app on your iPhone or iPad

How to Use Calm on an Amazon Kindle or Fire Tablet

Currently, the Calm app cannot be downloaded on Amazon Kindle e-readers or Amazon Fire Tablets. It is not available in the Amazon Appstore.

(Please note: Standard black-and-white e-ink Kindles, like the Paperwhite or Oasis, do not support media streaming and cannot play Calm content at all).

How to Use Calm on a Fire Tablet (Browser Workaround)

If you have a color-screen Amazon Fire Tablet, you can attempt to access our library of meditations and Sleep Stories by logging into the Calm website through your tablet’s internet browser.

⚠️ The Website is Not Optimized for Kindle

Please be aware that 0 is not fully optimized for Kindle web browsers (such as the Amazon Silk browser). While you can navigate to the site, you may experience slower loading times, display issues, or audio that stops playing when the screen turns off.

If you would still like to try the web browser workaround:

  1. Turn on your Amazon Fire Tablet and open your internet browser.

  2. Type 0 into the address bar and hit enter.

  3. Tap Log In in the top right corner of the website.

  4. Sign in with the exact same method (Email, Apple, Google, or Facebook) you used to create your account.

For the smoothest and most reliable Calm experience, we highly recommend downloading the official mobile app on a supported Apple iOS or Android device, or using a standard desktop computer.

How to Use the Calm App on Apple TV

You can enjoy a relaxing selection of Calm content directly from your television by downloading Calm for Apple TV. The app is available to download in the “Health & Fitness” category of the Apple TV App Store.

No Login Required (Free Access)

There is currently no login option for Calm on Apple TV. All content available in the Apple TV app can be enjoyed completely for free without signing into a Calm account! This free selection includes:

  • The Daily Calm

  • A library of guided meditations

  • Calm Music

  • A selection of our most popular Sleep Stories

  • Please note: Because there is currently no way to log in to your account, our complete Premium library is not accessible directly through the native Apple TV app.

Pro Tip: How to Play Premium Content on Your TV (AirPlay)

If you are a Calm Premium member and want to listen to our full, unrestricted library of content through your television speakers, you can easily use Apple AirPlay as a workaround! Just open the Calm app on your iPhone or iPad, start playing any Premium session, and tap the AirPlay icon to cast the audio directly to your Apple TV.

How to Watch Background Scenes

All of Calm’s beautiful background scenes can be played from the Apple TV app.

  • To watch them, open the Calm app and simply stop using your remote for a few seconds. The controls and text descriptions on the screen will fade away, leaving you with just the background scene to watch.

  • You can also manually launch the Full-Screen option.

Using Apple TV “Top Shelf” Shortcuts

You can drag the Calm app from your main list of apps up to the very top row of your home screen (referred to as the “Top Shelf”).

  • If you add Calm to your Top Shelf, highlighting the app will allow you to quickly view our content categories and launch the Daily Calm right from your home screen.

Things to Know & Troubleshooting

  • Session History: Because there is not yet a login option available for Apple TV, the sessions you listen to on your television will not be logged or synced to your Calm account’s session history.

  • Sequential Programs: Individual sessions in our multi-day meditation programs (such as 7 Days of Calm) must be completed in chronological order. For example, you must complete Day 1 on the Apple TV before you can move on to Day 2.

  • Loading Errors: If you see an error message or a button asking you to reload content, please ensure you have a strong internet connection and try again.

  • For general help using your device, you can read more about using Apple TV here.

How to Use the Calm App on Apple Watch

This guide explains how to install and use the Calm app on your Apple Watch, including available features and system requirements.

The Calm Apple Watch app offers a limited selection of content that is available to all Apple Watch users.

Important Limitations to Know Before You Begin

  • No Account Login: There is no option to sign in to your Calm account on the Apple Watch app.

  • No Complications: Apple Watch complications are not available for the Calm app at this time.

  • Bluetooth Required: You must connect Bluetooth headphones to your Apple Watch to listen to Calm audio.

Available Features on Apple Watch

Because the watch app features a limited selection of content, you will only find the following sessions available:

  • Daily Calm

  • Youth Meditations

  • Breathing exercises

  • Stretches

  • Mindful walking meditations

  • Emergency Calm

How to Install the App

To learn how to download and install apps directly to your Apple Watch, please refer to this Apple Support article.

System Requirements

  • Your Apple Watch must be running WatchOS 6 or later to use the Calm app.

How to Change Your Language Settings

The language displayed on your Apple Watch app is determined automatically based on your devices:

  • If you have the Calm app downloaded on an iPhone or iPad: The watch app will default to the language you currently have selected inside the Calm app on your phone/tablet.

  • If you do not have the Calm app downloaded on an iPhone or iPad: The watch app will default to the overall language setting of your Apple Watch.

Play Calm on Smart Speakers: Sonos, Alexa, Google & HomePod Guide

How to Play Calm on Smart Speakers (Alexa, Google, Sonos & HomePod)

Connection Method Update
  • Direct Voice Commands: Calm is no longer supported via native voice assistants (e.g., “Alexa, open Calm” or “Hey Google, play Daily Calm”).
  • The Solution: You must stream content from your mobile device using AirPlay, Chromecast, or Bluetooth.
Streaks & Session History
  • Will this count toward my stats? generally, NO.
  • Playing a session via AirPlay, Bluetooth, or Casting often bypasses the app’s internal timer. This means the session will not automatically count toward your Daily Streak or appear in your History.
  • The Fix: If you want to keep your streak alive, you must add the session manually after you finish listening. See How to Correct a Broken Streak 
Apple HomePod (Siri) 

The best way to listen is via AirPlay.

  1. Open the Calmapp on your iPhone or iPad.
  2. Start playing your session.
  3. Tap the AirPlayicon (triangle with circles) in the player or Control Center.
  4. Select your HomePod from the list.
  • Note: You cannot ask Siri on the HomePod to start a session directly.
Google Home / Nest Audio 
Method 1: Google Cast (Recommended)
  1. Ensure your phone and Google Speaker are on the same Wi-Fi network.
  2. Open the Calmapp on Android or iOS.
  3. Play a session and look for the CastIcon (rectangle with Wi-Fi waves) in the top corner.
  4. Select your speaker to begin casting.
Method 2: Bluetooth
  1. Say “Hey Google, pair Bluetooth.”
  2. Go to your phone’s Bluetooth settings and tap your Google Speaker.
  3. Any audio from the Calm app will now play through the speaker.
Sonos Speakers Important Update: As of January 2025, the direct “Calm Service” within the Sonos app has been discontinued.How to listen now:
  • iOS Users: Use AirPlay to stream from the Calm app to your Sonos speaker.
  • AndroidUsers: Use Bluetooth (if your Sonos speaker, like the Roam or Move, supports it) or Line-In connection.
  • Note: You can no longer browse the Calm library directly inside the Sonos controller app.
Amazon Echo (Alexa) Calm does not have an active Alexa Skill. You must use Bluetooth.
  1. Say “Alexa, connect to my phone.”
  2. Go to your phone’s Bluetooth settings and select your Echo device.
  3. Open the Calm app and play your session.
  4. Warning: If you find a “Calm” skill in the Alexa store, it is likely “Calm Radio,” a separate company not affiliated with us.

Calm App Setup

Download Calm App: iOS, Android & Desktop Access Guide

How to Download & Install the Calm App

Device Compatibility 

To run the Calm app smoothly, your device must meet these minimum requirements:

  • iPhone/iPad: Must be running iOS 17 or later.
  • Android: Must be running Android 8.1 (Oreo) or later.
  • KindleFire: Not currently supported.
Method 1: iPhone & iPad (iOS)

  1. Open the App Store on your device.

  2. Tap Search (bottom right) and type “Calm”.

  3. Look for the Blue Icon with “Calm - Sleep, Meditate, Relax”.

  4. Tap Get or the Cloud Icon to install.

Method 2: Android Phones & Tablets

  1. Open the Google Play Store.

  2. Search for “Calm”.

  3. Tap Install.

  • Note: If you do not see the app listed, your device may be too old to support the latest version.
Method 3: Desktop & Laptop Computers

  • There is no desktop app to download.

  • You do not need to install anything! Simply open your web browser (Chrome, Safari, Edge) and go to www.calm.com to log in and listen.

Troubleshooting Download Issues

  • “App not compatible with this device”: This means your phone’s operating system is outdated. Please update your iOS or Android version in your phone settings.

  • “Pending” or “Waiting”: Check your internet connection. Try switching from Wi-Fi to Mobile Data.

  • “Not enough storage”: You may need to delete unused apps or photos to make room for Calm (approx. 100MB + extra for downloads).

How to Log In and Log Out of Your Calm Account

You can log in to your Calm account on the Calm app or at calm.com. Follow the steps below to log in, switch accounts, or log out.

How to Log In On the Calm App (iOS or Android)

  1. Open the Calm app.
  2. Tap Profile at the bottom of the screen.
  3. Tap Log In.
  4. Enter or select the log in method used to create the Calm account
    • Email and password
    • Sign in with Facebook
    • Sign in with Apple*
    • Sign in with Google
  5. Follow the prompts to log in.

How to Log In On On a Web Browser (Calm.com)

  1. Visit www.calm.com/login
  2. Enter or select the log in method used to create the Calm account
    • Email and password
    • Sign in with Facebook
    • Sign in with Apple*
    • Sign in with Google
  3. Follow the prompts to log in.

How do I log out of my Calm account?

To log out of your Calm account, follow the steps below:

Calm app
  1. Tap ‘Profile’ on the home screen.
  2. Select the gear icon in the upper left corner.
  3. Scroll down and you will see a ‘Logout’ option.

 

Calm website
  1. Go to 0.
  2. Click on ‘Profile’ in the left navigation panel.
  3. Select the ‘Logout’ button from the Profile menu.

How to Uninstall and Reinstall the Calm App (Troubleshooting)

Uninstalling and reinstalling your Calm app is a great way to quickly troubleshoot and fix many common glitches or performance issues.

⚠️ Before You Begin: Important Notes

  • Save Your Data: If you use Calm as a “Guest” and have not yet created a free Calm account with an email address, you must create an account before deleting the app. If you delete the app without an account, your session history and data will be permanently lost.

  • Your Subscription is Safe: Do not worry—deleting the app will not cancel your active subscription or charge you again! Your premium access is tied directly to your account, not the app installation itself.

How to Reinstall on iPhone or iPad (iOS)

  1. On your device’s home screen, press and hold your finger on the Calm app icon.

  2. Tap Remove App (or Delete App), and confirm your choice.

  3. Open the App Store and search for “Calm” (or tap Download Calm for iOS).

  4. Tap the download icon to reinstall the app.

How to Reinstall on Android Devices

  1. On your device’s home screen or app drawer, press and hold your finger on the Calm app icon.

  2. Tap the “i” (App info) icon, then select Uninstall and confirm your choice.

  3. Open the Google Play Store and search for “Calm” (or tap Download Calm for Android).

  4. Tap Install to download the app again.

Logging Back In Once you have successfully reinstalled and opened the app, be sure to select Log in (instead of “Sign up”) to access your existing account, profile history, and premium subscription!

How to Update the Calm App (iOS & Android)

We frequently release updates to the Calm app to introduce new features, add fresh content, and fix technical bugs. To ensure you have the best possible experience, we highly recommend keeping your app updated to the latest version.

Not sure if you need an update? Learn how to check which version of the Calm app you are currently running here: How to Check Which App Version You Are Running.

Here is how to check for updates depending on your device:

How to Update on iOS (iPhone & iPad)

  1. Open the App Store on your device.

  2. Tap your Profile icon at the top right of the screen.

  3. Scroll down to see pending updates and release notes.

  4. Find Calm in the list and tap Update next to it. (Note: If it says “Open” instead of “Update,” you are already on the latest version!)

How to Update on Android

  1. Open the Google Play Store app.

  2. Tap your Profile icon at the top right of the screen.

  3. Tap Manage apps & device.

  4. Under the “Updates available” section, tap See details.

  5. Find Calm in the list and tap Update.

Pro Tip: Enable Automatic Updates

If you don’t want to worry about manually checking for new versions, you can set your device to update Calm automatically in the background!

  • For iOS: Go to your device Settings > App Store and toggle App Updates to ON under the Automatic Downloads section.

  • For Android: Open the Google Play Store, go to the Calm app page, tap the three dots in the top right corner, and check the box for Enable auto update.

Troubleshooting: The App Won’t Update?

If you are tapping “Update” but the download is stuck or failing, try the following quick fixes:

  • Check device compatibility: If you do not see an “Update” button but know a new version exists, your device’s operating system might be too old to support it. Please ensure your device meets our minimum operating system requirements (e.g., updating your iPhone to the newest iOS or Android to the newest OS).

  • Check your connection: Ensure you are connected to a strong Wi-Fi network. Large updates sometimes will not download over a weak Cellular connection.

  • Check your storage: Your device may not have enough free storage space to download the update. Try deleting old photos or unused apps to free up space.

  • Restart your device: A simple phone restart often clears up temporary app store glitches.

How to Use Dark Mode Scenes on iOS (iPhone & iPad)

If you are using an iOS device (iPhone or iPad), the Calm app can sync directly with your device’s system settings. When your device is set to Dark Mode, selecting one of our Dark Mode-compatible backgrounds will cause the scene to automatically switch from a daytime view to a nighttime view!

How to Set Scenes to Switch Automatically

In order for your device to seamlessly switch between the day and night scenes as the sun goes down, you will need to enable the “Automatic” appearance setting on your iOS device:

  1. Open your device’s primary Settings app.

  2. Navigate to Display & Brightness (or search for ‘Appearance’ in your Settings search bar).

  3. Under the Appearance section, toggle Automatic to the ON position.

Your phone will now automatically switch between Light Mode and Dark Mode based on the time of day, and the Calm app will follow suit!

Using a Locked Light or Dark Mode

If you prefer to keep your phone locked in either Light Mode or Dark Mode (rather than having it switch automatically), our compatible scenes will simply adapt to whichever setting you have chosen:

  • Locked to Light Mode: Scenes will permanently stay on the daylight version.

  • Locked to Dark Mode: Scenes will permanently stay on the nighttime version.

Which Scenes are Compatible with Dark Mode?

Currently, the following background scenes offer both day and night versions:

  • Jasper Lake

  • The Waterfall

  • Blue Gold

  • Calm River

Further Reading Want to learn more about how to change your background or adjust the volume of the ambient sounds? Check out our complete guide here: Calm Scenes Guide: Change Background, Volume & Playback Timer

How to Use Your Calm Account on Multiple Devices

Can I use Calm on my phone and my tablet?

Yes! You can use your Calm account on multiple devices, including smartphones, iPads, Android tablets, and the Calm website. Just make sure you’re logged in on each device using the same login method (such as email, Apple ID, Google, or Facebook) that you originally used to create your account.

 

How to Log In on Each Device
On an iPhone, Android Phone, iPad, or Tablet
  1. Download the Calm app.
  2. Tap the Profile icon in the bottom right corner.
  3. Tap Sign up or Log in, and make sure the screen says Log into your Calm account.
  4. Choose your login method (email, Apple ID, Google, or Facebook) and sign in with the same account you used to create your Calm profile.
On the Website
  1. Go to calm.com/login.
  2. Make sure the screen says Log into your account.
  3. Choose your login method (email, Apple ID, Google, or Facebook) and sign in with the same account you used to create your Calm profile.

Once you’re logged into the same account, your subscription and favorites will sync across all your devices. This includes your phone, iPad, tablet, and web browser.

Troubleshooting: Missing Subscription?

If you have logged into your new device but your premium content is still locked, you may have accidentally logged into a different account! For help locating your active subscription, please see our troubleshooting guide: Paid For a Calm Subscription But Premium Content is Still Locked

Troubleshooting App Download Issues

  • “App not compatible with this device”: This means your phone’s operating system is outdated. Please update your iOS or Android version in your phone settings.

  • “Pending” or “Waiting”: Check your internet connection. Try switching from Wi-Fi to Mobile Data.
