How to Change the Language of your Calm Health app

Calm Health automatically matches the language set on your device. See Calm Health Supported Languages for the full list of supported languages. 

iOS / Apple Users

You can set a language for Calm Health individually, without changing your entire device’s language.

  1. Open the Settings app.
  2. Scroll down and tap Calm Health.
  3. Tap Language.
  4. Select your preferred language from the list.
  5. Launch the Calm Health app, and your language preferences will be updated.

Android Users

Android doesn’t currently support setting an individual app language for Calm Health. To change the app’s language, you’ll need to change your device’s language.

  1. Open the Settings app.
  2. Tap General Management.
  3. Select Language.
  4. Tap Add Language.
  5. Select your preferred language and set it as your default.
  6. Launch the Calm Health app, and your language preferences will be updated.

Web Users

The Calm Health website experience matches your browser’s language setting. To change it, update your browser’s language preference in its settings.

Additional information

  • Some Clinical Program content isn’t yet available with non-English audio.
  • If the audio is only available in English, closed captions will still display in your selected language. Just look for the speech bubble symbol when listening to a session.
  • If a specific program you’re looking for isn’t visible, it may only be available in English at this time.

Calm Premium

Calm Premium vs. Free: Features, Content List & Benefits

What is Included in Calm Premium?The Short Answer”Calm Premium” is the term we use when your account has full access to our entire library. It means all content is unlocked.

You do not need to purchase multiple subscriptions. One valid active plan—whether it’s a free trial, a gift, or a work benefit—unlocks the entire app for you.

Calm Free vs. Calm Premium
  • Feature: Calm Free (Basic) Calm Premium (Unlocked)
  • Content Status: Locked (Padlock icons visible) Unlocked (Full Access)
  • Daily Calm: No Yes
  • Sleep Stories: A few selected titles All 300+ Stories
  • Music Library: Limited tracks Full Library (Disney, Lofi, etc.)
  • Masterclasses: No Yes
Ways to Get Premium Access

You might already have access! “Premium” comes from any of the following sources:

  • Individual Subscription: You purchased a yearly or monthly plan via Apple, Google, or our website.

  • Free Trials: A 7-day (or extended) free trial gives you full Premium status for the duration of the trial.

  • Employer Benefit: Your company provides “Calm Business” access.

  • Partnerships: You activated a special offer through partners like Chase, AARP, Lincoln, or Kaiser Permanente.

  • Family Plan: You are a member of someone else’s Calm Family Plan.

  • Lifetime Membership: You paid the one-time fee for forever access.

Important: All of these provide the exact same content. Having a subscription from your employer and a personal Apple subscription does not give you extra features.

Key Premium Features

  • The Daily Calm: A 10-minute meditation every single day, exploring a fresh theme (e.g., Gratitude, Anxiety, Focus).

  • Sleep Stories: Bedtime stories for adults and kids, narrated by celebrities like Harry Styles, Matthew McConaughey, Idris Elba, and Kate Winslet.

  • Exclusive Music: Hand-picked playlists for focus, relaxation, and sleep featuring top artists.

  • Masterclasses: Deep-dive audio courses taught by world-renowned experts.

  • Daily Move: Gentle video lessons for stretching and mindful movement.

Accessibility & Languages

  • Platforms: Your Premium access travels with you. Log in to the same email address on iOS, Android, or Web (www.calm.com) to access your content.

  • Languages: Content availability may vary by language. Check your app settings to switch between English, Spanish, German, French, and more.

**Access to some content may be limited under certain languages, regions or locations.*

Calm Web Free Trial: Sign Up & Cancellation Steps

Which trial do you have?

  • Web Trial: You entered your credit card at calm.com. Follow the guide below.

  • App Store Trial: You double-clicked the side button on your iPhone or Android. Please see How a Free Trial via Apple Works or Canceling a  Subscription or Free Trial Through the Google Play Store. The rules are different.

How the Web Trial Works 

New users can activate a 14-day free trial of Calm Premium at www.calm.com/freetrial.

  • Duration: 14 Days.

  • Access: Unlocks the full library (Meditations, Sleep Stories, Music) on both the Website and the Mobile App.

  • Requirement: A valid credit card or Google Pay.

Billing & Auto-Renewal

  • The Charge: You will see a $0.00 authorization initially.

  • The Auto-Renew: If you do not cancel, the trial automatically converts into a paid Annual Subscription ($79.99/year) on Day 15.

  • Cancellation Deadline: You must cancel at least 24 hours before the trial ends to avoid being charged.

How to Cancel Your Web Trial 

Since you signed up on the website, you generally cannot cancel via your iPhone/Android system settings.

  1. Log in at www.calm.com/login.
  2. Go to Profile > Settings > Manage Subscription.
  3. Follow the prompts to Cancel Trial.

Tip: You can cancel immediately after signing up. You will keep your Premium access for the full 14 days, but you won’t be charged when it ends.

How to Use Your Web Trial on the App

  1. Download the Calm app (Blue icon) from the App Store or Google Play.
  2. Log In: Use the same log in method selected on the website.
  3. Everything should be unlocked. If it’s locked, you may have logged into a different account.

 

Please note: If you have previously completed a free trial for Calm Premium, you will not be eligible for the 14-day free trial. For further questions, please reach out to our support team here. 

How a Free Trial via Apple Works

When you download the Calm app on an iOS device (such as an iPhone or iPad), you have the option to start a 7-day free trial of Calm Premium. If not canceled before the trial ends, it will automatically renew into an annual subscription. If you have previously completed a free trial for Calm Premium, you will not be eligible for the 7-day free trial

Please note: Since the free trial is managed through the Apple App Store, Apple handles all billing, activation, and cancellations. Calm is unable to cancel or modify trials or subscriptions purchased through Apple on your behalf.

For more details on how the free trial works, please see below.

  1. Create an account: Before seeing the free trial screen, users will first have the option to create a Calm account. We recommend doing this if you plan to use a free trial, so that we’re able to locate your information in our system should you need any assistance.  

  2. Unlock Calm: Shortly after signing in, you will see a screen inviting you to try 7 days of subscription access. This unlocks a 7-day trial to a Calm subscription on your Calm account. You can read more about what a Calm subscription includes here.

     

  3. If you don’t want to start a trial: If you do not wish to start a free trial, you can use the ‘X’ in the top right corner of the screen to exit. This will take you to the main home screen of the Calm app, where you can explore our free content. Subscription content will have lock icons next to it, such as the Daily Calm. Tapping on a subscription option will bring up a screen to unlock our full library of Calm Premium subscription content.  

  4. Confirm Subscription: If you decide to start a free trial, click on “Try for $0”. You will then see a screen with an explanation of the trial terms. The free trial will be active for 7 days, at which point you will be charged the full yearly subscription price. 

     

  5. Start your free trial: Congratulations! You’ve started your 7 days of free access to a Calm Premium subscription. We have hundreds of hours of meditations, stories, and music for you to enjoy during your trial. You will know the trial activation has been successful if you see everything unlocked in the app. If you still see lock icons next to some of the content after starting the free trial, please see HERE.  

  6. Cancel your free trial: If you do not wish to continue with a yearly subscription at the end of the trial period, please be sure to cancel at least 24 hours before the free trial ends to avoid to avoid any charges through Apple. This article includes details about how to cancel your free trial through the App Store settings on your device.

     

  7. Using Calm after your trial: If you cancel the free trial before it ends, the app will revert back to the locked version at the end of 7 days. You will then only be able to access our free content, which you’re welcome to listen to as much as you’d like. If you do not cancel, you will be billed the full annual subscription price and you will continue to have access to the full library of Calm Premium content for the duration of your 1-year subscription. You can read more about how subscriptions through Apple work here.

How to Change Your Calm Subscription Plan (Monthly vs. Annual)

How to Switch from Monthly to Annual

To upgrade your subscription from a monthly plan to an annual plan, you must first cancel your current monthly subscription.

Once your monthly auto-renewal is canceled and your current billing cycle ends, you can re-subscribe to the annual option right here: Calm Subscription Page

  • Pro Tip: Your progress is safe! Don’t worry, canceling your monthly subscription to switch plans will not delete your data. Your saved favorites, meditation streaks, and session history are securely tied to your Calm account and will be waiting for you when you activate your new annual plan.

To cancel your current monthly subscription, follow the instructions for the platform where you originally purchased it:

If you purchased through Apple (iPhone/iPad): You can access your Apple subscription settings through the Calm app.

  1. Open the Calm app and tap Profile.

  2. Tap the Settings (gear icon) in the upper left corner.

  3. Tap Manage Subscription.

  4. On the next page, tap the word ‘here’ to be redirected to your phone’s Apple subscription settings.

  5. Find Calm and select the option to cancel. (Note: If you see Calm listed but it only shows an expiration date and no option to cancel, your subscription has already been canceled).

If you purchased through Google Play (Android): You can manage your subscription via the Calm app or Google Play.

  • Via the Calm App: Tap Profile > tap the Settings (gear icon) > tap Manage Subscription > tap the ‘here’ link to be redirected to your Android subscription settings.

  • Via the Google Play app: Open the Google Play Store app > select My Apps > look for the Subscriptions tab.

  • Via the Web: Go to the Google Play Store website > click Account in the left-hand menu > scroll down to the Subscriptions section.

  • If you purchased through the Calm Website: If you originally subscribed to a monthly plan directly through our website, please contact us for assistance! Reach out to our support team at calm.com/contact and provide the email address associated with your account so we can cancel the subscription for you.

How to Switch from Annual to Monthly

Unfortunately, we do not have the ability to change an active annual subscription into a monthly subscription mid-cycle.

  • You can cancel your current annual subscription on your device by following the platform instructions above to ensure it does not auto-renew next year.

  • Because annual plans are paid upfront, you will still retain full access to all Premium content until your current annual subscription ends.

How to Check Calm Premium Subscription Pricing

Because Calm is used all over the world, the cost of a Calm Premium subscription varies from country to country. Pricing is based on localized markets, local currencies, and any applicable regional taxes.

To see the exact, up-to-date cost of a subscription for your specific region, please use the links below:

  • Individual Yearly Subscription: To view the current pricing for a standard Calm Premium plan in your country, click here.

  • Family Plan: To view the current pricing for our Calm Premium Family Plan option (which covers up to 6 members), click here.

Website vs. App Pricing

When viewing prices, please note that the cost shown inside the Calm mobile app may sometimes differ from the cost shown on the Calm website.

  • Why does this happen? In-app purchases are managed entirely by the Apple App Store or Google Play, which may have different regional pricing structures or fees.

  • Promotional Discounts: Any discount offers or promotional emails you receive from us will always be based on the subscription price offered through our website, not the app stores.

Try Calm Premium for Free

If you would like to explore our full Premium library of meditations, Sleep Stories, and music before making a purchase, you can sign up for a 7-day free trial here.

For further reading about managing subscriptions and billing, please see our Profile and Billing section here.

How to Check the Status of Your Calm Subscription & Billing Details

You can easily check the status of your Calm Premium subscription to see when your trial or paid plan began, when it is set to renew, or if it is currently active.

How you check your status depends on where you originally purchased your subscription. Please check the Calm app first, or use the device-specific instructions below.

Method 1: Check in the Calm App (All Platforms)

You can access your basic subscription information directly within the Calm mobile app.

  1. Open the Calm app and tap Profile on the home screen.

  2. Tap the Settings (Gear icon ⚙️) in the upper left corner.

  3. Tap Manage Subscription.

  • Note: This page will display details about your subscription status, including when your trial or subscription began. It also includes a link to cancel your automatic renewal if you wish to do so.

Method 2: Check via the Calm Website (Desktop/Browser)

If you purchased your subscription directly through the Calm website, you can view your details online.

  1. Go to the Calm website and log in.

  2. Navigate to your Profile.

  3. Select Manage Subscriptions.

  4. The Manage Subscriptions tab will be automatically selected and will display your current subscription and billing details.

Method 3: Check via Apple (iPhone/iPad)

If you signed up for Calm through the Apple App Store, your subscription is managed directly by Apple.

  1. Open the Settings app on your iOS device.

  2. Tap your Name/Apple ID at the very top of the screen.

  3. Tap Subscriptions.

  4. Look for Calm in your list of Active subscriptions to see your renewal date and status.

  • If you have trouble locating this menu in your device settings, please follow Apple’s official instructions here.

Method 4: Check via Google Play (Android)

If you signed up for Calm through Google Play, your subscription is managed directly by Google.

  1. Open the Google Play Store app on your Android device.

  2. Tap your Google profile photo in the top right corner of the screen (next to the search bar).

  3. In the menu that appears, tap Payments & Subscriptions.

  4. Tap Subscriptions.

  5. Tap the Calm subscription listed there to view your status.

  • Note: If you do not see Calm listed in this menu, it means you do not have an active subscription attached to that specific Google account.

Troubleshooting: Can’t Find Your Subscription?

If your app says you are on a “Free Plan” and you don’t see a subscription listed in Apple or Google Play, you may have signed up using a different email address or Apple ID/Google Account. Please view our guide on what to do if you paid for a subscription but your content is still locked: Paid For a Calm Subscription But Premium Content is Still Locked

How to Purchase a Monthly Subscription

Yes, Calm offers monthly subscription plans! Depending on how you prefer to sign up, there are two ways to purchase a month-to-month subscription.

Note: If you already have an active subscription and simply want to change your billing cycle, please skip these steps and see our guide here: How to Change Your Calm Subscription Plan (Monthly vs. Annual)

Method 1: The Fastest Way (Via the Calm Website)

The easiest way to purchase a monthly subscription without any waiting period is directly through our website.

  1. Visit our personalization quiz at quiz.calm.com.

  2. Answer a few quick questions to help us tailor your Calm experience.

  3. At checkout, you will be presented with our available subscription options. Simply select the Monthly (or Quarterly) plan and complete your purchase!

  4. Once purchased, simply log in to the Calm app on your mobile device using the same email address, and your Premium access will unlock.

Method 2: Purchasing via the Calm App (iOS & Android)

If you prefer to purchase directly through the Apple App Store or Google Play Store, the process requires a few extra steps.

What to Know Before You Begin

Because all new in-app free trials default to an annual subscription, purchasing a monthly plan via the app is a multi-step process. You must first start a free trial, immediately cancel its auto-renewal, and then wait for the trial to end before the monthly option becomes available.

Follow the instructions below for your specific device.

How to Purchase on Apple (iOS)

(Note: Please ensure your Calm app is updated to version 4.2 or higher to see these menus).

Step 1: Start and Cancel the Free Trial

  1. Open the Calm app and tap any locked Premium item to begin your 7-day free trial.

  2. Once the trial is active, you must immediately cancel it so you are not charged for a yearly plan. Tap Profile on your home screen, then tap the Settings (gear icon) in the upper left corner.

  3. Tap Manage Subscription.

  4. Tap the word ‘here’ to be redirected to your phone’s Apple App Store subscription settings.

  5. Find the Calm subscription on your Apple list and select the option to cancel. (If you have trouble finding this, please see Apple’s cancellation instructions here).

Step 2: Wait and Purchase the Monthly Plan

  1. Wait for your 7-day free trial to completely end. Once it expires, the premium content in your Calm app will lock again. 

  2. Tap any locked item in the app. A page displaying subscription options will appear. 

  3. Select the monthly subscription option located on the far left of the screen to begin your monthly plan!

How to Purchase on Google Play (Android)

(Note: Please ensure your Calm app is updated to version 4.0 or higher to see these menus).

Step 1: Start and Cancel the Free Trial

  1. Open the Calm app and tap any locked Premium item to begin your 7-day free trial.

  2. Once the trial is active, you must immediately cancel it so you are not charged for a yearly plan. Tap Profile on your home screen, then tap the Settings (gear icon) in the upper left corner.

  3. Tap Manage Subscription.

  4. Tap the word ‘here’ to be redirected to your phone’s Google Play subscription settings.

  5. Find the Calm subscription on your Google list and select the option to cancel. (If you have trouble finding this, please see Google’s cancellation instructions here).

Step 2: Wait and Purchase the Monthly Plan 

  1. Wait for your 7-day free trial to completely end. Once it expires, the premium content in your Calm app will lock again.  

  2. Tap any locked item in the app. A page displaying subscription options will appear. 

  3. Select the monthly subscription option located on the far left of the screen to begin your monthly plan!

How to Purchase a Yearly Calm Premium Subscription

A Calm Premium subscription unlocks our entire library of guided meditations, Sleep Stories, music, and masterclasses. You can purchase a yearly subscription directly through our website, or via the Calm app on your mobile device.

A Quick Note on Free Trials: If this is your very first time subscribing to Calm, your yearly plan will likely begin with a 7-day free trial! You will be required to enter your payment details to start the trial, but you will not be charged until the 7 days have ended.

Choose your preferred purchasing method below:

Method 1: Purchase via the Calm Website (Recommended)

Purchasing directly through our website ensures your billing is managed directly by Calm, rather than a third-party app store.

  1. Go to www.calm.com on your computer or mobile web browser.

  2. Log in to your existing Calm account (or create a new free account if you don’t have one).

  3. Navigate to our subscription page here: 0

  4. Select the Yearly subscription option.

  5. Enter your payment details and complete the checkout process.

Method 2: Purchase via the Apple App Store (iOS)

If you prefer to have your billing managed by Apple, you can upgrade directly inside the Calm iPhone or iPad app.

  1. Open the Calm app on your iOS device and ensure you are logged in.

  2. Tap on any Premium content (look for the padlock icon) or tap the Premium banner on your home screen.

  3. The subscription upgrade screen will appear. Select the Yearly option.

  4. Confirm your purchase using Face ID, Touch ID, or your Apple ID password.

Method 3: Purchase via the Google Play Store (Android)

If you prefer to have your billing managed by Google, you can upgrade directly inside the Calm Android app.

  1. Open the Calm app on your Android device and ensure you are logged in.

  2. Tap on any Premium content (look for the padlock icon) or tap the Premium banner on your home screen.

  3. The subscription upgrade screen will appear. Select the Yearly option.

  4. Confirm your purchase through the Google Play billing prompt.

Buying for Multiple People? Check out the Family Plan!

If you want to share Calm with your loved ones, a standard yearly subscription is only meant for one user. Instead, check out the Calm Premium Family Plan! It covers up to 6 people under one discounted yearly price. Learn more about purchasing a Family Plan here.

Important Note: Automatic Renewal

All Calm Premium yearly subscriptions are set to automatically renew at the end of your billing cycle. If you wish to turn off auto-renewal, you can manage your subscription settings at any time before your renewal date. For instructions on how to check your subscription status and manage auto-renew, please click here: How to Check the Status of Your Calm Subscription & Billing Details

Missing Subscription from a Facebook or Instagram Ad?

How to activate your new subscription:

When you purchase a Calm subscription through a Facebook or Instagram ad, we immediately send a welcome email to the exact address provided at checkout. To sync your purchase to the Calm app, you must open that welcome email and click the verification link. If you do not see the welcome email in your main inbox, please check your spam or junk folders.

Still can’t find the welcome email?

If you haven’t received the email, it is highly likely there was a typo in the email address entered during checkout or a different email address was used. Because our system uses email addresses to locate and sync subscriptions, we will need to search for your account manually using your purchase details.

What to do next:

To help us locate your missing account as quickly as possible, please submit a ticket to our support team and include the following information in your first message:

  1. Email Addresses & Login Info Please provide any email address that could possibly be tied to the charge, including:

    • Alternate Emails: Any other personal or work emails you might have used or accidentally mistyped.

    • Apple Users: If you used Apple Pay, you may have used the “Hide My Email” feature. See Manage the Hide My Email addresses

    • Facebook Users: If you created your Calm account using Facebook, please provide the email address associated with your Facebook account, as well as your first and last name exactly as they appear on your profile.

  2. Proof of Purchase

    • A screenshot or forwarded copy of your email receipt.

    • If you didn’t receive a receipt: Please provide a clear screenshot or image of the charge directly from your bank or credit card statement.

  3. Specific Purchase Details If you cannot locate a receipt, we will need the following details to search our billing system manually:

    • Payment Method used: Did you enter your card details directly, or did you use Apple Pay or Google Pay?

      • Apple Pay or Google Pay Users: If you used one of these methods, your device likely assigned a unique “virtual card number” to the purchase. We need the last 4 digits of this virtual number, not your physical card.

      • Learn how to find your Apple Pay virtual card number here.

      • Learn how to find your Google Pay virtual card number here.

    • Name on the card used

    • Last 4 digits of the card

    • Expiration date 

    • Exact amount charged

    • Date of the charge

    • Zip/Postal Code of the card

    • Brand of the card (amex, visa, etc.)

  4. Submit a ticket HERE with the requested information

(Note: For your security, please NEVER share your full credit card number).

Paid For a Calm Subscription But Premium Content is Still Locked

If you paid for Calm Premium but your account still shows “locked” content, you’re likely logged into a different account than the one that has your subscription. This guide will help you identify the correct account and regain Premium access.

Why This Happens

This issue usually occurs when:

  • You purchased Calm using one email, but logged in with a different email.
  • You created multiple Calm accounts without realizing it.
  • You logged in using Apple or Google login instead of the email used at purchase.
  • You’re using a work or school email that isn’t the one tied to your subscription.

Step 1: Confirm Where You Purchased Calm

If you purchased through Apple (iOS / App Store)

  • Open the Apple App Store → Tap your profile → Purchases → look for a Calm subscription.

  • The email on your Apple ID is the one tied to your Calm Premium subscription.

  • To access Premium, log in to Calm using the same Apple ID login option.

If you purchased through Google Play (Android)

  • Open Google Play → Tap your profile → Payments & Subscriptions → Subscriptions.

  • The Google account shown here is the one tied to your subscription.

  • Log in to Calm using that Google account login option.

If you purchased on Calm.com

  • Search your inbox(es) for a receipt from hello@breathe.calm.com

  • The email receiving the receipt is the one tied to your subscription.

Step 2: Log Into the Correct AccountTry these steps:

  • Log out of the Calm app or website.
  • Log back in using the email or login method confirmed above:
    • Email
    • Apple login
    • Google login
    • Facebook login
  • Once logged in with the correct account, your Premium access should appear immediately.

Step 3: If You’re Still Unsure Which Email Is CorrectTry these steps:

  • Search all inboxes (personal, work, school) for emails from Calm.
  • Check for a receipt from Apple, Google Play, or Calm.com.
  • Try logging in with any email addresses you commonly use.
  • Try using the Apple, Google or Facebook Login button on the Calm app.

Step 4: When to Contact Calm SupportReach out to Support if:

  • You cannot locate a purchase receipt in any inbox.
  • Apple or Google shows an active subscription, but Calm still shows locked content.
  • You suspect you created multiple Calm accounts and want them merged.
  • You’ve tried all login emails and still see the free version.
  • We’ll help you get access back quickly.
Contact Calm Support

If you’ve reviewed the steps above and still can’t access your Premium subscription, please contact Calm Support and include:

  • The email(s) you may have used
  • Screenshot of your Apple/Google Play receipt, if applicable
  • Description of what you see in the app

We’ll take care of the rest!

Understanding In-App Offers, Free Trials & Unexpected Charges

As a free Calm user, you may occasionally see in-app notifications or upsell screens with various promotions. These offers may include an additional free trial, an extended 14-day trial, or a discount (such as 40% to 50% off a yearly subscription).

 

Eligibility & Checking Out

  • Am I eligible for these trial or discount offers? Not all users are eligible for the offers shown. It is very important that you read the order details carefully prior to checking out. The checkout screen will always show your trial start date, your payment start date, the exact amount to be charged, and your subscription details.

Troubleshooting Unexpected Charges

  • Why was I charged immediately while redeeming an in-app offer? The charge amount and the date of the expected charge are always shown prior to completing a purchase. If you were charged immediately instead of receiving a free trial, it is likely that you were ineligible for that specific offer.

How to Get Help with a Charge (By Platform)

If you need to make changes to your subscription or transfer it to another account, please follow the instructions below based on where you made the purchase.

If you purchased through Apple (iTunes):

  • Changes/Refunds: Apple does not grant Calm the ability to make changes to your Apple-billed subscriptions. You will need to reach out to Apple Support here for additional assistance.

  • Transfer Option: Alternatively, Calm is able to transfer the subscription to another Calm account. Please contact our support team here with the Order ID number (located on your Apple receipt) and provide the email address of the account you’d like to transfer the subscription to.

If you purchased through Google Play:

  • Changes/Refunds: Google Play does allow its developers the ability to make subscription changes. Please contact our support team here for assistance. You will need to provide your GPA number, which is located on your Google Play receipt.

  • Transfer Option: Calm is also able to transfer the subscription to another Calm account. Please contact us and provide the email address of the account you’d like to transfer the subscription to.

Calm Premium Family Plan

Calm Family Plan: Price, Invites & How to Share Premium

Calm Premium Family Plan: Pricing, Invites & Setup

Important: Not the same as Apple Family Sharing
  • Crucial: Calm does NOT support Apple Family Sharing or Google Play Family Library.
  • TheFix: You must purchase the “Calm Premium Family Plan” directly from our website to share access. You cannot share a standard individual subscription bought on the App Store.
How the Family Plan Works

  • Web Cost:$99.99 USD / year (plus tax).

  • Members: 1 Admin + 5 Members (6 accounts total).

  • Privacy: Everyone gets their own private account. You cannot see each other’s history, stats, or favorites.

  • Eligibility: 

    • Household: Members do not need to live in the same household. Friends and family are welcome.

    • Age Requirement: All Family Plan members must be 18 years of age or older.

Managing Subscriptions 

Before you buy, check your current status:

  • If you already have a subscription:

    • Buying a Family Plan does NOT automatically cancel your old personal subscription.

    • Action: You must manually cancel your old subscription (Apple/Google/Web) to avoid double billing.

    • No Pro-Rating: We cannot apply the remaining value of your personal subscription toward the Family Plan cost.

Lifetime Memberships

  • Is there a Lifetime Family Plan?

    • No. The Calm Premium Family Plan is currently available as a yearly subscription only. There is no “pay once forever” option for families.

  • I already have a Lifetime Membership. Can I buy a Family Plan?

    • Yes! You can purchase a Family Plan to share with loved ones without disrupting your personal Lifetime status.

    • How it works: You will become the “Family Admin.” Your account retains its Lifetime status, and you gain the ability to invite 5 others to the annual plan you just bought.

How to Invite People

  1. Purchase: Buy the plan at www.calm.com/family.

  2. Invite: Go to Profile > Settings > Manage Subscription > Manage Members.

  3. Copy Link: Send the unique invite link to your 5 chosen members via text or email.

  4. Join: They click the link, log in to their own free account, and are instantly upgraded to Premium.

Troubleshooting

  • “Link Invalid”: This usually means the member already has an active Premium subscription. They must wait for their current plan to expire before they can join your Family Plan.

  • “Remove Member”: The Admin can remove members at any time from the “Manage Members” screen. The removed member will revert to the free version immediately.

Manage Calm Family Plan: Add/Remove Members & Cancel

How to Manage Your Calm Premium Family Plan

Admin Access Only

  • Who can make changes? Only the Family Admin (the person who purchased the plan) can add/remove members or cancel the subscription.

  • Where to manage: You must log in to the Calm Website to make these changes. These settings are not available inside the mobile app.

How to Add & Invite Members

You can invite up to 5 other people to your plan.

  1. Log in to your account at www.calm.com.

  2. Click Profile (top right) > Settings.

  3. Click Manage Subscription.

  4. Click Manage Members.

  5. Click Invite Members.

  6. Choose a method:

    • Copy Link: Copy the unique URL and paste it into a text/email to your family member.

    • Email Link: Enter their email address to send the invite directly from Calm.

How to Remove a Member

Need to free up a slot?

  1. Go to Profile > Settings > Manage Subscription > Manage Members.

  2. Find the member you want to remove.

  3. Click the “X” icon next to their email address.

  4. Confirm: The member will be removed immediately.

    • Warning: Unlike canceling a subscription (where access continues until the end of the billing cycle), removing a member revokes their Premium access instantly.
How to Cancel the Entire Family Plan

If you no longer want to pay for the Family Plan:

  1. Go to Profile > Settings > Manage Subscription.

  2. Click Cancel Plan.

  3. Follow the prompts to confirm cancellation.

What happens next?

  • You and all 5 members will keep Premium access until the current billing year ends.

  • After the expiration date, all 6 accounts (Admin + Members) will revert to the Free version of Calm.

Privacy & Data

  • Can the Admin see my history?

    • No. The Admin can only see your name and email address to manage the subscription. They cannot see your meditation history, favorites, or check-ins.

  • Does removing a member delete their data?

    • No. If a member is removed from the plan, their account simply downgrades to the Free version. Their history and favorites remain saved on their personal account.

Upgrade to Calm Family Plan: Switching from Individual to Family

Upgrading to the Calm Premium Family Plan

Avoid Double Billing

  • If you pay via Apple or Google: Buying a Family Plan does NOT cancel your old subscription. You must manually cancel it in your phone settings to stop future charges.

  • If you pay via Calm.com: Our system will automatically cancel your old individual plan when you upgrade.

Step 1: Check Your Current Subscription

Before you upgrade, know who charges you.

  1. Open the Calm app > Profile > Settings (Gear icon).

  2. Tap Manage Subscription.

  3. Look for “Apple,” “Google,” or “Calm.com.”

Step 2: The Upgrade ProcessScenario A: I pay via Apple (iPhone) or Google Play (Android)

  1. Cancel First: Go to your phone settings and cancel your current individual subscription: How to Cancel Your Subscription or Free Trial

    • Note: You will keep your remaining paid time.

  2. Wait (Recommended): We suggest waiting until your current subscription expires before buying the Family Plan to get the full value.

    • Why? We cannot refund or pro-rate your unused time.

  3. Buy Family Plan: Visit www.calm.com/family to purchase the new plan ($99.99/year).

  4. Invite Others: You are now the Admin!

Scenario B: I pay directly via Calm.com

  1. Go to Family Page: Log in and visit www.calm.com/family.

  2. Purchase: Buy the plan.

  3. Automatic Switch: Our system will automatically stop your old individual billing and start the new Family billing.

    • Note: The new Family year starts immediately. Any remaining time on your old plan is forfeited (not refunded).

Scenario C: I am a Lifetime Member

  • Can I switch? You cannot “convert” your Lifetime Membership into a Family Plan, nor can you add family members for free.

  • The Solution: You can purchase a Family Plan separately to share with others.

    1. Log in to your Lifetime account at www.calm.com/family.

    2. Purchase the Family Plan subscription.

    3. Result: You retain your personal Lifetime status forever, but you will now also pay the annual fee for the Family Plan slots you share with others.

Frequently Asked Questions

  • Does my history get deleted? No! As long as you log in with the same email address, all your favorites, stats, and session history will transfer to the Family Plan automatically.

  • Can I get a discount for my unused months? No. We cannot apply the cost of your previous subscription toward the Family Plan. This is why we recommend waiting for your current plan to expire before switching.

Canceling a Trial or Subscription

Cancel Calm Free Trial: Website & App Instructions

Cancel Calm Free Trial: Website & App Instructions

Where did you sign up?
  • I signed up via the Website (Calm.com): Follow the steps below.
  • I signed up via the App (Apple/Google): These steps will NOT work for you. Please see:
    • Canceling a Subscription or Free Trial Through Apple
    • Canceling a  Subscription or Free Trial Through the Google Play Store
Cancellation Policy
  • Deadline: You must cancel at least 24 hours before your trial ends to avoid being charged.
  • Access: You will keep your Premium access until the full trial period is over, even if you cancel immediately.
Method 1: Cancel via the Calm App 

Use this method if you started your trial on the website but want to cancel using your phone.

  1. Open the Calmapp.
  2. Tap Profile (bottom right).
  3. Tap the GearIcon (Settings) in the top-left corner.
  4. Tap ManageSubscription.
  5. Tap CancelSubscription.
  6. Confirm: Follow the prompts until you see the final confirmation screen.
Method 2: Cancel via the Website
  1. Log in to www.calm.com/profile.
  2. Go to Settings > ManageSubscription.
  3. Click CancelSubscription.
  4. Confirm: Follow the prompts until you see the final confirmation screen.
Troubleshooting

I don’t see a “Cancel” button. This usually means:

  • You are already canceled (check for an “Expires on” date).
  • You signed up via Apple or Google, not the website. Please check your phone’s subscription settings instead.
  • You are not signed into the correct account. Please try another email or log in method.

Cancel Calm on Android: Google Play Subscription Guide

To cancel a Calm subscription on Android via Google Play, you must manually do so in your Google Play Store subscriptions; uninstalling the app does not cancel it. Cancel at least 24 hours before a free trial ends to avoid charges. Troubleshooting tips and direct links are available in the Calm app settings.

How to Cancel a Calm Subscription on Android (Google Play)

Method 1: “Direct Link” (Fastest)
  1. On your Android phone, tap this link: Manage Google Play Subscriptions.
  2. Find Calm in the list.
  3. Tap CancelSubscription.
  4. Follow the on-screen instructions to confirm.
Method 2: Manual Steps via Play Store App
  1. Open the GooglePlayStore app.
  2. Tap your ProfileIcon (top right corner).
  3. Tap Payments & subscriptions.
  4. Tap Subscriptions.
  5. Select Calm.
  6. Tap Cancelsubscription (bottom of the screen).
  7. Confirm: You may be asked for a reason. Select one and tap Continue > Cancelsubscription.
Troubleshooting

I don’t see “Calm” in my subscriptions. This usually means:

  • You are logged into the wrong Google Account. Check your other emails.
  • You subscribed via the Website (Calm.com) or Apple, not Google Play.
  • It says “Expires on [Date]” instead of “Renews on”. This means you have already successfully canceled. No further action is needed.
  • If you run into any trouble, this Google Play help article includes additional details for canceling your free trial.

Cancel Calm on iPhone: Apple Subscription Guide

To cancel a Calm subscription on Apple via the App Store, you must manually do so in your App Store subscriptions; uninstalling the app does not cancel it. Cancel at least 24 hours before a free trial ends to avoid charges. Troubleshooting tips and direct links are available in the Calm app settings.

How to Cancel a Calm Subscription on iPhone or iPad (Apple)

App Compatibility
  • Calm (The Blue App): Follow the instructions below.
  • CalmSleep: This is a separate app, but is also managed in the same Apple Settings menu described below. Look for the specific app name in your list.
Method 1: “Direct Link” (Fastest)
  1. On your iPhone or iPad, tap this link: Manage Apple Subscriptions.
  2. Find Calm in the list.
  3. Tap CancelSubscription (or CancelFreeTrial).
  4. Confirm: Follow the on-screen prompts.
Method 2: Manual Steps via iPhone Settings
  1. Open your iPhone’s Settings app (grey gear icon).
  2. Tap your Name/AppleID at the very top.
  3. Tap Subscriptions.
  4. Tap Calm.
  5. Tap CancelSubscription.
Troubleshooting: “I don’t see a Cancel button” 

If you are looking at the Calm subscription screen and there is no “Cancel” button, check the text:

  • If it says “Expires on [Date]”: You have already canceled. No further charges will occur.
  • If it says “Renews on [Date]”: The subscription is still active. Look for the cancel button again.
  • If you don’t see Calm listed at all:
    • You might be logged into a different Apple ID.
    • You subscribed via the Website or Google Play, not Apple. Check those accounts instead.
  • Please see this support article from Apple for details on how to manage a subscription. You can contact Apple Customer Support directly at .

Frequently Asked QuestionsHow do I know where I purchased my subscription?

Do I need to contact Calm Support to cancel?

No. All subscriptions must be canceled through the original purchase platform (Calm.com, Apple, or Google Play).

I canceled but still see Calm Premium — why?

That’s normal. You’ll keep Premium access until your current billing or trial period ends.

My trial ended and I was charged — can I get a refund?

Refunds are available only for Calm.com and Google Play purchases within 30 days. For Apple subscriptions, please contact their support directly.

I don’t see my Calm subscription listed in Apple or Google Play. What should I do?

Content Recommendations

Best Calm Content & Meditations for Anxiety

If you are looking for ways to ease anxiety and build a more mindful, less stressful life, Calm offers a wide variety of programs centered around working through those difficult feelings.

Whether you want to sit, breathe, or move your body, here are some of our top recommended programs for anxiety:

Top Recommendations for Anxiety Relief

  • For Immediate Panic Relief: Emergency Calm — A 3-minute rapid relief exercise designed to help ground you quickly when you are feeling actively overwhelmed.

  • For Learning Coping Skills: Overcome Stress and Anxiety — A multi-part series from clinical psychologist and educator Dr. Julie Smith.

  • For Sitting With Your Feelings: Calming Anxiety — A guided meditation from our Head of Mindfulness, Tamara Levitt, designed to relieve physical and mental tension. (Note: You can choose the exact length of this meditation from 3 to 30 minutes!)

  • For Breathwork: Breathe into Relaxation — A guided breathing exercise from Jay Shetty to help regulate your nervous system.

  • For Bedtime: Unwind Into Sleep — A sleep-focused meditation led by Chibs Okereke to help you ease body tension and stop a racing mind so you can drift off.

  • For Physical Movement: Evening Wind Down — A collection of Daily Move sessions from Mel Mah designed to help you use gentle stretching and movement to physically release the anxiety stored in your body after a long day.

How to Find More Anxiety Content

Our library is constantly growing! To find these specific sessions and discover new ones:

  1. Open the Calm app and tap the Discover tab at the bottom of the screen.

  2. Tap the Search icon (magnifying glass) at the top of the screen.

  3. Type “Anxiety” into the search bar to browse our entire catalog of anxiety-related meditations, Sleep Stories, masterclasses, and music.

Looking for even more resources? We highly recommend checking out the Calm Blog * for deeper insights, tips, and tools for leading a calmer life.*

⚠️ Medical Disclaimer Please note: While Calm is a wonderful tool for daily mindfulness and stress management, it is not a replacement for professional medical advice, diagnosis, or clinical therapy. If you are experiencing severe anxiety or panic, please reach out to a qualified healthcare professional.

Best Calm Content & Tools for Advanced Meditators

While Calm is widely known for helping beginners start their mindfulness journey, our library also features robust tools and deep content designed specifically for experienced practitioners looking to sustain or expand an established, silent, or insight-based practice.

If you are looking to transition away from heavy verbal guidance or explore advanced meditation concepts, here are our top recommended tools and programs:

Top Tools and Content for Deep Practice

  • For Silent or Self-Guided Sits: The Less-Guided & Unguided Timers — If you prefer to meditate in silence without a guide, navigate to our Timer feature. You can select an open-ended or timed session, choose a background soundscape, and set ambient interval bells (such as every 5 or 10 minutes) to support your focus.

  • For Extending Your Sitting Time: Adjustable Session Lengths — Many of Calm’s core themed meditations allow you to customize the duration before you begin. You can stretch individual sessions up to 20 or 30 minutes, allowing for more extended periods of silent integration between verbal prompts.

  • For Advanced Theory & Insight: Masterclasses and Advanced Teachers — Go beyond foundational breathing by exploring series led by world-renowned mindfulness teachers known for their deep, systematic approaches to practice. Look for programs by teachers like Shinzen Young, who specializes in advanced insight frameworks.

  • For Deepening Specific Techniques: Vipassana and Loving-Kindness (Metta) — Calm features dedicated, minimalist tracks centered around traditional insight meditation, body scans, and intensive loving-kindness practices that allow experienced meditators to refine their mental clarity and emotional resilience.

💡 Mindful Tip: Minimize Screen Distractions

Advanced traditions (such as Zen or Vipassana) often involve keeping your eyes slightly open, which can make bright menus or moving phone screens highly distracting. To establish a clean environment for your sit:

  • Lock Your Screen: Calm fully supports background audio playback. Once you set your unguided timer or begin your session, you can lock your device screen entirely. The ambient audio track and your interval bells will continue playing normally in the dark.

  • Hide App Menus: If you prefer keeping your device open but want to eliminate visual clutter, simply tap a blank space on your app’s home screen. This instantly hides all buttons and text overlays, shifting the screen into a distraction-free backdrop.

Best Calm Content for Pain Management

If you are living with chronic pain or navigating an illness, mindfulness and meditation can be powerful tools for helping your mind and body cope with physical discomfort. While meditation cannot cure physical pain, it can significantly alter how you perceive and respond to it by reducing stress, calming your nervous system, and easing mental resistance.

Here are our top recommended programs and tools for pain management:

Top Recommendations for Pain Relief

  • For a Structured Approach: 7 Days of Soothing Pain — A flagship course by Oren Jay Sofer that teaches you how to meet physical discomfort with compassion, helping to reduce the emotional suffering often associated with chronic pain.

  • For Physical Tension: Body Scan Meditations — Systematically focusing attention on different areas of the body can help you consciously release the secondary tension and bracing that occurs when we are in pain.

  • For Nervous System Regulation: Breathing Exercises — Using the built-in Breathe tool in the app to practice slow, intentional breathwork can help activate your parasympathetic nervous system, counteracting your body’s automatic stress response to pain.

  • For Light, Mindful Movement: The Daily Move — Gentle stretching can be incredibly beneficial for releasing bodily stagnation and managing chronic discomfort. Note: We highly recommend listening closely to your body and only engaging in light movement that feels entirely safe for you.

  • For Sleep and Mind Distraction: Sleep Stories — Pain often triggers late-night insomnia by keeping your mind locked on the physical discomfort. Tuning into a detailed, relaxing Sleep Story can provide a soothing mental distraction, taking your focus away from the pain so you can drift off.

  • For Acute Pain Flare-ups: Emergency Calm — If a sudden surge of physical discomfort causes acute anxiety or panic, this 3-minute rapid grounding track is designed to help stabilize your breathing and center your mind.

How to Find More Pain Management Content

To find specific sessions for physical discomfort or explore new ones:

  1. Open the Calm app and tap the Discover tab at the bottom of the screen.

  2. Tap the Search icon (magnifying glass) at the top of the screen.

  3. Type keywords like “Pain,” “Healing,” “Body Scan,” or “Movement” into the search bar to browse our related meditations, music, and talks.

⚠️ Medical Disclaimer Please note: While Calm is a wonderful tool for daily mindfulness and stress management, it is not a replacement for professional medical advice, pain medication, physical therapy, or clinical treatment. Always consult with your doctor or healthcare provider regarding chronic pain, injuries, or illness management.

Whether you need help managing physical discomfort, dealing with postpartum anxiety, or building a mindful environment for your growing family, here are our top recommended programs:

Top Recommendations for Parents & Parents-to-Be

  • For the Fourth Trimester: Postpartum Healing — A specialized collection from Kate Johnson focused on all things postpartum, including finding a new sleep rhythm and breathwork exercises for postpartum anxiety.

  • For Raising Mindful Kids: Conscious Parenting — A Calm Masterclass led by clinical psychologist Dr. Shefali Tsabary, offering deep insights on how to create a calm, emotionally supportive environment for your children.

If you are looking for audio to help soothe your children directly, we highly recommend exploring our Calm Kids section! It features a robust library of lullabies, relaxing music, mindfulness exercises tailored for younger minds, and specialized children’s Sleep Stories.

Best Calm Content for Work Stress, Focus & Burnout

Whether you are navigating a demanding project, dealing with meeting fatigue, or trying to leave your work stress at the office, Calm offers a variety of tools to help you stay grounded during the workday.

From deep-focus music to quick mental resets, here are our top recommended programs for managing work stress:

Top Recommendations for the Workday

  • For Deep Work & Productivity: Focus Music & Soundscapes — Need to block out office noise or focus on a big project? Calm offers a massive library of Lo-Fi beats, ambient electronic music, and nature soundscapes specifically designed to help you drop into a flow state.

  • For Quick Reset Breaks: 60-Second Reboot (and other short sessions) — You don’t need 30 minutes to reset your mind. We offer bite-sized, 1-to-3 minute breathing exercises and meditations perfect for clearing your head between back-to-back meetings.

  • For Desk Tension: The Daily Move — Sitting at a desk all day can cause physical stiffness that compounds your mental stress. Tune into brief, chair-friendly movement sessions to release neck, shoulder, and back tension.

  • For Navigating Burnout: Bilateral Stimulation (EMDR) for Burnout Recovery — A specialized audio session that uses alternating left-and-right sound (bilateral stimulation) to help your nervous system process workplace stress, release deep exhaustion, and guide your brain toward recovery. (Note: This track is best experienced while wearing headphones!)

  • For the Commute: Commute Meditations — Creating a boundary between “work time” and “home time” is vital. Our commute meditations help you decompress during your drive or train ride so you don’t carry workplace stress into your evening.

💡 Mindful Tip: Block Your Calendar If your workday is packed, we highly recommend physically blocking out 5 to 10 minutes on your work calendar (like Outlook or Google Calendar) dedicated solely to a quick Calm session. Treating your mental reset like a mandatory meeting helps ensure you actually take the break you deserve!

Adjustable Meditations (Pick Your Length)

Did you know you can pick the exact length for many of our standalone meditations?

  • Breathing: Our unguided ‘Breathe’ sessions can also be extended for longer periods of time.

Pro Tip: Keep the Relaxation Going with Background Scenes

If you want to enjoy an adjustable 10-minute meditation but keep the relaxation going afterward, you can use our background Scenes!

  • If a background Scene (such as rain sounds or ambient music) is enabled, it will continue to play in the background even after your meditation has finished.

  • Where to find Scenes: Tap the Mountain icon in the top left corner of your Home page to choose a Scene. You can adjust the volume of the Scene as needed so it perfectly complements your meditation.

Features and Customization

Calm Music Settings: How to Loop, Shuffle & Autoplay Songs

How to Use Autoplay, Loop & Shuffle for MusicWhat do these settings do?

  • Autoplay: Continues playing music automatically after the current track ends. You can choose to repeat the same song or play the next song in the list.

  • Shuffle: Plays songs from an album or playlist in a random order.

  • Note: These settings are currently available on the mobile app only. The website defaults to autoplaying all tracks.

How to Change Autoplay Settings

  1. Start playing any music track.

  2. Open the Player (full screen).

  3. Look for the Loop Icon (two arrows in a circle) to the right of the Forward button.

  4. Tap the icon to cycle through the three modes:

The 3 Autoplay Modes:

  • Enable Autoplay (All Tracks):

    • Icon: Green Loop Arrows (No number).

    • Action: The app will play the next song in the list/album automatically. It continues until you stop it.

  • Loop Current Track (Repeat One):

    • Icon: Green Loop Arrows with a “1” in the center.

    • Action: The app will play the current song on repeat forever until you stop it.

  • Disable Autoplay (Stop After One):

    • Icon: Grey Arrows.

    • Action: The music will stop completely once the current track finishes.

How to Use Shuffle

  • Enable Shuffle: Tap the Shuffle Icon (crossed arrows) to the left of the Rewind button.

  • Green Icon: Shuffle is ON (Random order).

  • Grey Icon: Shuffle is OFF (Standard order).

  • Important Rule: For Shuffle to work, you must also have Autoplay (All Tracks) enabled. If Autoplay is off or set to “Loop 1,” Shuffle cannot function.

Limitations & Exceptions

The Autoplay and Shuffle features are designed for the “Music” menu. They will NOT appear or work in the following situations:

  • Soundscapes: Nature sounds (Rain, White Noise) cannot be shuffled or autoplayed into other tracks. They only loop themselves.

  • Search Results: If you play a song directly from search results (and it’s not part of an album context), it may not autoplay the next track.

  • Isolated Tracks: If you play a song from “Recently Played,” “Favorites,” or “Downloads” and there are no other music tracks listed in that specific list/carousel, the app has nothing to autoplay next.

Calm Scenes Guide: Change Background, Volume & Playback Timer

Changing Background Scenes & Sound SettingsWhat is a “Scene”? A Scene is the visual background and ambient sound (e.g., Rain on Leaves, Mountain Lake) that plays on the home screen and during meditations.

How to Change the Scene

  1. Open the Menu: Tap the Mountain Icon in the top left corner of the Home screen.

  2. Browse: Scroll through the list of available scenes (Nature, Water, Abstract, etc.).

  3. Download: Tap any scene to download it to your device.

  4. Select: Once downloaded, tap it to set it as your background.

How to Adjust Scene Volume

Is the rain too loud? Or do you want silence?

  1. Tap the Mountain Icon (top left).

  2. Look for the “Scene Volume” slider at the bottom.

  3. Adjust:

    • Slide Left: Lowers the volume.

    • Slide All the Way Left: Mutes the scene completely.

    • Silent Scenes: You can also choose “Silent Earth” or “Silent Clouds” from the menu if you never want background noise.

“Play Outside App” & Duration Settings

Control how long the background sound plays after you close the app or finish a session.

  • Where to find it: Tap the Mountain Icon and look for the Duration Selector (e.g., “0 min”, “1 hour”).

  • How it works:

    • 0 min: The sound stops immediately when you close the app or lock your phone.

    • 1 hour / 4 hours / etc.: The sound continues playing for that duration after you close the app or after a meditation/story ends.

    • Example: If you select “1 Hour” and listen to a Sleep Story, the rain sounds will continue for 60 minutes after the story finishes to help you stay asleep.

Important Notes

  • Sleep Stories: Background scenes do NOT play during Sleep Stories. Each story has its own unique sound design that overrides your home screen scene.

  • Meditations: Your selected scene (and its volume) WILL play in the background during meditations.

Can I Reset My Progress on Multi-Day Programs?

At this time, there is currently no way to reset your progress on our sequential, multi-day programs (such as the 7 Days of Calm). We sincerely apologize for any inconvenience this causes! We recognize that this is a highly requested feature, and we hope to make it available in the future.

Workaround: How to Track Your Repeated Programs

If you would like to repeat a program from the beginning, you can still listen to the older sessions. To keep track of where you left off during your second time through, you can check your recent session history:

  1. Open the Calm app and tap Profile in the bottom menu.

  2. Select the History tab at the top of the screen.

  3. Review your recently completed sessions to see which day of the program you last listened to.

Note: Your session history is currently only available within the Calm mobile app, and cannot be viewed on the Calm website.

Fix Broken Calm Streak: How to Add & Edit History

How to Restore a Broken Streak (Manual History)

Why did my streak break?

  • Music & Soundscapes: Listening to music tracks or soundscapes (like “Rain on Leaves”) does NOT count toward your daily streak. Only Meditations and Sleep Stories count.

  • Time Zones: Traveling can sometimes confuse the app’s clock.

  • The Fix: You can manually add a “missed” session to yesterday (or any past date) to repair the gap and restore your streak.

How to Add a Missing Session Follow these steps to fill the gap in your calendar:

  1. Open the Calm app.

  2. Tap Profile (bottom menu).

  3. Tap History (near the top).

  4. Tap the Plus Button (+) or “Add Session”.

  5. Select the Date: Choose the exact date you missed (e.g., yesterday).

  6. Set Duration: Enter a time (e.g., 10 minutes).

  7. Tap Add.

    • Result: Your streak count will automatically recalculate to include this new session.
How to Delete a Mistake

  • iOS (iPhone/iPad):

    1. Go to Profile > History.

    2. Find the session you want to remove.

    3. Swipe Left on the session.

    4. Tap the Trash Icon (Delete).

  • Android:

    • Currently, you cannot delete individual sessions manually on Android.

    • Note: Having an extra session does not hurt your streak, so deletion is usually unnecessary unless you just want a clean history. Please reach out to our support team if you need it removed.

Troubleshooting

  • “I added the session, but my streak number is still wrong.”

    • Force-close the app and reopen it to refresh the data.

  • “I meditated offline.”

    • Offline sessions should sync when you reconnect to Wi-Fi. If they don’t, simply add them manually using the steps above.

How to Add, Remove, and Access Your Favorites

You can easily save the sessions, music, and stories you love most so you can return to them later.

How to Add or Remove a Favorite

There are two ways you can add or remove a program from your list of Favorites.

  • Visual Guide: A filled-in heart means the content is saved to your Favorites, while an empty (unfilled) heart means it is not.
Method 1: From the Session Player (Mobile & Web)

  1. Start playing the program you want to save.

  2. Tap the Heart icon at the bottom of the session player screen to add it to your Favorites (the heart will fill in).

  3. To remove it: Simply tap the filled-in heart again so it becomes empty.

Method 2: From Your Session History (Mobile App Only)

  1. Tap Profile in the bottom right corner of the Calm app.

  2. Tap the History tab near the top of the screen.

  3. Scroll down below the calendar to see your list of previously completed sessions.

  4. Tap the Heart icon next to any session in the list to add or remove it from your Favorites.

  • Important Note: Your session history can only be accessed in the Calm mobile app at this time. It is not currently available on the Calm website.

How to Find and View All Your Favorites

Once you have favorited content, it syncs across your account so you can access your saved list from any of your logged-in devices.

On the Mobile App (iOS & Android):

  1. Open the Calm app and tap Profile in the bottom right corner.

  2. Select the Library tab located near the top of the screen (in between Dashboard and History).

  3. Look for the carousel labeled Your Favorites.

  4. Tap ‘See All’ next to the carousel title to view your entire list of saved content.

  • Filter your list: At the top of the Favorites screen, you can use the category tabs to easily filter your saved content by type (e.g., All, Collections, Meditations, Sleep Stories, Music, Soundscapes, Movement, Masterclass, and The Spark).

  • Remove a Favorite: You can remove a session directly from this list by tapping the filled-in heart icon next to it.

On the Calm Website (Desktop/Browser):

  1. Log in to your account at Calm.com.

  2. Navigate to the Home page.

  3. Scroll down to the second row, located just below the Popular on Calm carousel.

  4. Your saved content will be displayed in the Favorites row.

How to Change Narrators During a Meditation

While Tamara has written and recorded most of the meditations within Calm, many of our sessions have also been recorded by John Armstrong for users who prefer a male voice.

If a meditation offers multiple voice options, you can easily switch to your preferred narrator right from the playback screen!

⚠️ Not All Meditations Have Multiple Voices

Please note that not all meditations offer multiple narrators. If you follow the steps below and do not see the narrator options, that specific session is currently only available in one voice.

How to Switch Narrators

To change the voice during a session:

  1. Start your chosen meditation in the Calm app, such as 7 Days of Calm

  2. Swipe up on the session player at the bottom of your screen to expand the playback menu.

  3. If multiple narrators are available for that specific session, you will see options to switch between Tamara and John.

  4. Simply tap the name of the narrator you would like to listen to.

Note: The ability to switch between narrators is currently only available on the Calm mobile app, and cannot be done on the Calm website.

Looking for More Voices?

We also offer a wide collection of meditations led by expert guest instructors, who both write and record their own unique guided sessions. You can learn more about finding and listening to these sessions here: Meet the Calm Instructors (Meditation Teachers & Guest Experts

How to Create and Manage Custom Playlists

Our Playlists feature allows you to create customizable playlists composed of your favorite Calm content. At this time, custom Playlists can include a mix of Meditations, Sleep Stories, and Music tracks.

How do I create a custom Playlist?

Playlists can be created directly from a supported audio session:

  1. Start your Meditation, Sleep Story or Music track to open the player screen.
  2. Next, tap the Playlist button next to the ‘share’ icon.
  3. Here, you will have the option to create a new playlist. Enter a title for your new Playlist and tap ‘Save Playlist’.
    • If you already have a Playlist created, you will see an option to add your current session to this playlist, or to create a new playlist.

 

Why can’t I see the Playlist button on my current session?

Currently, there are a few content types within the app that do not support this feature. These content types are:

  • Movement - Daily Move and Calm Body sessions
  • Timed Meditations
  • Manual Sessions
  • Soundscapes
  • Breathing Exercises
  • Taptivities

How do I add sessions to my custom Playlist?

To add a session to your custom Playlist:

  1. Simply start your Meditation, Sleep Story or Music track to open the player screen. 
  2. Next, tap the Playlist button, next to the ‘share’ icon. 
  3. You will then have the option to add the current session to an existing playlist or create a new playlist.

How do I remove sessions from my custom Playlist?

Sessions can be removed directly from the Player screen:

  1. If the session is already playing, tap the Player bar to expand the Player screen.
  2. Tap the Playlist button (next to the ‘share’ icon).
  3. Here, you will see a green check mark next to all Playlists containing the current session. Tap the green check mark to remove the session from the Playlist you are editing. This change will update automatically. 

If the session isn’t playing yet:

  • You can navigate to Profile > Library > My Playlists and then tap ‘See all’ to view your custom Playlist library. Select the Playlist you want to edit, tap the session you wish to remove, and follow the steps above to complete the removal.

How do I delete a Playlist? 

To delete a Playlist entirely:

  1. Navigate to Profile > Library > My Playlists and then tap ‘See all’ to view your custom Playlist library.
  2. Here, open the Playlist you wish to delete. 
  3. Tap the 3 dots button in the top right-hand corner of your screen, and a ‘Remove Playlist’ button will show. 
  4. Click the ‘X’ next to ‘Remove Playlist’ to delete your custom Playlist. 

Please note: Once a Playlist is deleted, it cannot be recovered. 

Where can I find all of my custom Playlists?

You can view all of your custom Playlists in the Profile tab of the app. Once here, select the ‘Library’ button at the top of the page, and scroll down to see ‘My Playlists’. Next, tap ‘See all’ to view your custom Playlists library.

How to Delete Downloaded Content & Clear Device Storage

If the Calm app is taking up too much storage on your mobile device, you can delete your downloaded session files and background scenes to free up space.

What to Know Before You Begin: Deleting by Category

Currently, the option to delete downloads individually is not available. You cannot select a single meditation or single song to delete. The only way to clear downloaded content is to delete all files of a specific category (for example, deleting all downloaded Sleep Stories or all downloaded Music at once).

Steps to Delete Downloaded Audio and Scenes

Follow these steps to clear your saved files:

  1. Open the Calm app and tap Profile on the home screen.

  2. Tap the Settings (Gear icon) in the upper left corner.

  3. Tap Downloads.

  4. Here, you will see a list of the various types of content categories you have saved to your device.

  5. Tap the specific category you would like to remove (e.g., Audio or Scenes) to delete all downloaded files in that category.

How to Re-Download Content Later

Once deleted, you can always re-download content at any time if you need it again.

  • Audio Sessions: Please see our related article on how to for offline listening.

  • Background Scenes: Scenes can be re-downloaded directly from the Scenes menu, which is found by tapping the Mountain icon at the top left corner of your home screen.

  • See How to Download Content & Use the Calm App Offline

How to Download Content & Use the Calm App Offline

You can listen to the Calm app without an internet connection by downloading your favorite meditations, stories, and music ahead of time. This is perfect for flights, commuting, or saving mobile data.

How to Download Meditations, Masterclasses, and Dailies

  1. Open the Calm app and select the program you want to download.
  2. Look for the Download (Cloud) Icon in the top corner of the program description page.
  3. Tap the icon.
  4. Wait for the checkmark to appear inside the cloud, indicating the download is complete.

How to Download Music and Sleep Stories

  1. Start playing the track or story you want to save.
  2. Tap the player bar at the bottom of the screen to expand it.
  3. Tap the Download (Cloud) Icon in the upper right corner. Note: Ensure the session duration bar has filled completely before tapping download.

How to Access Your Downloaded Content

  1. Go to the Profile tab in the app.
  2. Tap Library.
  3. Select My Downloads to view and play your offline content.

Troubleshooting

Download not starting? Try waiting a few seconds for the session to buffer (duration bar fills up) before tapping the download button.

Need to free up space? See How to Delete Audio or Scene Sounds to Save Device Space

How to Find and Listen to Past Daily Practice Sessions

If you missed yesterday’s Daily Practice (such as the Daily Calm, Daily Jay, Daily Trip, or Daily Move) or simply want to listen to an old favorite again, you can easily locate past sessions within the Calm app!

Here are three ways to access past Daily sessions:

Method 1: Replay from Your History

If you have already listened to a session and want to hear it again, it is automatically saved to your History tab:

  1. Open the Calm app and tap Profile in the bottom menu.

  2. Tap the History tab near the top of the screen.

  3. Scroll down beneath the calendar to view your list of completed sessions.

  4. Tap any past session in the list to replay it.

Method 2: Use the Search Feature

If you did not listen to the session when it was originally released, it will not be in your History. However, you can still find it using the search tool:

  1. Tap the Discover tab at the bottom of the home screen.

  2. Tap the Search icon (magnifying glass) at the top of the screen.

  3. Type in the specific name of the daily session, or search for “Daily Calm” (or your preferred program) to browse the available library.

Method 3: Save to Your Favorites

Want to keep a specific Daily session easily accessible for the future? You can bookmark it!

  • While playing the session, tap the Heart icon to save it.

  • To find your saved sessions later, tap Profile, select the Library tab, and open the Your Favorites carousel.

Note: Your session history and the full search function are currently only available on the Calm mobile app, and cannot be accessed on the Calm website.

Troubleshooting: Did Missing a Day Break Your Streak?

If you are tracking down a past session because a missed day broke your meditation streak, don’t worry! You can easily log your missed session to restore your count. Learn how here: Fix Broken Calm Streak: How to Add & Edit History

How to Hide App Menus & View Scenes Only (Zen Mode)

What is Zen Mode?

Zen Mode is a feature that allows you to listen to and enjoy our background Scenes without any buttons, menus, or widgets blocking the image. It hides the app interface so you can view your Scene without disruption.

Here is how to use Zen Mode depending on your device:

How to Use Zen Mode on the Mobile App (iOS & Android)

There are two ways to enter Zen Mode on a mobile device:

Method 1: Tapping the screen

  1. Open the Calm app and go to your Home screen.

  2. Tap the background in a blank space (away from any icons).

  3. All buttons and menus will instantly disappear.

Method 2: Landscape Mode You can also automatically enter Zen Mode by rotating your phone horizontally into landscape mode.

  • Important Note for Android Users: The ability to view background Scenes in landscape mode has been temporarily disabled for Android devices to optimize app performance. If you are on an Android device, please use the tapping method above instead.

  • Troubleshooting for iOS Users: If your iPhone does not enter Zen Mode when you turn it sideways, make sure your iPhone’s system-wide “Portrait Orientation Lock” is turned off in your device’s Control Center.

How to Exit Zen Mode on Mobile: 

To bring your buttons and menus back, hold your phone vertically (in portrait mode) and tap the screen again.

How to Use Zen Mode on the Website (Desktop)

If you are using Calm on a computer web browser, follow these steps to hide the menus:

  1. Go to and select the scene you would like to view.

  2. Check the box labeled “Show scenes when idle”.

  3. Navigate back to the main Home screen.

  4. Wait 15 seconds without moving your mouse or cursor. The buttons will automatically fade away, leaving you with a distraction-free scene. 😌

How to Exit Zen Mode on the Website: 

To bring your menus and buttons back, simply move your mouse or trackpad. The interface will instantly reappear.
