How to Manage Your Calm Health Plan

Your Calm Health Plan is a personalized collection of program and content recommendations based on your mental health screening results, goals, topics, and conditions. You can find your Plan under the Plan tab in the Calm Health app or website, and update it at any time.

What’s in your Plan

Your Plan tab has two views:

  • Overview: your personalized Plan at a glance, showing your top clinical program recommendation, goal-based content, and resources
  • All Recommendations: a full list of your recommendations, split by clinical programs and your personalized goals

Please note: Resources and some other features may not be available in your region or through your sponsor.

 

Changing your goals, topics, conditions, or screening results

To change your goals, topics, conditions, or screening, tap the Update Plan button in the lower right corner of the Plan tab in the Calm Health app or on the website. Tapping it reveals four options:

  • Goals: update what you want to work on
  • Topics: change your areas of focus
  • Conditions: add or update your health conditions
  • Screening: retake the mental health screening

Any changes you make will update your Plan overview and recommendations.

 

What is the mental health screening?

The mental health screening is a short survey that personalizes your Calm Health Plan based on how you are feeling. It asks about your thoughts and feelings over the past two weeks. For more information about the screening, visit our Calm Health Product FAQs.

 

My health condition, topic, or goal isn’t listed

Calm Health strives to be as inclusive as possible, and we are always working to improve the experience. If you don’t see your condition, topic, or goal listed in the app, we’d love to hear from you. Contact our Support team to share your feedback:

  • Calm Health members: Click here
  • Optum/UHC members: Click here

How to Manage your Clinical Recommendations

The Home tab on the Calm Health app and website offers personalized clinical program recommendations to help you get started. These recommendations are tailored to your mental health screening results, goals, topics, or conditions.

Please note that certain product features may not be available in your region or with your sponsor.

 

Dismissing a clinical recommendation

To dismiss a clinical program recommendation from the Home tab:

  1. Tap the menu button (three dots in the upper-right corner of the program).
  2. Choose one of the following options:
    • View the Program
    • Join the Program
    • Dismiss the Program

If you select Dismiss the Program, you’ll be prompted to either:

  • Snooze for 10 Days
  • Permanently Dismiss

After confirming your choice, your Home screen will update accordingly.

How to Search for Content on the Calm App

Whether you are looking for a specific narrator, a Sleep Story title, or a musical artist, you can find them using the search tool on both the mobile app and the web.

On the Mobile App (iOS & Android)

  1. Open the Discover Tab: Tap the Discover icon (magnifying glass) at the bottom of your screen.
  2. Locate the Search Bar: Tap the search bar at the very top of the screen.
  3. Enter Your Keyword: Type the title, narrator (e.g., Harry Styles), or theme (e.g., Anxiety).
  4. View All Results: You must tap “Search” or “Enter” on your keyboard to see the full list of matches.
  5. Filter Your Search: Once results appear, use the tabs below the Search bar (Category & Time) to narrow down your selection.

On the Calm Website

  1. Log In: Go to Calm.com and sign in to your account.
  2. Click Search: Locate the Search option in the top navigation menu.
  3. Browse & Filter: Type your search term. Once results appear, use the tabs below the Search bar (Category & Time) to narrow down your selection.

 

💡 Pro-Tips for Better Results

  • Search by Narrator: If you love a specific voice, just type their name (e.g., “Matthew McConaughey”) to see every session they’ve recorded.
  • Use Broad Keywords: If you can’t remember a title, try searching for a mood or instrument, like “Rain,” “Piano,” or “Focus.”
  • Can’t Find It? Check that your app is updated to the latest version in the App Store or Google Play Store to ensure the search index is current.

How to Set Mindfulness & Bedtime Reminders on the Calm App

Important: App Compatibility

  • Calm: Follow the instructions below.
  • Calm Sleep / Calm Health: Please check your specific app settings menu, as options may differ.

Step 1: Enable Notifications on Your Device Before setting reminders in the app, you must allow Calm to send notifications to your phone.

  • iOS (Apple): Go to Settings > Notifications > Calm and toggle “Allow Notifications” ON.
  • Android: Go to Settings > Apps > Calm > Notifications and toggle ON.

Step 2: Set Your Reminders in the Calm App

  1. Open the Calm app and tap Profile (bottom right).
  2. Tap the Gear Icon (Settings) in the top-left corner.
  3. Tap Notifications.
  4. Select the type of reminder you want to customize:
    • Daily Mindfulness: Set a time to meditate.
    • Bedtime: Get a nudge when it’s time to wind down.
    • MoodCheck-In: Reminders to track how you’re feeling.
    • Gratitude: Prompts to log what you are grateful for.

Customizing Your Schedule Once you tap a reminder type (e.g., Mindfulness), you can:

  • Set the Time: Scroll to choose your preferred hour and minute.
  • Choose Days: Tap the days of the week you want the reminder (e.g., Mon-Fri only).
  • Save: Ensure the toggle next to the reminder name is switched ON.

How to Share & Redeem Calm Guest Passes (30-Day Free Trial)

Calm’s Guest Pass feature allows you to share a 30-day free trial of Calm Premium with friends and family. This grants them full access to our entire library of meditations, Sleep Stories, music, and other Premium features.

What to Know Before You Begin (Eligibility & Rules)

  • Who is eligible to receive a pass? The recipient must be completely new to Calm. They cannot have an active subscription, and they must never have completed a free trial of Calm Premium in the past.

  • Payment information is required: The recipient must enter payment details to start the 30-day free trial. They will not be charged if they cancel before the 30 days are up.

  • Auto-renewal: At the end of the 30-day trial period, the pass will automatically renew into a yearly Calm Premium subscription unless the recipient cancels it.

  • Sending limits: You can send unlimited Guest Passes, but each recipient can only redeem one pass.

How to Share a Guest Pass (For Senders)

Note: Guest passes can only be sent from the Calm mobile app. This feature is not available on the Calm website.

Steps to share:

  1. Open the Calm app and log in to your account.

  2. Tap the Bell Icon in the upper right corner of the Home screen to open the Notification Center Hub.

  3. Tap “Share a 30-day free trial”.

  4. Choose how you want to send your unique link (e.g., via text message, email, or another messaging app).

  5. Note: You can also share from Profile > Dashboard.

How to Redeem a Guest Pass (For Recipients)

If someone sent you a Guest Pass, follow the instructions below based on your device to activate your 30-day free trial.

Option A: On iOS (iPhone or iPad)

  1. Download the Calm app from the App Store.

  2. Click the Guest Pass link that was sent to you.

  3. Select Log in (if you have a free account) or create a new free Calm account.

  4. Follow the on-screen prompts to redeem and activate the free trial securely through the App Store.

Option B: On Android, Web, or Other Platforms

  1. Open the Guest Pass link you received in a web browser (Do not open it directly in the Calm app).

  2. Select Log in (if you have a free account) or create a new free Calm account.

  3. Enter your payment details on the web page to activate the free trial.

  • Troubleshooting: If the link accidentally redirects you to a standard 7-day trial instead of the 30-day offer, try reopening the link using a desktop computer browser.

How to cancel auto-renewal: If you do not wish to be charged for a full year of Calm Premium, you must cancel auto-renewal before your 30-day trial expires.

Frequently Asked Questions

  • Do I need to input my payment details to start my Guest Pass? Yes. Payment information is required to activate your 30-day free trial of Calm Premium through Guest Pass. You will not be charged unless you choose to continue the subscription after the trial period ends.

  • Can I send a Guest Pass to someone who already actively uses Calm? No. Only users who have never trialed Calm in the past and do not have an active subscription are eligible for the 30-day free trial through a Guest Pass.

  • What happened to the Guest Pass Rewards Program? The Guest Pass Rewards program was officially discontinued as of September 2025.

How to Stop Background Scene Sounds in the Calm App

By default, the relaxing background scene sounds in the Calm app will play anytime you have the app open. If you are noticing that the sounds continue playing even after you exit the app or finish a meditation, your “Play Outside App” timer is likely enabled!

Here are three simple ways to manage or stop the background sounds from playing.

Method 1: Turn Off the Background Timer

If you want the sounds to play while you are browsing the app, but want them to stop the moment you close it, you can adjust your duration settings:

  1. Open the Calm app and navigate to your Home screen.

  2. Tap the Mountain icon in the top left corner to open the Scenes menu.

  3. Look for the setting that says “Play sounds outside the app” (Duration Selector).

  4. Tap the duration currently listed on the right and update it to “0 min”.

The sounds will now stop immediately when you close the app or lock your phone.

Method 2: Switch to a Silent Scene

If you prefer to never have any background sounds playing at all—even while you are actively browsing the app—you can switch to one of our silent backgrounds:

  1. Tap the Mountain icon in the top left corner to open the Scenes menu.

  2. Scroll through the options and select either Silent Earth or Silent Clouds.

  3. Tap your selection to set it as your background.

Method 3: Lower the Background Volume

If you do not want the sounds to stop completely but simply find them a bit too loud, you can lower the volume instead of muting it:

  1. Tap the Mountain icon to open the Scenes menu.

  2. Locate the Scene Volume slider at the bottom of the screen.

  3. Drag the slider to the left to lower the background noise to a comfortable level.

Further Reading Want to learn more about how to customize your app’s background? Check out our complete guide here: Calm Scenes Guide: Change Background, Volume & Playback Timer

How to Unlock the Next Day in a Multi-Day Program

If you are listening to a sequential, multi-day program (such as 7 Days of Calm or 21 Days of Calm), the app requires you to fully complete your current day before the next session will unlock.

How to Check Your Progress

To see which days you have completed:

  1. Navigate to the Meditation menu.

  2. Tap on the name of the program you are listening to.

  3. Look at the session circles on the program page.

  • Completed: If a circle is filled in with green, that day is complete and the next day will be unlocked.

  • Incomplete: If the circle is not filled in with green, the app did not register that session as complete.

How to Unlock a Stuck Session

If you do not see a green circle for a session you already listened to, it means the session did not register as complete. To fix this and move on to the next day, simply let the session play all the way through one more time. Once it finishes and the circle turns green, you will be able to tap the circle for the next day!

Troubleshooting: Are you playing from your Favorites?

If you are playing a meditation from the Favorites section of your Library (located in the Profile tab), it will not advance to the next day.

  • Why? The ‘Favorites’ section is meant to save and replay specific days, rather than an entire program. For example, if you favorite ‘Day 2’ of the 21 Days of Calm, tapping it in your Favorites will only ever play ‘Day 2’.

  • The Fix: To advance to Day 3, you must access the main program page through the Meditation menu instead of using your Favorites list.

How to Use Activities in Calm Health

Activities in the Calm Health app offer quick, meaningful actions to help you reach your wellness goals. You receive new Activities each day and can pin, replace, complete, and set reminders for them at any time.

Daily Activities

Each day, you receive 3 new Activities on your Home tab. These refresh at midnight unless pinned.

  • Default Activities: general well-being actions (e.g., a 10-minute walk)
  • Goal-based Activities: personalized actions based on your health goals

Managing your Activities

Tap the Today’s Activities prompt on your Home screen to see your list. Tap any Activity card to open the Activity Sheet, where you can:

  • Pin Activities: Tap Pin to keep an Activity in your daily list (up to 17 pins).
  • Replace: Swap an Activity for a different one.
  • Mark Complete: Tap the “I Did This” button. The button will turn into a checkmark.

Correct a Mistake: If you accidentally marked an activity as done, simply tap the checkmark icon to mark it as incomplete again.

Using Reminders

Reminders send push notifications to your mobile device when it’s time to complete your daily Activities. You have full control over the frequency and timing and can update your preferences at any time.

Setting up a Reminder
  1. Tap the Today’s Activities prompt on your Home screen.
  2. Select + Add Reminder below your activity list.
  3. In the Reminder Detail screen, choose your preferred Days and Time.
  4. Tap Save.
Editing or deleting a Reminder

Tap your existing reminder under the Activities list. From the detail screen, you can:

  • Update the scheduled days or time.
  • Delete the reminder entirely if you no longer need it.    
Device settings required for Reminders

To receive Reminders, you must allow Calm Health to send notifications in your device’s system settings. You will receive an automatic in-app prompt when creating a reminder to allow the required settings.

  • Platform: Required device settingHow to enable

  • iOS: Allow Notifications Settings > Notifications > Calm Health > Toggle Allow.

  • Android:

    AllowNotifications AllowAlarms & Reminders

    Settings > Apps > Calm Health > Notifications > Toggle Allow.

    Settings > Apps > Calm Health > Alarms & Reminders > Toggle Allow.

Note: If notifications are disabled, you will see an alert within your Activities and Reminders. Tapping this alert will take you directly to your device settings to update your preferences.

How to Use Check-Ins (Mood, Sleep & Gratitude Tracker)

Check-ins are a great way to bring more awareness to how you are feeling over time. This feature provides a dedicated space in the Calm app to record your mood, sleep quality, gratitude, and personal reflections.

The 4 Types of Check-Ins

  • Mood: Keep a journal of what is impacting your emotions by choosing from a dozen different moods.

  • Sleep: Track your sleep quality, log the exact amount of sleep you had, and select the various factors that impacted your rest that night.

  • Gratitude: Answer daily prompts (ranging from “What are you grateful for today?” to “What inspired you today?”) and list up to three reasons for your choice.

  • Mindfulness (Daily Calm Reflection): Based on the Daily Calm, you can reflect and answer the specific prompt provided from that day to express your thoughts.

How to Find and Log a New Check-In

To log a new Check-In, follow these steps:

  1. Tap the Discover tab at the bottom of your screen.

  2. Underneath the daily content, locate the Navigation menu.

  3. Swipe left on the Navigation menu and tap Mindful Tools.

  4. Scroll down to the Check-Ins section (located just below Breathing Exercises).

  5. Swipe across the boxes to view the different types of Check-Ins, or tap ‘See All’ to view them on a single page.

  6. Tap the Check-In you wish to complete.

How to View or Delete Your Check-In History

You can review all of your past journal entries and track your progress over time.

  1. Tap Profile in the bottom right corner of the Calm app.

  2. Tap Check-Ins (located just below the Profile title).

  3. Here you can review your entire check-in history.

  4. To delete an entry: If there is a specific log you no longer want, you can easily delete it directly from this screen.

  • Important Note for Android Users: Currently, you cannot add a new Mood Check-In directly from the History screen on an Android device. To log a new mood, you must follow the “How to Find and Log a New Check-In” steps outlined in the section above.

Troubleshooting: Missing Pre-Meditation PromptsI accidentally selected ‘Never Show Again’ on the pre-meditation Mood Check-In. How can I turn these check-ins back on? 

  • To bring back the automatic pre-meditation Check-In prompt, you will need to completely delete and reinstall the Calm app on your device.

  • Don’t worry! Deleting the app from your phone will not delete your Calm account or erase your data. After reinstalling the app, simply log back in. All of your subscription details, session history, past check-ins, and favorites will still be there.

  • Streak Safety Net: If deleting and reinstalling the app causes you to get signed out and accidentally miss a day, you can easily restore your streak. Please see our guide on How to Add & Edit Your History to Fix a Broken Streak here: Fix Broken Calm Streak: How to Add & Edit History

How to Use Emotions Check-In on the Calm Health App

Emotions Check-In is an interactive, science-backed feature to decode emotions and deliver personalized next steps within the Calm Health app. Please note that this feature is only available for US mobile users at this time. 

You can select the emotion that most closely matches your current emotional state and intensity, input additional information that might offer insight into why you feel a certain way, and ultimately, receive suggestions and recommendations based on the emotion you selected. 

What is the clinical basis of the Emotions Check-In feature?

The Emotions Check-In feature is based on research by Dr. Paul Ekman, who tied emotions to facial expressions. The primary emotions included in this feature are: Anger, Enjoyment, Sadness, Disgust, and Fear. Each emotion will also have between 7-11 sub-emotions ranging in intensity. 

How can I access the Emotions Check-In feature?

You can access the Emotions Check-In feature via the Home screen. It is below the “For You” section and above the “Today’s Dailies” section on the Home screen. 

How can I use the Emotions Check-In feature?

  • You will select from 1 of 5 primary emotions. 
  • The primary emotion is selected, you will select a sub-emotion, ranging in intensity level. 
    • The circle will change in shape and volume will fluctuate to reflect change in intensity.
  • Upon selecting a primary emotion and sub-emotion, you will tag the reasons that may contribute to feeling that emotion. 
  • After completing the Emotions Check-In, you will see the completion screen. 

 

 

 

 

 

What happens after the Emotions Check-In is complete?

  • You will see 3 pieces of content recommendations. 
  • The Mind-Body Connection section will display how the top-line emotion may manifest in physical sensations. 
  • You will also see a summary of the insights gathered intensity level (sub-emotion) and factors of its impact. 

How can I access my Emotions Check-In history?

You will see a history of Emotions Check-Ins from most recent to oldest for all Emotions Check-Ins. You can click the “View All” button to show all Emotions Check-Ins. 

You can delete specific Emotions Check-Ins by tapping the three dots in the upper right corner.

How to Use Journaling in Calm Health

Journaling is available to Calm Health users 18+ with their app language set to English. Not all Calm Health members will have access to this feature. For questions about access to this feature, please contact your HR, benefits, or health plan administrator.

Journaling in the Calm Health app uses AI-guided prompts to help you reflect on and practice skills learned in specific clinical programs. Your entries are saved automatically in your Profile, so you can revisit your progress at any time. This feature is not an AI chatbot nor therapy. It does not give advice, diagnosis, or health recommendations.

Where to Start a Journal Entry

You can access Journaling from the Profile tab, or after completing certain sessions within the following clinical programs:

  • Anxiety Survival Guide — Dr. Julie Smith
  • Anxiety 2.0: Next Level Tools for Anxiety — Ian Fishman
  • Build Stress Resilience — Chibs Okereke

Completing a Journal Entry

Tapping “Start Journaling” from the Profile tab or your clinical session resources walks you through a short, guided reflection:

  1. Answer each prompt in the space provided by typing or using voice-to-text. There is a 1,000 character limit per response.
  2. A progress bar shows how far along you are in the exercise.
  3. After each response, you’ll see a short AI-generated reflection, before moving to the next prompt.
  4. Tapping “Finish” at the end of the session will save the entry to your Profile.

Note: You don’t have to complete a journal entry. You can skip the exercise or cancel at any point. If you cancel, that entry won’t be saved or show up in your Profile.

Viewing and Managing Your Entries

To see your past Journal entries, go to Profile:

  • Your 3 most recent entries appear with an AI-generated title and summary.
  • Tap View All to see your full entry history.
  • Tap any entry to read the full reflection.
  • You can delete an entry at any time by opening the individual entry and using the three dots in the upper right corner, then tapping Delete Journal Entry.

Once you submit an entry, you can’t edit it. If you’d like to make a change, you’ll need to delete the entry and start a new one.

Who Can See Your Journal Entries

Your journal entries are completely private to you. Your employer, health plan, or organization cannot see the contents of your individual entries or usage within the Calm Health app. For more information, please see our Privacy Policy. 

Please note: While your entries are private, our system does scan for language that may raise a safety concern. If something you write is flagged, you’ll see a supportive message along with a crisis resource banner. You can tap the banner for more information, or continue on to the next part of your entry.

How to Use Playlists in Calm Health

Playlists allow you to create custom collections of your favorite Meditations, Sleep Stories, and Music tracks within the Calm Health app. At this time, Playlists can only be created, edited, and deleted on the mobile app.

Creating a Playlist

  1. Start your Meditation, Sleep Story, or Music track to open the player screen.
  2. Tap the Playlist icon (+☰) next to the playback speed control.
  3. Select Create New Playlist.
  4. Enter a title for your Playlist, and tap Create. Your playlist is created, and your current session is added automatically.
  5. Tap Save to save your changes.

Please note: Clinical Programs and Infinite Music sessions aren’t supported with Playlists. If the Playlist icon doesn’t appear on a session, that content type isn’t supported.

Adding a Session to a Playlist

  1. From your current session player screen, tap the Playlist icon (+☰).
  2. You’ll see your existing playlist(s) listed, along with the option to create a new one.
    • To add to an existing playlist: Tap the circle next to each playlist you’d like to add the track to. If you only have one playlist, it’s selected automatically. If the track is already in a playlist, that playlist shows as selected. 
    • To create a new playlist: Select Create New Playlist and follow the same steps above. 
  3. Tap Save to save your changes. 

Locating Your Playlists

Once you’ve created a playlist, a Your Playlists section appears on your Home tab.

  • Tap View All to see your full playlist library.
  • Tap into any playlist to see the full details, including track count and total runtime. From here, you can play the playlist or shuffle it.

Removing Tracks or Deleting a Playlist

  • Remove a track: Open the playlist from the Home tab, tap the dot menu next to the track, and select Delete.
  • Delete a playlist: Open the playlist from the Home tab, tap the dot menu, select Delete Playlist, then confirm.

How to Use the Calm App to Fall Asleep: Sleep Timers & Playlists

Whether you prefer to drift off to music, a guided meditation, a Sleep Story, or background nature sounds, Calm has features to help you fall asleep peacefully.

Important Tip: Conserve Your Battery 

For any of our programs, please be sure to lock your phone screen after beginning your session. This will help to conserve your phone’s battery as the session plays.

Choose your preferred listening experience below for specific setup instructions:

Option 1: Using Calm Music (The Music Sleep Timer)

If you would like to listen to a music selection as you are falling asleep, we recommend using the dedicated Music sleep timer.

Steps to set a Music timer:

  1. Start playing a music track.

  2. Tap the player bar at the bottom of the screen to open the full player.

  3. Tap the Clock icon located just above the pause button.

  4. Select the amount of time you would like the music to play.

  • What happens next: The Calm app will close when the music timer ends. Additionally, by using the app’s autoplay settings, you can choose whether to play the same song on repeat or advance to the next song on the album or playlist.

Option 2: Using Meditations or Sleep Stories (The Scene Timer)

If you would like to listen to a meditation or Sleep Story as you fall asleep, you can adjust your background Scene settings.

  • Note: Individual Sleep Stories themselves do not have a sleep timer function. Instead, you use the “Play sounds outside the app” setting to control the amount of time that your chosen background Scene sound will continue playing after your meditation or Sleep Story has ended.

Steps to adjust the Scene timer:

  1. Tap the Mountain icon on the top left-hand corner of the home screen to open the Scenes menu.

  2. Find the “Play sounds outside the app” setting listed at the top.

  3. Choose the amount of time you would like Scenes to play after your story or meditation has ended.

  • For more detailed instructions, see our help article on the Scene settings here.

Option 3: Using Curated Sleep Playlists

You can seamlessly listen to hours of our most popular Sleep Stories with our curated Sleep Playlists. Each playlist is about 3-5 hours long, and some include a soothing soundscape at the end.

Steps to use a Sleep Playlist:

  1. Go to the Sleep content library.

  2. Tap on the “Playlists” tab.

  3. Select a playlist and ensure your autoplay settings are turned on correctly so the tracks play continuously.

  • Using a timer with playlists: You can also set a sleep timer so the playlist will stop after a certain amount of time. Just make sure your sleep timer is set for a shorter amount of time than the playlist’s actual length.

Option 4: Using Background Scenes to Fall Asleep 

If you only want to listen to a background “Scene” (like rain or white noise) without a story or music:

  1. Open the Scenes menu (the Mountain icon).

  2. Set the “Play sounds outside the app” timer to the amount of time you would like your Scene to play.

  3. Turn your screen off. The background sounds will continue to play until the timer ends.

  • For more detailed instructions, see our help article on the Scene settings here.
Recommendations & Feedback

  • Need suggestions? If you’d like some recommendations for content to fall asleep to, please see this article.

  • Have feedback? Have questions or feedback about Calm’s sleep stories? Share your feedback for our team to review by reaching out to us here.

Calm’s Blog also includes articles and resources on setting yourself and your bedroom up for a successful night’s sleep:

We’ve also created The Sleep Superpower Playbook, which includes some tips and techniques, as well as answers to common questions that may come up on your journey to better sleep. You can download the playbook on our blog here.

How to View Your Meditation Stats, History, and Streak in Calm

You can view your meditation stats, session history, calendar, and streak from the Dashboard in the Calm app. The Dashboard is available on all platforms. From the History section, you can also edit past sessions and replay meditations — including Daily Calms you’ve already listened to. Session history is only available in the app, not on the Calm website.

How to access your meditation stats and history

These steps apply to all platforms (iOS, Android, and desktop app).

  1. Tap the Profile button on your home screen.
  2. Tap Dashboard in the upper right corner.
  3. You will see an overview of your meditation stats.

What you can see in the Dashboard

The Dashboard includes:

  • An overview of your meditation stats
  • A History section with a list of meditations you have completed
  • A calendar showing the days you meditated
  • Your current meditation streak

What you can do from the History section

From your History, you can:

  • Edit your session history
  • Replay past meditations, including Daily Calms you have already listened to

About your meditation streak

Your streak shows how many consecutive days you have completed a meditation or Sleep Story session. You can view your current streak from the Dashboard.

What counts toward your streak:
  • Meditations
  • Sleep Stories
  • Breathe Bubbles
  • The Spark
  • Calm Body
  • Masterclasses
What does not count toward your streak:
  • Music tracks
  • Soundscapes (such as “Rain on Leaves”)
  • Check-Ins

If your streak looks wrong or has broken, you can manually add a missed session to repair it. See Fix Broken Calm Streak: How to Add & Edit History for full instructions.

Viewing your history on the Calm website

At this time, session history is not available on the Calm website. You can always view your meditation stats, history, and streak by using the Calm app.

Troubleshooting: Background Scene Sounds Won’t Play

If you open the Calm app and the background ambient noise (like the sound of rain, crickets, or a crackling fireplace) is completely silent, a setting may have been accidentally adjusted.

(Please note: This guide is specifically for missing background sounds on your home screen. If you cannot hear your guided meditations or Sleep Stories either, please see our broader guide here: How to Fix Calm Audio Stopping or Pausing During a Session

Follow the steps below to restore your background scene sounds:

Fix 1: Check the In-App Scene Volume

The Calm app has a dedicated volume slider just for background scenes. It is very common for this to accidentally get turned all the way down!

  1. Open the Calm app to your home screen.

  2. Tap the Mountain icon (Scenes) in the top-left corner of the screen.

  3. Look for the Scene Volume slider.

  4. Drag the slider to the right to increase the volume.

Fix 2: Check Your Device’s Audio and Hardware Settings

If your in-app slider is turned up, your device’s external settings might be blocking the sound:

  • Check the Mute Switch (iPhone/iPad): Check the physical Ring/Silent switch on the left side of your Apple device. If you can see an orange line, your phone is on silent. Flip the switch toward the screen to turn the ringer back on.

  • Check Bluetooth Connections: Your phone may still be connected to a pair of wireless headphones, an external speaker, or a car stereo in another room. Toggle your device’s Bluetooth off and back on to ensure the audio isn’t being routed elsewhere.

Fix 3: Change the Scene to Force a Refresh

Sometimes, a specific scene’s audio file gets stuck while downloading or loading. Changing the scene can force the app to refresh the audio.

  1. Tap the Mountain icon (Scenes) in the top-left corner.

  2. Swipe through the available scenes and select a completely different one.

  3. Wait a few seconds to see if the new scene’s audio begins playing.

  4. If it works, you can switch back to your original favorite scene and it should now play correctly!

Fix 4: Force Quit and Restart the App

If the volume is up and changing the scene didn’t work, the app may just need a quick reset.

  • Force close the Calm app completely on your device, and then reopen it.

  • If the issue persists, check your device’s App Store or Google Play Store to ensure you are running the most recent version of the Calm app.

Troubleshooting: Not Receiving Mindfulness Reminders

If you have set up a mindfulness reminder in the Calm app but are not receiving it, the issue is usually related to your device’s notification settings or a recent change to your reminder schedule.

Here is how to make sure your reminders are delivered successfully:

1. Verify Your Reminder Settings in the App

First, make sure your reminder is actively saved and toggled on within the Calm app:

  1. Tap Profile in the bottom menu.

  2. Tap the Gear icon (Settings) in the top left corner.

  3. Select Mindful Reminders and ensure your desired times are toggled on.

2. Enable Push Notifications on Your Device

Calm’s mindfulness reminders are sent as standard push notifications. If your phone or tablet is currently set to block notifications from the Calm app, your reminders will not appear.

  • Please check your device’s main settings to confirm that push notifications are enabled for Calm. (Need help with this? Follow the device-specific instructions here: How to enable push notifications)

3. Check Focus or Do Not Disturb Modes

Even if push notifications are fully enabled for the Calm app, modern smartphones have aggressive focus features that can suppress alerts. Double-check that your device’s Do Not Disturb, Sleep Mode, or Focus Mode isn’t actively silencing notifications at the time your reminder is scheduled to go off.

4. Note the 24-Hour Update Window

If you recently updated the time of your reminder, please note that it can take a full 24 hours for the new schedule to take effect.

  • For example: If you change your daily reminder from 2:00 PM to 5:00 PM today, the system may not send the notification at the newly requested time until 5:00 PM tomorrow.

Troubleshooting: The Daily Practice (Dailies) Won’t Update

If the Daily Practice on your Calm app home screen hasn’t updated to today’s session, your app may just need a quick manual refresh. Please try the following troubleshooting steps in order to sync your app and load the newest Dailies.

How to Refresh Your DailiesStep 1: Use Pull-to-Refresh

  1. Open the Calm app and navigate to the Home screen.

  2. Drag your finger down from the top of the page.

  3. A loading icon will appear, which should instantly refresh the Dailies at the top of your screen.

Step 2: Force-Quit the App and Check Your Connection 

If the pull-to-refresh method does not work:

  1. Ensure your device is currently connected to a strong WiFi or cellular data signal.

  2. Completely close (hard-quit) the Calm app. See How to Fix Calm App Crashes, Freezing, or Loading Issues (Troubleshooting) for additional troubleshooting tips.

  3. Re-launch the Calm app to force a new connection to our servers.

Step 3: Log Out and Log Back In 

If the app is still stuck on a previous day, logging out of your Calm account and logging back in will force your profile to re-sync. Once you log back in, the newest meditation should appear.

Still having trouble? 

If you have tried all of the steps above and your Daily Practice is still not updating, we are here to help! Please write to our support team directly at here so we can investigate further.

For Dependents

How to Invite or Remove a Dependent on Your Calm Account

Important Age and Access Warning: Dependents must be 16 years of age or older to redeem a Calm Premium invite. Removing a dependent from your benefit will result in their immediate loss of access to all Calm Premium content.

You can invite and remove dependents from your Calm benefit at any time. To manage your dependents, you must log into your Calm account using a web browser or desktop computer.

Note: You cannot manage dependents from inside the Calm mobile app.

How to Invite or Remove a Dependent

  1. Log into your account on a desktop or laptop browser at Calm Website.

  2. Click on Profile in the top navigation.

  3. Click on the Manage Subscription tab.

  4. Click the Manage Dependents button.

  5. Select your next action:

    • To Invite a Dependent: Click the Invite Dependents button. You can then copy the direct link or click Email Link to send it directly.

    • To Remove a Dependent: Click the X next to the dependent’s email address.

Troubleshooting: Missing the “Manage Dependents” Option?

If you do not see the Manage Dependents option, please double-check that you are logged into the specific Calm account where your employer-provided subscription was originally redeemed. This setting will not appear on secondary accounts.

How to redeem Calm for Dependents

Important: You must click the invitation link and accept the invite using a web browser (like Safari or Chrome). You cannot redeem the initial invitation inside the Calm mobile app.

If an employee has shared their Calm benefit with you, you will receive an invitation link either via an email notification or directly from the employee.

Follow these steps to activate your account:

Step 1: Accept the invitation

  1. Open the email notification or the direct link provided by the employee.

  2. Click the Accept Invite button or link. This will open your web browser.

Step 2: Log in or create an account

Once the web browser opens the Calm portal, follow the on-screen prompts:

  • If you already have a Calm account: Log in with your existing email and password.

  • If you are new to Calm: Click Create an Account and follow the registration steps.

Troubleshooting: What if I already have a subscription?

If you currently have an active Calm Premium subscription, the dependent benefit cannot override it automatically. You must cancel your current billing first.

  1. Cancel your active subscription by following the steps in How to cancel my subscription.

  2. Wait for your current billing cycle to end (your account will revert to the free version).

  3. Return to your invitation link and follow the steps above to redeem your free access as a dependent.

Need more help?

For common questions regarding eligibility and plan limits, please see our Calm for Dependents FAQ.

How to Switch to and Use a Calm for Dependents Subscription

Important Note on App Limitations and RefundsDo not use the app to redeem your invite: You must accept your Calm for Dependents invitation using a web browser (desktop or mobile), not within the Calm app itself.

Refund eligibility: Full refunds for existing annual subscriptions are only available within 30 days of purchase and vary by payment method (detailed below).

If you are transitioning an existing, paid Calm Premium subscription over to a free Calm for Dependents benefit provided by a family member or friend, your saved favorites, downloads, and session history will not be lost.

Follow the steps below to make the switch seamlessly.

Step 1: Cancel your current subscription

Before you can activate your new dependent benefit, you must stop the auto-renewal on your existing paid account.

  1. Cancel your current active subscription by following the step-by-step guide in our article: How to Cancel Your Calm Subscription.

  2. Once canceled, your account will remain eligible to accept a dependent invite.

Step 2: Accept your dependent invitation

  1. Locate the invitation link sent to you. This will arrive via email or be shared with you directly by the employee providing the benefit.

  2. Open a web browser (like Safari or Chrome). Do not use the Calm app for this step.

  3. Click the Accept Invite button within the notification or link.

  4. Log in using your existing Calm account credentials to merge your history with the free benefit.

Frequently Asked Questions

Can I get a refund for my previous yearly subscription?

Refund eligibility depends entirely on how and when you purchased your original subscription:

  • Purchased via Calm.com or Google Play Store: We happily issue refunds for annual subscriptions purchased within the last 30 days. We cannot issue refunds for purchases older than 30 days. Please view How to Request a Refund to submit a request.

  • Purchased via Apple App Store: Apple manages its own billing. You must request a refund directly through them. While they typically honor requests within 30 days, it is at their sole discretion. Contact them via Apple Customer Support.

Who is eligible to join as a dependent?

Employees with a Calm benefit can invite a select number of family members or friends.

  • Age Requirement: All dependents must be 16 years of age or older to create an account.
Do dependents share a single Calm account?

No. Each dependent creates and logs into their own individual account using a unique email address and password.

Your session history, daily streaks, check-ins, and personal statistics are completely private. They are never shared with other dependents or the employee who gifted you the benefit.

For Employees, Students, and Members

Accepting a Friends and Family Invite for Calm Health

If someone with a Calm Health benefit has added you as a friend or family member, you will receive a unique invite link via email, text message, or directly from them. Follow the steps below to complete your registration.

Please note: You must be 13 years of age or older and permanently reside in the United States, or 18 years of age or older and permanently reside in Canada, the European Union, or the United Kingdom to access Calm Health. You must also be in the same country as the person who invited you.

Completing your Calm Health registration

  1. Download the Calm Health app first, before clicking your unique invite link.
  2. Launch the Calm Health app. You will see two options:
    • Create a Calm account: select this option if you do not have an existing Calm account and continue with your preferred sign-up method.
    • Log in to your Calm account: select this option if you already have a Calm account and continue with your existing email address or sign-in method.
  3. Enter a valid phone number to set up Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA) and select your preferred verification method (text message or phone call). To learn more about MFA for Calm Health, visit this page.
  4. Enter your First and Last name and accept the Terms of Service and Privacy Policy. Tap Continue.
  5. Select your country of residence to confirm your eligibility for the Friends and Family benefit.
  6. Once complete, you will see a success page confirming your Calm Health registration. You will then be prompted to complete a brief survey before being taken to your personalized Plan.

For answers to common questions about the Friends and Family benefit, visit our Friends and Family for Calm Health FAQ.

Calm Premium Through Your Organization: Common Questions About Access and Redemption (FAQ)

How do I redeem my subscription access?

For steps on redeeming your organization-provided subscription, see How to Redeem Your Organization’s Free Calm Premium Subscription.

Can I redeem the subscription if I already have a paid subscription on my account?

You can redeem your organization’s subscription once your current paid subscription has been canceled. For instructions, see How to Cancel Your Subscription or Free Trial.

My organization now provides Calm Premium for free. Can I get a refund for my previous purchase?

We can refund yearly subscription purchases made within the last 30 days through the calm.com website or the Google Play Store. We’re unable to refund purchases older than 30 days. For details on eligibility and how to request a refund, see Receiving a Refund.

I received an error while trying to redeem my Calm benefit. What now?

See our Troubleshooting: Error Messages When Redeeming a Calm Benefit guide for next steps.

I redeemed my subscription, but Premium content still shows as locked. What are my next steps?

Try these steps in order:

  1. Confirm you’re signed in with the same email and password you used to redeem your subscription.
  2. On the Calm website: Click Log in in the upper right corner.
  3. On the Calm app: Tap Profile, then Sign up or Log in. If you see “Create your Calm account,” tap Log in at the bottom instead.
  4. If you don’t see a login option: Log out of the Calm app first (Profile > Settings gear icon > Log out), fully quit the app, then relaunch it and log back in.
  5. Still not working? Make sure your Calm app is updated to the latest version.

If content is still locked after trying these steps, see Troubleshooting: Premium Content is Locked After Redeeming an Organization Benefit.

Can I keep using my Calm account after I leave my organization?

Yes! You’ll keep your Calm account, stats, and history, but you’ll lose access to the Premium subscription provided through your organization.

If you signed up using your work email, you can switch to a personal one: tap Profile > Settings > Account Info, then update your email address.

Please Note: We do not recommend deleting your Calm account as a troubleshooting step while trying to redeem your organization’s subscription. Deleting your account can complicate the redemption process rather than fix it.

Still need help? If you’re still having trouble accessing your employer-provided subscription, or if these steps didn’t resolve your issue, please submit a request directly to our Calm Support Desk.

Friends and Family for Calm Health FAQ

Not all Calm Health members are eligible for Friends and Family. Eligibility is determined by your sponsorship details, as provided by your organization. For questions about this benefit, please contact your HR, benefits, or health plan administrator. 

I already have a subscription to Calm Premium. How do I switch to accept a Friends and Family invite for Calm Health?

To clarify, Calm has multiple apps: the Calm app, and the Calm Health app. You can learn more here: 0

At this time, the regular Calm app and the Calm Health app are separate offerings. You can have a Calm Premium subscription and accept an invite to Calm Health at the same time. However, you will not be able to share your existing Calm app data with the Calm Health app. Additionally, streaks and session history from Calm will not carry over to the Calm Health app and vice versa.

To accept your invite, please see: Accepting a Friends and Family invite for Calm Health

 

Now that Calm Health is provided to me for free as a friend or family member, can I receive a refund for my previously purchased Calm Premium yearly subscription for the regular app?

We’ll be happy to offer a refund for yearly Calm Premium subscription purchases that were made within 30 days through the calm.com website or the Google Play Store. However, we are unable to issue refunds for purchases that are older than 30 days. 

Customers who subscribed to Calm through the Apple App Store will need to reach out to Apple directly with any refund requests. They are typically able to honor all refund requests within 30 days of the purchase date (but it’s at their discretion). You can contact Apple Customer Support directly at 0 

To learn more about our refund policy and how to request a refund, please view the following Help Center article: Receiving a Refund  

Who can join as a Friend or Family Member?

If you are eligible for the Friends and Family for Calm Health benefit, you can select up to five individuals to receive free access to Calm Health. Friends and family members must be 13 years or older and permanently reside in the United States unless otherwise noted, or 18 years or older and permanently reside in Canada, the European Union, or the United Kingdom. The Friend or Family member will need to be in the same country as the employee who has the Calm Health benefit. 

Please note that certain product features may not be available in your region or with your sponsor.

 

Do Friends and Family members share a single Calm Health account, or do we each get our own account?

Each friend or family member has their own individual Calm Health account and logs in using a unique login method. Personalized plans, mental health screening results, and other personal information tied to an individual’s Calm Health account are not shared between the Calm Health plan admin and friends or family members.  

Can anyone else see my session history or account activity?

No, individual usage information is not shared with other users on the Friends and Family benefit for Calm Health. For more information, please see our Privacy Policy.

How to invite Friends and Family

Not all Calm Health members are eligible for Friends and Family. Eligibility and available features are determined by your sponsorship details, as provided by your organization. For questions about this benefit, please contact your HR, benefits, or health plan administrator.

If you are eligible for the Friends and Family benefit, you can select up to five individuals to receive free access to Calm Health. Friends and family members must meet the following requirements:

  • Be 13 years of age or older and permanently reside in the United States, unless otherwise noted
  • Be 18 years of age or older and permanently reside in Canada, the European Union, or the United Kingdom
  • Reside in the same country as the Calm Health member sending the invite

Optum / United Healthcare members: The Friends and Family option will not appear in your Calm Health account. If your health plan includes dependent eligibility, dependents on your insurance plan who are 13 years or older may be able to redeem a Calm Health sponsorship using their own eligibility details through the myUHC or Optum Live and Work Well website. For further assistance, dependents can contact us here.

Inviting via the Calm Health app 

  1. Launch the Calm Health app. From the home screen, tap the Profile tab in the bottom right of the navigation panel.
  2. Scroll to the bottom of this page and tap Manage Friends and Family. 
  3. Tap the plus sign+ next to each invite and type in the nickname of the friend or family member being invited.
  4. Tap Share Invite and use the sharing options available on your device to send the invite to your friend or family member.

Please note: Each invite has a unique link that expires after 30 days. If an invite link has expired, you can generate and send a new unique link or remove the recipient by clicking on their nickname. 

Inviting via the Calm Health website

  1. Launch the Calm Health website. From the home screen, click the Profile tab on the upper navigation panel.
  2. Scroll to the bottom of this page and tap Manage Friends and Family. 
  3. Click the plus sign + next to each invite and type in the nickname of the friend or family member being invited.
  4. Click Copy link or Email link to send the invite to your friend or family member with your preferred delivery method.
  5. Click Invite Sent to save your friend or family member’s invite.

To remove a friend or family member:

  1. From the Manage Invites screen, click on the arrow next to your friend or family member’s nickname.
    • If the friend or family member has not accepted their invite, click Withdraw Invite.
    • If the friend or family member has already accepted their invite, click Revoke Access.

Important: Once access has been revoked, the friend or family member will lose access to the full Calm Health experience but will retain access to their account.

If you have questions about your Calm Health benefit offerings, please contact your HR, benefits, or health plan administrator for clarification.

How to Join and Manage Calm Pathways

⚠️ Important: Pathways are currently only available in English. If your Calm app language is set to a non-English language, your Pathway content will still display in English. Dependents on your Calm benefit are not eligible to enroll in Pathways.

What are Pathways?

Pathways are tailored, 2-week programs designed by your organization using curated Calm content. They provide practical tools and techniques to help you build skills, tackle mental health challenges, and improve your overall well-being.

New guidance and content are typically sent every other business day.

How to Participate in a Pathway

Step 1: Check your inbox

Look for an email from your HR or benefits administrator inviting you to opt into the Pathway.

  • Didn’t receive the email? Please contact your HR department to confirm you were enrolled. Only users explicitly added by their administrator will receive a launch email.
Step 2: Log into Calm

  1. Download the Calm app from the Apple App Store or Google Play Store.

  2. Log in using the exact same email address and password you used to redeem your Calm Business benefit.

  3. If you haven’t redeemed your benefit yet: Reach out to your HR team for instructions, or view our Benefit Redemption Support Article.

Step 3: Access your content

Open the Calm app and navigate to the For Work section to view your curated Pathway content.

How to Manage Notifications or Opt Out

To receive mobile alerts when new Pathways content is added, or to opt out of communications entirely, follow these steps:

  1. Open the Calm app and tap Profile on the home screen.

  2. Tap the Gear icon (⚙️) in the top-left corner to open your Settings.

  3. Tap Notifications.

  4. Toggle Pathways on (to receive alerts) or off (to opt out).

Note: If you do not see “Pathways” as an option, please update your Calm app to the latest version.

Privacy & Support

Is my activity private?

Yes. Your individual usage data and the specific content you listen to within a Pathway are entirely confidential. Your organization will never see your personal activity.

Who do I contact for help?

  • For enrollment or launch email questions: Please contact your organization’s HR or benefits administrator.

  • For technical troubleshooting: Contact the Calm Support team directly via our Contact Support Page.

How to Redeem Your Calm Health Sponsorship

Calm Health is an invitation-only app provided through your employer, organization, or health plan. To get started, you will need the invitation email sent by your organization, which contains your invitation link and access code.

UHC or Optum member? See: UHC & Optum Members: How to Redeem Your Calm Health Sponsorship

 

Step 1: Find Your Invitation Email

Find the invitation email sent by your employer, organization, or health plan and click the unique link to begin the sponsorship redemption process.

If you did not receive an invitation email, but received an access code, you can begin your redemption online or by downloading the Calm Health app on your device:

  • iPhone
  • Android

Step 2: Create or Log In to Your Calm Account

Select one of the following options shown on your screen:

  • Log in with your Calm account: Choose this if you have an existing Calm account. Continue with the email or sign-in method previously used with Calm.
  • Create a Calm account: Choose this if you don’t have a Calm account. Continue with your preferred sign-up method.

 

Step 3: Set Up Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA)

You will be prompted to secure your account with MFA. Enter a valid phone number and select your preferred verification method: text message or phone call. To learn more about MFA for Calm Health, visit our MFA guide.

 

Step 4: Confirm Your Eligibility Details

Enter your First and Last name, then accept the Terms of Service and Privacy Policy. Tap Continue.

Next, enter your access code. If you used your invitation link, this may be pre-filled. Tap Continue.

When prompted, enter the eligibility details requested by your organization. These can be found in your invitation email. Tap Submit.

Not sure what your eligibility details are? Reach out to your HR or benefits administrator for assistance.

 

Step 5: Complete Your Registration

Once your eligibility is confirmed, you’ll see a success screen. You’ll then be asked to complete a short survey before being taken to your personalized Plan.

You can now access Calm Health. If you completed registration on a computer, scan the QR code shown to download the Calm Health app on your mobile device. Or use the links below:

  • iPhone
  • Android

Log in to Calm Health using the same sign-in method you selected in Step 2.
