Troubleshooting & FAQ

I followed the steps, but Premium content is still locked. What should I do?

This is almost always caused by being logged into a duplicate or incorrect account.

Step 1: Verify your account

  • On the Web: Click Log in in the upper right corner of the Calm Website.

  • In the App: Tap Profile on the home screen. If you see Sign up or Log in, tap it and select Log in at the bottom.

Step 2: Try a clean log out and restart 

If you do not see a login option, you are already signed into an account. Try clearing the app’s state:

  1. Tap Profile (bottom right) > Gear icon (top left).

  2. Scroll to the bottom and select Log out.

  3. Force-close the Calm app completely, then reopen it.

  4. Go back to Profile and log back in with the email address used during redemption.

Step 3: Check for updates 

Ensure your Calm mobile app is fully updated to the latest version via the App Store or Google Play Store.

I see an error message while trying to redeem.

If you hit an unexpected error while validating your account, please review our troubleshooting guide at How to Resolve Organization Redemption Errors. If you still need help, contact the Calm Support Team, click Continue on the pop-up, and provide a screenshot of the error along with your Calm account email.

What happens to my data if I leave the company?

If you leave Comcast NBCUniversal, your account will automatically transition to a free basic account. You will not lose your data. All of your session history, favorites, and usage stats will remain completely intact.

If you used a work email address, you can update it to a personal one at any time:

  1. Tap Profile > Gear icon.

  2. Tap Account info.

  3. Edit your email address and tap Update to save.

How to Redeem Your NYSUT Member Benefits Calm Premium Subscription

⚠️ CRITICAL WARNINGS BEFORE YOU BEGIN:

  • Do NOT delete your Calm account to troubleshoot redemption issues. This can result in permanent data loss and will not fix activation errors.

  • Existing Paid Subscriptions/Free Trials: If you currently have an active paid subscription or free trial, you cannot redeem your NYSUT benefit until your current plan is canceled and expires. Learn How to cancel auto-renewal or cancel a subscription and how to request a refund for a recent purchase.

How to Redeem via the Calm Mobile App

If you are a new user, first download the Calm app for iPhone/iPad or Android and create a free account using your personal email address.

  1. Open the Calm app and log in to your account.

  2. Tap Profile in the bottom right-hand corner of the screen.

  3. Tap the Settings (gear icon ⚙️) in the top left-hand corner.

  4. Select Link Organization Subscription.

  5. Type NYSUT Member Benefits when prompted for your organization’s name, then tap Continue.

  6. Enter your NYSUT ID# to validate your eligibility and tap Submit.

  7. Once you see the success page, tap the Home button. Your Premium content will now be unlocked.

How to Redeem via the Website

Note: For the best experience, we recommend using the Google Chrome web browser.

  1. Go to the NYSUT Calm Subscription Portal.

  2. Log in to your existing Calm account, or sign up for a free account.

  3. Enter your NYSUT ID# to validate your eligibility.

  4. Once confirmed, your subscription will unlock. You can now download the app via Apple App Store or Google Play Store and log in with these same credentials.

Troubleshooting & Frequently Asked Questions

The Premium content is still showing a locked symbol. What should I do?

First, verify that you are logged in using the exact same email address you used to redeem your subscription.

  • To verify on the website: Click Log in in the upper right corner.

  • To verify in the app: Tap Profile -> Sign up or Log in. If you see “Create your Calm account,” tap Log in at the bottom.

If you are logged into the wrong account, follow these steps to log out and reset:

  1. Tap Profile (bottom right) -> Settings (gear icon ⚙️) (top left).

  2. Scroll to the bottom and select Log out.

  3. Force-close and re-launch the Calm app.

  4. Go back to Profile and select Log in to enter your correct email.

  5. Ensure your app is updated to the latest version.

If content remains locked, contact our team at Calm Support Team and include a screenshot of the locked content along with your Calm account email.

I see an error message while trying to redeem.

If you experience an error during validation, please follow the troubleshooting steps outlined in our Redemption Error Guide.

How do I share my Calm benefit with my dependents?

NYSUT members can invite a select number of family members or friends to access Calm Premium for free.

  • Learn How to invite dependents to your Calm benefit.

  • Direct your dependents to How dependents redeem their Calm invitation for activation steps.

What happens if I leave NYSUT?

If you are no longer an active or retiree NYSUT member, you will retain your history on your free Calm account, but you will lose Premium access. Premium features will expire at the end of the calendar month in which you are removed from the NYSUT membership file (e.g., if removed on April 15th, access ends on April 30th).

How to Redeem Your Organization’s Free Calm Premium Subscription

Important Note before you begin: Redemption processes vary by organization. If you are unsure of your specific sign-up link, Access Code, or Group Code, please contact your company’s HR or benefits administrator first.

If your employer or organization provides Calm Premium as a wellness benefit, follow the steps below to unlock your free account.

Step 1: Set up your Calm account

Before linking your benefit, you need a basic Calm account.

  • Existing users: Open the Calm app or website and log in with your current email and password.

  • New users: Download the Calm app for iPhone/iPad or Android and create a free account using a personal email address.

Method 1: Redeem via the Calm Mobile App

  1. Open the Calm app and tap Profile in the bottom right corner of your screen.

  2. Tap the Gear Icon (⚙️) in the top left corner to open your Settings menu.

  3. Tap Link Organization Subscription.

  4. Enter your organization’s name when prompted, then tap Continue.

  5. Validate your eligibility:

    • If prompted, enter your work email, employee ID, Access Code (e.g., C-56K9L1), or Group Code (e.g., Calm2026), then tap Submit.

    • Note: If your company uses an internal employee portal, you will be automatically redirected to your company’s login page. Log in there to proceed.

  6. You will see a success page. Tap Home to access your unlocked Premium content.

    • Seeing an error? Please visit our Subscription Error Troubleshooting Guide for next steps.

Method 2: Redeem via a Web Browser

For the smoothest experience, we recommend using the Google Chrome browser on a desktop computer.

  1. Go to your company’s unique Calm sign-up link or internal employee benefits portal.

  2. Log in to your existing Calm account, or sign up for a new one if you haven’t already.

  3. Enter your unique identifier (work email, employee ID, or Access Code) if prompted to validate your eligibility.

  4. Once confirmed, your Premium subscription will immediately unlock.

  5. Open the Calm app on your phone and log in using the exact same account credentials you used in Step 2.

Troubleshooting: Error Messages When Redeeming a Calm Benefit

Important: Before troubleshooting, please ensure you are logged into your primary Calm account. If you accidentally redeem your benefit on a duplicate account, it cannot be easily transferred.

If you encounter an error while activating your Calm benefit, locate the specific message below for step-by-step instructions.

“Oops! Something went wrong”

This message can appear at different stages of the activation process:

  • After entering an Organization ID or Email: The information entered does not match your organization’s eligibility records. Check for typos and try again. If it still fails, contact your HR or benefits administrator to confirm your listed details.

  • After entering a Group Code: Your current code may be invalid or expired. Please reach out to your HR team to request a new, active Group Code.

  • After logging into an internal company portal: Your company login credentials may be incorrect. Verify your login details or contact your IT helpdesk to ensure your account is active.

“Premium already redeemed”

This means your benefit has already been successfully linked to a Calm account.

  • Log out of the app and log back in using the specific email address you used to redeem the benefit.

  • If you are logged into the correct account but content is still showing up as locked, please contact Calm Support Team for manual account provisioning.

“No more memberships can be claimed”

Your organization has filled all available subscription “seats” allocated for this specific Group Code.

  • Please notify your HR or benefits administrator. They will need to log into their partner dashboard to allocate more seats before you can complete your registration.

“Your Email/ID used is not on your organization’s eligibility file”

Calm does not have your specific unique ID or corporate email address on record.

  1. Double-check for any typos or spacing issues in the text field.

  2. Ensure you are using your official work email rather than a personal one.

  3. If the error persists, contact your HR department to verify that your unique ID has been uploaded into the Calm Partner Portal.

“Welcome to Calm! Single Sign On is required”

This error occurs when Calm detects that you are trying to access the benefit without passing through your company’s secure login network.

  • Make sure you are actively logged into your company’s internal employee portal or intranet before clicking the Calm redemption link.

  • Contact your internal HR team to ensure your Single Sign-On (SSO) profile is properly mapped for the Calm integration.

Still need help? If you are seeing an error code not listed here, or if these steps didn’t resolve your issue, please submit a request directly to our Calm Support Desk.

Troubleshooting: Premium content is locked after redeeming an organization benefit

Critical Note: Before troubleshooting, please ensure your organization’s benefit registration process was completed fully. If you used a specific work email or unique link to redeem the offer, you must use that exact same account information to log in to the Calm app.

If you have already successfully redeemed your subscription through your employer or organization but Premium content is still locked, you are likely logged into the wrong Calm account, or completely logged out.

Follow these sequential steps to verify your account and unlock your Premium content.

Step 1: Verify your login status or refresh the app

Sometimes the app needs a quick refresh to recognize your updated subscription status.

  1. Open the Calm app and tap Profile on your home screen.

  2. Tap the Settings gear icon in the upper left corner.

  3. If you see Sign up or Log in, tap it, and select Log in at the bottom of the page.

  4. If you are already logged in, scroll to the bottom, tap Log out, fully close/hard-quit the Calm app, reopen it, and log back in.

Step 2: Confirm you are using the correct email address

It is very common to accidentally create a duplicate Calm account using a personal email instead of the work email used to claim the benefit.

  1. Navigate back to your Profile and tap the Settings gear icon.

  2. Look at the email address listed at the top of the menu.

  3. Check if this matches the exact email address you used when redeeming your organization’s benefit.

  4. If it doesn’t match: Tap Log out at the bottom of the screen, and log back in using the correct email address.

Still unable to unlock Premium?

If you can’t remember which email address you used to redeem the benefit, try logging in with any alternative personal or professional email addresses you own.

If you are still stuck, our support team can find your active subscription for you. Please contact us via Calm Support Portal so we can look up your account details.

For Partner Portal Administrators

How to Manage, Downgrade, or Cancel Your Organization’s Calm Renewal

⚠️ Important Access Check 

This article only applies to administrators who have a Plan & Payment tab inside their Partner Portal account. If you do not see this tab, please contact your Customer Success Manager (CSM) directly to manage your upcoming renewal.

How to Check Your Renewal Date

To see when your current plan ends and when your next billing cycle begins:

  1. Log in to the Calm Partner Portal.

  2. Click on the Plan & Payment tab.

  3. Locate your upcoming renewal date in the Your plan section, listed right next to Next bill date.

How to Cancel Your Auto-Renewal

If you decide to cancel your plan’s auto-renewal, you must do so before your next bill date.

  1. Navigate to the Plan & Payment tab within the Calm Partner Portal.

  2. Scroll to your plan details and click the red Cancel plan button.

💡 Note: Once canceled, your organization and team members will continue to have full access to Calm Premium content until your current annual subscription period ends.

How to Confirm Your Cancellation Is Successful

If you aren’t sure if your cancellation went through, check your Plan & Payment tab.

  • If you see a Reactivate button, your subscription is successfully canceled and will not renew.

  • If you see the red Cancel plan button instead, your account is still set to auto-renew.

If your portal shows the Reactivate button but you were still unexpectedly charged, please contact the Calm Support Team. 

Downgrading Covered Lives (Seats) Before Renewal

The cost of your plan is determined by the total number of “covered lives” (member seats) you purchase. While larger seat counts yield higher discount rates, you can request a downgrade if your hiring plans have changed.

⚠️ Critical Warning: Simply removing users or updating the list on your Eligibility tab does not automatically change your billing tier or lower your seat count for renewal.

Steps to Safely Reduce Your Seat Count:

  1. Update your roster: Ensure your user eligibility file is fully updated on your Eligibility tab to reflect your preferred lower seat count.

  2. Contact Support early: Reach out to the Calm Support Team within two weeks of your upcoming renewal date to explicitly request a plan downgrade.

Tracking User Adoption Safely

To protect user privacy, Calm cannot provide a list of specific individuals who have redeemed their benefits. However, you can check your overall team adoption rates:

  • Go to the Reporting tab.

  • Look for the Signups All Time metric to see the percentage of your eligible population using the app.

  • Note: This data will remain hidden until at least 10 people in your group have redeemed their Calm benefit.

Billing, Pricing, and Downloading Receipts

  • Billing Frequency: Calm for organizations operates strictly on annual subscription plans. We do not offer monthly billing options.

  • Payment Terms: The credit or debit card provided at checkout is billed as a single, upfront payment for the full year at the time of purchase.

How to Download a Receipt or Invoice:

  1. Log in to your Calm Partner Portal Admin Account.

  2. Click on the Plan & Payment tab.

  3. Scroll down to the Billing history section.

  4. Locate the Invoice column and click on the Cloud Icon next to the charge to download your PDF invoice.

If the cloud icon is missing next to a finalized charge, please contact the Calm Support Team.

Need Extra Help? Contact Support

While Calm does not offer phone-based customer service, our email support team can assist you with any billing or renewal questions.

When you message the Calm Support Team, please include the following details so we can find your account instantly:

  • Your Partner Name (Found under Account tab > Account Details)

  • Your Partner subscribe link (Found under Account tab > Account Details)

How to Migrate from a Legacy Team Plan to Calm for Organizations

Important Product Availability Notice 

This article applies only to admins managing a group of 5 to 100 people who purchased a Calm Team Plan before July 16, 2024. If you purchased Calm for Organizations on or after July 16, 2024, please see our Calm for Organizations FAQ.

With the launch of our new self-serve experience, the legacy Team Plan subscription has been discontinued.

Rest assured that all of your existing benefits will remain seamlessly intact. We have updated our platform to include exciting new features in your Partner Portal to better support your team’s mental well-being—at no additional cost to you. Your current pricing will remain exactly the same.

Exploring Your New Features

To get familiar with your updated dashboard, you can take a quick, guided walk-through by visiting the Interactive Calm Partner Portal Tour.

How to Upgrade Your Covered Lives

If you need to add more spots for your team immediately, you can self-serve directly from your dashboard:

  1. Log in to your account and open the Partner Portal.

  2. Click on the Plan & Payment tab.

  3. Select the Upgrade coverage button and follow the prompts.

How to Downgrade Your Covered Lives for Renewal

⚠️ Critical Billing Step: Simply removing users or updating your list under the Eligibility tab will not automatically lower your bill or reduce your paid slots upon renewal.

To officially decrease your total covered lives for your next billing cycle, you must complete the following two steps:

  1. Navigate to the Eligibility tab in your Partner Portal and update your employee list to reflect your new, preferred number of seats.

  2. Contact the Calm Support Team at calmbusinesshelp@calm.com to request the downgrade. You must send this email within two weeks of your upcoming renewal date.

How to Purchase and Manage Calm for Organizations (Groups of 5–300)

⚠️ CRITICAL SUBSCRIPTION & BILLING POLICIES

  • Payment Terms: Subscriptions require a single, upfront annual payment in USD only. Monthly billing, billing by usage, invoicing, purchase orders, and payment by check are not available.

  • Seat Limitations: You can increase your number of covered lives or transfer seat ownership at any time, but you cannot decrease your seat count or receive a refund mid-cycle.

  • Automatic Renewal: Your plan is set to auto-renew annually. You can disable auto-renewal at any time before your cycle ends.

If you have already purchased a plan and need help navigating your dashboard, please visit the Partner Portal Support Center.

For organizations with over 300 people, please submit a request via our Large Enterprise Contact Form to receive custom high-volume discounts from our sales team.

Program Overview & Pricing

How is Calm for Organizations different from a regular subscription?

It allows companies to purchase Calm Premium for groups of 5 to 300 people at a discounted rate. Every member assigned to a seat receives full, unrestricted access to the Calm app, identical to an individual Calm Premium subscription.

How much does it cost?

Pricing is based on the total number of “covered lives” (seats) you purchase—the larger the group, the deeper the discount. To view specific tier pricing for your team size, use the interactive slider on our Pricing Calculator & Scrub Bar.

Can we try it before buying?

We do not offer trial access to the administrative dashboard prior to purchase, but you can view a preview via our interactive Partner Portal Product Tour. To explore our content library as an individual, you can sign up for a Calm Premium 14-Day Free Trial.

Subscription Activation & Timelines

When does our subscription and team access begin?

  • The Plan: Activates immediately at checkout. Your annual renewal date is set exactly one year from the date of purchase.

  • The Members: Access begins as soon as you upload your team’s eligibility list into the portal.

  • Seat Redemptions: Members can claim their seat at any point while the subscription is active. Late redemptions do not shift or extend your plan’s overall annual renewal date.

Account Setup & Admin Management

Who manages the organization’s account?

The individual who completes the purchase is designated as the primary Administrator and will be prompted to create an Admin account. Once setup is complete, you can add additional team members as Administrators inside the dashboard.

Should Admins be included in our total seat count?

Only include Administrators in your total “covered lives” headcount if they intend to use the Calm app for personal use. If an Admin only intends to manage the portal, they do not need a paid seat.

Managing Member Access & Seats

Can I change who has access mid-cycle?

Yes. You can manage your employee roster at any time within the portal. Members removed from your eligible pool will lose app access at the end of the current calendar month, and new members can activate their accounts instantly.

Option A: Bulk Update (Add or Remove Multiple Members)

  1. Log in to your account and open the dashboard.

  2. Upload a revised CSV eligibility file. Note: If your new list exceeds your paid seat capacity, you will be prompted to upgrade your plan.

Option B: Individual Removal

  1. Go to the Eligibility tab.

  2. Select the Users menu.

  3. Click the red “-” (Minus) button next to the specific team member you wish to remove.

Can I add more seats later in the year?

Yes. Administrators can purchase additional covered lives at a prorated rate directly inside the portal at any time.

Features, Engagement Analytics & Availability

Can I see how my team is utilizing Calm?

Yes. Your dashboard provides aggregated, privacy-compliant analytics including:

  • Employee sign-up and activation rates

  • Overall engagement and utilization rates

  • Top content categories preferred by your team

To see how these insights are displayed, check out our Partner Portal Product Tour.

Does this plan include access for family dependents?

Yes. This plan supports Calm Premium access for dependents. For setup steps, please review our Dependent Access Activation Guide.

Where is Calm available?

  • Mobile App: Available globally in any country supported by the Apple App Store or Google Play Store.

  • Web Browser: Accessible from anywhere in the world with an active internet connection.

Still have questions before making a purchase?

Our team is here to help. Get in touch directly through the Calm Organization Support Desk.

How to Set Up and Use Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA) for the Calm Partner Portal

⚠️ Important: You cannot use your standard mobile device camera app to scan the login QR code. You must download and use a dedicated authenticator app (like Google Authenticator or Okta Verify) to complete this setup and avoid being locked out of your account.

Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA) is required for all Calm Partner Portal Admins. To log in, you will need to verify your identity using a third-party authenticator app.

First-Time Setup: How to Scan the QR Code

When you launch the Partner Portal and enter your email and password for the first time, a Secure Your Account screen will appear displaying a QR code.

Choose one of the supported authenticator apps below to complete your setup:

Option A: Google Authenticator Setup

  1. Download the Google Authenticator app from your device’s app store: Download for Android or Download for iOS.

  2. Open Google Authenticator and tap the + (plus) icon in the bottom right corner of your screen.

  3. Select Scan a QR code and scan the QR code displayed on your Calm Partner Portal screen.

  4. Your app will generate a temporary 6-digit code. Enter this code into the portal screen and click Continue.

  5. (Optional) Check the box for Trust this device to log in faster next time.

Option B: Okta Verify Setup

  1. Download the Okta Verify app from your device’s app store: Download for Android or Download for iOS.

  2. Open Okta Verify and tap the + (plus) icon at the top of your screen.

  3. Select Organization, then tap Skip when prompted to “Add Account from Another Device”.

  4. When asked “Do You Have Your QR Code?”, select Yes, Ready to Scan, and scan the QR code on your Calm Partner Portal screen.

  5. Your app will generate a temporary 6-digit code. Enter this code into the portal screen and click Continue.

  6. (Optional) Check the box for Trust this device to log in faster next time.

Using a different MFA app? If your organization uses another authentication tool and you need assistance, please Contact Calm Support. Tap Submit a Request in the bottom right-hand corner, and include your Partner Name and the name of the app you are using.

Returning Users: Logging In with MFA

Every time you log into the Calm Partner Portal moving forward, you will be prompted to enter a new 6-digit verification code.

  1. Open your chosen authenticator app on your mobile device.

  2. Search or look for the entry labeled calm-partner-portal.

  3. Copy the current 6-digit code shown in the app.

  4. Enter this code on your Calm Partner Portal login screen to complete your login.

Troubleshooting: MFA Code Errors

If the 6-digit code you entered is rejected, please verify the following:

  • Expiration: Did the code refresh before you finished typing it? Ensure you enter the code while it is still active in your app.

  • Account Match: Verify you are looking at the specific code labeled calm-partner-portal (and not an MFA code for a different account).

  • Device Clock: Ensure your mobile device’s system time is set to update automatically and matches your local time zone exactly. Code generation relies heavily on precise time syncs.

App-Specific Troubleshooting Guides

  • For Google Authenticator issues, view the Google Authenticator External Help Guide.

  • For Okta Verify issues, view the Okta Verify External Help Guide.

Still Need Help?

If you’ve checked the steps above and still cannot log in, please reach out to our team at Calm Support Portal. Tap Submit a Request in the bottom right corner.

Please include the following details so we can assist you quickly:

  • Partner Name

  • The specific authenticator app you are using

  • A description or screenshot of the error message

How to Use and Manage the Calm Partner Portal (Admin Guide)

⚠️ CRITICAL WARNING: When uploading a new eligibility file, any team members omitted from the new file will lose access to Calm Premium at the end of the current month. Additionally, if you purchase extra seats (covered lives), this action is final and cannot be decreased or refunded once the purchase is complete.

If you are interested in purchasing Calm for your organization but have questions before getting started, please see our Pre-Purchase Organizational FAQ.

If you have already purchased a subscription, use the guide below to manage your group via the Calm Partner Portal.

Setting Up Your Account & Access

First-Time Admin Setup

  1. Look for the welcome email sent to the person who purchased the subscription. It contains your unique login link.

  2. If you do not see it, check your spam or junk folder.

  3. Log in to your account, click on the Home tab, and complete the steps under the Get Set Up section.

Troubleshooting Admin Login

  • Password Reset: On the login screen, click Forgot Password. The password reset link sent to your email will expire after 60 minutes.

  • Still Locked Out?: If you still cannot access your account, please reach out to our team via the Calm Support Contact Form.

Adding Multiple Admins

Yes, your group can have more than one Administrator.

  1. Navigate to the Account tab.

  2. Look under Account Details to invite additional team members. Note: If your portal includes a Plan & Payment tab, all invited Administrators will have the authority to manage the subscription and purchase additional seats.

Managing Team Members & Eligibility

How Team Members Activate Calm

Once your setup on the Home tab is complete, your members can activate their access.

  1. Share your organization’s unique Calm URL with your eligible team members.

  2. Members must visit this URL and enter the exact email address that you provided in your eligibility file.

  3. They will be prompted to log in or create a free Calm account. Calm Premium access will apply automatically.

If an employee runs into issues, please share this Employee Activation Troubleshooting Guide with them.

Modifying the Team Mid-Year

You can adjust who has access to your Calm benefit at any time using two methods:

  • Option A: Bulk Updates (Add or Remove Many Members) Upload a new Excel/CSV file on the Eligibility tab. New members can register immediately. Omitted members will lose access at the end of the current month. If the file exceeds your purchased seat count, you will be prompted to buy more seats.

  • Option B: Manual Removal (Remove One Member at a Time) Go to the Eligibility tab and click Users. Locate the specific employee and click the Red minus (-) button next to their name.

Seats, Billing, & Renewals

Adding Additional Seats Mid-Year

All Admins can buy additional seats (covered lives) at a prorated rate mid-year.

  1. Navigate to the Plan & Payment tab.

  2. Select the option to add seats and complete the checkout process.

Note: If you do not see a Plan & Payment tab, please contact your Customer Success Manager directly to process your upgrade.

Managing Auto-Renewal & Cancellation

  1. Go to the Plan & Payment tab.

  2. Click Cancel Subscription to turn off auto-renewal.

  3. After cancelling, your team will retain Calm Premium access until the end of your current paid contract period.

Note: If you do not see a Plan & Payment tab, please contact your Customer Success Manager directly to request a cancellation or change your upcoming renewal. For more detailed renewal information, view our guide: How to Manage Your Calm Renewal.

Usage Reporting & Privacy

You can monitor organizational engagement via the Reporting tab. This dashboard displays aggregated data, including:

  • Sign-up and activation rates

  • Overall engagement metrics

  • Top content categories chosen by your team

🔒 Privacy Note: To protect user data privacy in alignment with our Privacy Policy, reporting metrics will only populate in your dashboard once 10 or more team members have successfully redeemed their benefit. Individual user activity is never shared.

For UHC/Optum Members

UHC/Optum Members: Can My Dependents Access Calm Health?

Calm Health may be available to dependents on your UHC or Optum health plan as part of your benefits. We recommend reaching out to your provider first to confirm whether dependent access is included in your coverage.

Eligible dependents who are 13 years of age or older can redeem through the myUHC portal or Optum Live and Work Well using their own member details. To get started, see: UHC & Optum Members: How to Redeem Your Calm Health Sponsorship. 

UHC/Optum Members: How to Redeem Your Calm Health Sponsorship

Calm Health is available to eligible UHC and Optum members as part of your health plan benefits. You can access Calm Health through your member portal or via a direct link provided in your invitation email, depending on your plan.

 

Step 1: Access Calm HealthIf you received an invitation email: Click the link in your invitation email to go directly to the Calm Health registration page. Skip to Step 2.If you did not receive a direct link: Log in to your UHC/Optum member portal via web browser or your health insurance app and look for the Calm Health benefit tile.

  • Optum members: Optum Live and Work Well
  • UHC members: myUHC

Once you find the Calm Health tile, click it to begin the sponsorship redemption process. If you are completing registration on a mobile device, you may be prompted to download the Calm Health app before continuing. If so, download the app and continue your registration there.

Don’t see the Calm Health tile? You may not be eligible for this benefit. Call the number on the back of your insurance card to confirm your eligibility.

 

Step 2: Create or Log In to Your Calm Account

Select one of the following options shown on your screen:

  • Log in with your Calm account: Choose this if you have an existing Calm account. Continue with the email or sign-in method previously used with Calm.

  • Create a Calm account: Choose this if you don’t have a Calm account. Continue with your preferred sign-up method.

     

Step 3: Set Up Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA)

You will be prompted to secure your account with MFA. Enter a valid phone number and select your preferred verification method: text message or phone call. To learn more about MFA for Calm Health, visit our MFA guide.

 

Step 4: Confirm Your Eligibility Details

When prompted, enter your First and Last name, then accept the Terms of Service and Privacy Policy. Tap Continue.

You may be asked to confirm your eligibility details on a separate screen. If prompted, re-enter your First name, Last name, Date of birth, and Member ID as it appears on your insurance member card. Tap Continue.

Note: An access code is not required for UHC or Optum members. If you are asked for one, make sure you have completed Step 1 through your member portal or invitation email link before proceeding. 

Step 5: Complete Your Registration

Once your eligibility is confirmed, you’ll see a success screen. You’ll then be asked to complete a short survey before being taken to your personalized Plan.

You can now access Calm Health. If you completed registration on a computer, scan the QR code shown to download the Calm Health app on your mobile device. Or use the links below:

  • iPhone
  • Android

Log in to Calm Health using the same sign-in method you selected in Step 2.

 

 

Troubleshooting

If you receive an error message during the redemption process, please see: Calm Health Redemption Errors: What They Mean and How to Resolve Them

Gift cards

Buy a Calm Gift Card: 1-Year & Lifetime Subscription Gifts

Important: App Compatibility

Troubleshooting Calm Gift Card Redemption Errors

Redemption Page

  • Where to Redeem: You must redeem your code at calm.com/redeem. You cannot redeem codes inside the mobile app.

  • App Compatibility: These codes work for the Calm App only.

Error 1: “You already have an active subscription”

  • The Problem: You cannot apply a gift card on top of an active, auto-renewing subscription (Apple, Google, or Credit Card).

  • The Fix:

    1. Wait: You must wait for your current subscription to expire.

    2. Cancel Auto-Renew: Ensure you have canceled your current subscription so it doesn’t renew. See How to Cancel Your Subscription or Free Trial.

    3. Redeem Later: Once your account switches to “Free” status, the code will work. Note: Gift codes do not expire, so it is safe to wait

Error 2: “The code you entered is not valid”

  • The Problem: Usually a typo.

  • Check these Common Mistakes:

    • Zero vs. Letter O: Try swapping them.

    • One vs. Letter I: Try swapping them.

    • Spaces: Ensure there are no spaces before or after the code.

    • Case Sensitivity: Enter all letters in CAPS.

Error 3: “There was an error redeeming this gift”

  • The Problem: This vague error usually means you have a “Partner Subscription” (e.g., free access via Kaiser, Amex, or an employer benefit).

  • The Fix:

    • If you have an employer benefit: You cannot use the gift card on this account. You must create a new, separate free account to use the gift card.

    • If you have no active plans: Please Contact Support immediately with your code.

How Often Should I Meditate? (Recommended Frequency)

Because this is your personal practice, the exact frequency is entirely up to you. However, to notice the most benefits, we recommend meditating daily.

Consistency vs. Duration

Research shows that some of the greatest benefits of meditation come from the consistency of the practice, rather than the length of the session.

  • It is more beneficial to meditate every day for a short period of time than it is to meditate once a week for a longer period.

  • With daily consistency, your mindfulness skills grow stronger, and integrating mindfulness into your daily life becomes much more intuitive. That is when the real transformation begins!

How Long Should I Meditate?

However long you can meditate is the right length of time! Even if you don’t feel very different at first, you are creating new neural pathways and changing your brain.

  • Start Small: If you can spare just one minute, once per day, start there!

  • Build Up Over Time: From there, you might try 3 minutes, then 5, 10, or 15. With practice, what used to feel like a long session will begin to feel comfortable and natural.

Set Up Mindfulness Reminders

If you need help remembering to practice each day, the Calm app can help! We highly recommend setting up a daily alert using our Mindfulness Reminders feature. To learn how to set this up, please see our guide here: How to Set Mindfulness & Bedtime Reminders on the Calm App

Ready to Begin?

Making a new habit stick takes time, but guided meditations can provide a reassuring rhythm and keep you on track.

  • Try “7 Days of Calm”: Begin your daily practice right now with our 7 Days of Calm program. Each session is about 10 minutes and is designed to easily make mindfulness a daily habit.

  • Read Our Complete Guide: Want to learn more about the basics of posture, breathing, and clearing your mind? Check out our complete guide on the Calm Blog: How to Meditate in 5 Steps.

How to Breathe During Meditation (Breathing Techniques)

In mindfulness meditation, the primary goal is to bring awareness to the present moment by focusing on your body’s natural breathing pattern.

Whether you are trying a specific technique or just breathing naturally, the goal is always the same: to center yourself in the present moment with relaxed, focused attention.

Should I Breathe Through My Nose or Mouth?

  • Nose Breathing: If possible, try to breathe in and out through your nose. Breathing through your nose slows the flow of the breath, which allows for a longer window of time for maximum oxygen absorption.

  • Mouth Breathing: If breathing through your nose is difficult or uncomfortable due to allergies, congestion, or other health issues, it is perfectly fine to breathe in and out through your mouth! Doing so will not negatively impact your meditation experience.

Tips for Maintaining Focus on Your Breath

Sometimes it can be difficult to notice the subtle sensations of breathing. During guided sessions, you may be asked to try different exercises. Think of these techniques as “training wheels” when learning to ride a bike—they are simply tools to help you maintain focus!

If your mind is wandering, try experimenting with these techniques to see what works best for you:

  • The Hand Placement: Place one hand on your belly to physically feel the rise and fall of your abdomen with each breath.

  • Counting: Count each breath to give your mind a specific task to focus on.

  • Noticing the Pause: Pay close attention to the brief, natural pause that happens between your inhale and your exhale.

  • Deepening the Breath: Take intentionally slow, deep breaths to make the sensation of breathing more obvious.

Try the Breathe Bubble

If you find it helpful to use a visual guide to sync your breathing, try our free Breathe Bubble tool! It provides a calming, interactive visual rhythm to help you comfortably pace your inhales and exhales.

Further Reading

  • The Science of Breathing: Want to dive deeper into the science and practice of breathing? Check out our complete guide here: How to breathe properly and why it matters

  • Racing Thoughts: Is your mind wandering away from the breath? That is completely normal! Learn how to gently handle this in our guide: Wandering Mind and Racing Thoughts

How to Meditate When Focusing on the Breath is Difficult

For some people, especially those with asthma or other breathing difficulties, mindfulness meditation can feel challenging because of the heavy focus on the breath. If your physical body puts limits on your breathing, you can still meditate effectively! There are many ways to calm the mind and relax the body.

How Should I Breathe During Meditation?

Our Head of Mindfulness, Tamara Levitt, received a question from an asthmatic user about how to handle breathing exercises. Her advice applies to anyone struggling to focus on their breath:

“How do you breathe in daily life? Breathe just like that. Don’t worry about taking deep breaths. This practice involves learning and honoring our limitations. Breathe gently and lovingly and do the best you can.”

Are Deep Breaths Required?

No. It is important to remember that specific physical techniques—like counting deep breaths or sitting perfectly upright—are just supports for the practice. Think of them like using a ruler to help you draw a straight line. They can be helpful tools, but they are not requirements.

Alternative Meditations to Try

There is no one right way to meditate. In the beginning, it is important to explore, experiment, and find a technique that works for you.

If breathwork is frustrating, try shifting your focus. We recommend trying the following meditation styles. While they may still briefly reference the breath, they focus primarily on other areas of the mind and body which you may find easier or more interesting:

  • Body Scan: Tap here to listen 

  • Loving-Kindness: Tap here to listen 

  • Non-Judgment: Tap here to listen 

Where to find these alternative meditations manually: You can find all of the meditation styles listed above by opening the Calm app, tapping the Discover tab, selecting the Meditation button, and browsing the Beginners category.

Pro Tip: Try Mindful Movement!

If sitting still and focusing inward on your breathing feels uncomfortable, transferring your focus to physical movement is a fantastic alternative:

  • Walking Meditations: Practice mindfulness by focusing on the physical sensation of your footsteps instead of your lungs. Search “Walking” in the app or tap here.

  • Calm Body: Follow along with short, 10-minute gentle stretching and mindful movement video exercises designed to release tension. Tap here to explore.

How to Meditate When Sitting is Uncomfortable

While our mindfulness instructors generally recommend sitting with a straight back to help facilitate focus, this position is not comfortable or physically possible for everyone.

If sitting upright is painful or uncomfortable, it can make it very difficult to rest your mind and concentrate. Finding a position that works for your body is a normal part of the process of learning to meditate! The postural instructions given during audio sessions are only suggestions; you should always listen to your body first.

Alternative Meditation Positions

The most important thing is that you find a position where you can rest comfortably for the entire length of your meditation session. If sitting straight on a cushion or the floor does not work for you, try these alternatives:

  • Use Back Support: Try sitting in a comfortable chair, on a couch, or resting your back against a wall.

  • Lie Down: You can meditate while lying flat on your back in bed or on a comfortable mat (just be careful not to fall asleep!).

Try Mindful Movement (Walking & Stretching)

Meditation is about cultivating a relaxed, focused, and accepting mind. For some people, it is much easier to achieve this state while in motion! If sitting still is not working for you, try these active alternatives:

  • Mindful Walking: You may also want to try our “Mindful Walking” sessions to practice mindfulness while taking a gentle walk. You can find these by searching “Walking” in the app, or by tapping right here.

  • Calm Body: If you prefer gentle stretching, try Calm Body. These short, 10-minute video exercises guide you through mindful movement to release physical tension. You can find these under the Discover > Movement menu in the app, or by tapping here.

Ultimately, there is no single “right” way to physically meditate. Do what works best for you!

The Benefits of Family Meditation

By practicing mindfulness together, you can equip your children for lifelong success by teaching them how to:

  • Develop self-awareness.

  • Respond to challenging emotions.

What are the Benefits of Meditation? (Mental & Physical Health)

There are countless benefits to a regular meditation practice. Whether you are looking to manage stress, improve your attention, or simply learn more about yourself, here are a few of our favorite benefits you can experience through mindfulness:

Mental & Emotional Benefits

Meditation teaches us to calm the mind and increase our self-awareness. Over time, you can expect to:

  • Learn how to relate to yourself and your circumstances in a more objective, compassionate, and skillful way.

  • Understand and work through your emotions rather than burying them.

  • Break the habit of emotional reactivity so you can engage in life more completely.

Cognitive Benefits (Focus & Attention)

Another common outcome of meditation is a sharper mind. A regular practice can help you:

  • Increase your overall ability to focus and concentrate.

  • Recondition your mind to break bad habits, sidestep impulses, and avoid distractions.

  • Sustain attention: Research shows that meditation can significantly increase your ability to sustain focused attention on a single task for an extended period of time. (Looking to boost your concentration right now? Try exploring our curated Focus Music).

Physical Benefits & Brain Changes

Meditation doesn’t just change your thoughts; it can physically change your brain! With regular practice, researchers often see:

  • A decrease in grey matter in the amygdala: This is the part of the brain that plays a big role in anxiety, stress, and compulsivity.

  • An increase in grey matter in the pre-frontal cortex: This is the area of the brain associated with awareness, concentration, and rational decision-making.

  • Ultimately, these physical shifts help you respond to difficult situations with a more level-headed approach.

Managing Specific Conditions

In addition to general wellness, meditation can also be a helpful tool for people coping with specific conditions, including:

  • ADD / ADHD

  • PTSD

  • Chronic pain

  • Sleep difficulties (If you struggle to wind down at night, try listening to one of our soothing Sleep Stories).

Further Reading

If you want to learn more about the science and wellness behind mindfulness, our Calm Blog is a great place to find support as you evolve your practice! Check out these specific articles:

  • 7 Benefits of Mindfulness

  • 6 Health Benefits of Meditation

What to Do If You Fall Asleep While Meditating

If you find yourself feeling sleepy or nodding off while meditating, do not worry—you are not alone! When we meditate, our bodies naturally enter a deep state of rest. Feeling sleepy is simply your body’s way of telling you that it is relaxed.

Because of this, sleepiness is a very common challenge for both beginners and seasoned meditators alike. If your goal is to stay awake and mindful, here are a few quick adjustments and resources to help you combat the urge to snooze:

Quick Tips to Stay Awake

Before you begin your next session, try making a few simple changes to your environment and posture:

  • Change your posture: Avoid meditating while lying down in bed. Instead, try sitting upright in a chair with your feet flat on the floor, or sit cross-legged on a cushion.

  • Adjust your environment: Meditate in a well-lit room or open a window to let in some natural light and fresh air.

  • Shift your timing: Try not to meditate right after a heavy meal or right before your normal bedtime when your body is already naturally winding down.

Try the “Sleepiness” Meditation on Calm

We have created a specific guided meditation designed to help you gently work through the feeling of fatigue.

  • You can open and listen to the Sleepiness meditation directly in your app.

  • Alternatively, you can find it by tapping the Discover tab, searching for the Staying on Track series, and selecting the Sleepiness session.

Read Expert Advice from Our Head of Mindfulness

Tamara Levitt, Calm’s Head of Mindfulness, has put together a comprehensive guide featuring 6 practical tips to help you fight off your body’s tendency toward sleep while trying to meditate.

  • You can read her full Q&A and advice on our blog here: Q&A with Tamara: How to Combat Sleepiness during Meditation

What to Do When Your Mind Wanders During Meditation (Racing Thoughts)

If you are experiencing distracting or racing thoughts during meditation, do not worry—this is completely natural! A wandering mind is something that affects everyone, even seasoned meditators.

It is important to remember that progress along the path of mindfulness is not always linear. One day your mind may feel deeply concentrated, and the very next day it may seem like your thoughts are all over the place.

Redefining the Goal: What is Mindfulness?

Many people believe that the goal of meditation is to completely clear the mind of all thoughts. In reality, mindfulness is not the absence of thoughts; it is remembering to come back when you get lost.

  • Every single time you realize that you are distracted, that realization itself is mindfulness!

  • Each time you gently and lovingly bring your attention back to your breath (or the sound of a bell, or another intentional object of focus), that is meditation.

How to Handle Distracting Thoughts

When your mind starts to race or wander off, practice these two simple steps:

  1. Acknowledge: Notice the thought that has arisen without judging yourself for having it.

  2. Recenter: Gently let the thought go and bring your focus back to your body’s natural breathing pattern. (If you find this difficult, try experimenting with the focus exercises in our How to Breathe During Meditation (Breathing Techniques) guide).

Recommended Sessions for a Wandering Mind

If you want to actively practice this skill, we highly recommend checking out these specific sessions in the Calm app:

  • Monkey Mind (located within theStaying on Trackseries): This meditation was created by Tamara Levitt specifically to address the common challenge of a restless, wandering mind.

  • Returning to Now: This guided meditation uses gentle bells to remind the listener to routinely come back to the breath.

Further Reading
  • For more advice on this topic, check out Tamara Levitt’s response to this common question on the Calm Blog: Q&A with Tamara: deepening concentration when faced with a wandering mind

Kaiser Permanente provides its eligible members with access to Calm Premium at no additional cost, including the full library of sleep, meditation, and relaxation content available in the Calm app and on the website. 

How it works

Kaiser Permanente members receive unlimited access to Calm Premium for as long as their coverage is active. Calm access must be redeemed through your Kaiser Permanente account. Only one redemption can be made per account.

Who is eligible

Most Kaiser Permanente members are eligible for this benefit. To check your eligibility, visit kp.org/selfcareapps and select Get Calm. You’ll be prompted to sign in to your Kaiser Permanente account to confirm your eligibility.

Don’t see the Calm benefit? This may mean Calm is not included in your current health plan. Please contact Kaiser Permanente directly for further guidance on your benefits.

Ready to get started?

See our step-by-step sign-up guide: How to Sign Up for Calm as a Kaiser Permanente Member.

How to Sign Up for Calm as a Kaiser Permanente Member

As a Kaiser Permanente member, you can sign up for Calm Premium using the following steps.

  1. Visit kp.org/selfcareapps and select Get Calm. If prompted, sign in to your Kaiser Permanente account.
  2. You will be redirected to Calm’s website and asked if you already have a Calm account.
    • If you do not have a Calm account, select Sign up for free and follow the prompts to create one.
    • If you already have a Calm account, select Log in and follow the prompts to log in.
  3. Your Calm Premium access will automatically activate. 
  4. Download the Calm app on your device, and log in using the same email address and password used in Step 2. If needed, refer to our login guide.

For the best experience, we recommend redeeming your Calm benefit using a web browser rather than through the Kaiser Permanente app or Calm app.

If you have any trouble with the redemption steps, please contact our support team here.

I Received an Error While Redeeming My Kaiser Permanente Calm Benefit

If you encountered an error message while trying to redeem your Calm benefit through Kaiser Permanente, try the following steps.

Step 1: Sign out and sign back in to your Kaiser Permanente account

  1. Sign out of your Kaiser Permanente account at kp.org
  2. Sign back in and return to kp.org/selfcareapps
  3. Select Get Calm and try redeeming again

Step 2: Try a different web browser

If the error persists, try opening kp.org/selfcareapps in a different web browser or in a private/incognito window.

Contact Calm Support

If you are still seeing an error, please contact our support team here and include a screenshot of the error message.

Redeemed My Kaiser Permanente Calm Benefit But Premium Content is Still Locked

If you successfully redeemed your Kaiser Permanente Calm benefit but your account still shows “locked” content, you are likely logged in to a different Calm account than the one used during redemption.

Why this happens

This issue usually occurs when:

  • You redeemed your Kaiser Permanente Calm benefit using one email address, but logged in to Calm with a different one
  • A second Calm account was created by mistake during the redemption process
  • You are not yet logged in to the Calm app after redeeming on the website

Step 1: Check which account you used to redeem

There are two ways to check:

Check via kp.org

  1. Visit kp.org/selfcareapps and select Get Calm
  2. Sign in to your Kaiser Permanente account when prompted
  3. You will be redirected to Calm’s website
  4. Select the menu icon in the top right corner, then Profile, then Account Details to view the email address associated with your Calm account.

Check your email inbox

Search your email inboxes for a confirmation email from Calm. The email address that received this confirmation is the one linked to your Kaiser Permanente benefit.

Step 2: Log in to the correct account

  • On the Calm website: select Log in in the upper right corner
  • On the Calm app: tap Profile, select the gear icon in the upper left, then tap Sign up or Log in and select Log in

Make sure you are using the same email address and password used during redemption. Once logged in to the correct account, your Premium content should unlock immediately. If needed, refer to our login guide. 

Contact Calm Support

If you’ve reviewed the steps above and still can’t access your Premium subscription, please contact Calm Support here and include:

  • The email(s) you may have used
  • Description of what you see in the app
  • Screenshot (if applicable)

Overview of Calm content

Calm Breathing Exercises: How to Adjust Speed, Timers & Haptics/Vibrations

Breathing exercises are powerful tools to help you relax, refocus, or energize. These exercises feature a “Breathe Bubble” visualization with audio that can be customized to fit your needs.

How to Access Breathing Exercises

  1. Tap the Discover menu at the bottom of the home screen.

  2. Select Mindful Tools.

  3. Under the Breathing Exercises heading, swipe through the options or tap See All.

  4. Choose from our six breathing styles:

    • Relax (Free)

    • Balance

    • Restore

    • Focus

    • Energize

    • Unwind

Customizing your Exercise

Once you’ve selected an exercise, you can adjust the experience before you begin:

  • Set a Timer: Tap the Clock icon to choose a duration ranging from 1 minute to “Endless.”

  • Adjust Speed: Tap the Settings/Controls icon to select your pace (4, 6, or 8 breaths per minute).

  • Volume: Within the Settings/Controls icon, you can independently adjust the volume for the “Breathe” guidance and the background “Scene” sounds.

How to Manage Haptics & Vibrations

The settings for physical feedback (haptics/vibrations) differ depending on your device:

Apple (iOS) Instructions

  1. Launch the Calm app and go to your Profile.

  2. Tap the Settings (gear icon) in the top-left corner.

  3. Locate the Haptic Feedback toggle at the top of the page.

  4. Tap to toggle it Off or On.

Android Instructions

  1. Launch Calm and start your Breathing Exercise.

  2. Tap the Settings/Controls icon located next to the play/pause button.

  3. Locate the Vibrations toggle.

  4. Tap to toggle it Off or On, then press Save Settings.

 

Additional information

  • Save for Later: Tap the Heart icon to favorite an exercise so you can find it quickly in your Profile.

  • Classic Version: A simplified version of the Breathe Bubble is available on our website at calm.com/breathe.

Calm for Teachers: Free Classroom Resources & Lesson Plans

Using Calm in the Classroom: Free Resources for Teachers

Status of the “Calm Schools Initiative”

  • Free Teacher Subscriptions: We are no longer accepting applications for free Calm Premium subscriptions for educators.

  • The New Program: Instead, we have created a suite of free, accessible resources (PDFs) that do not require a login or subscription. This ensures students and parents can access them at home without barriers.

Free Classroom Resources (No Login Required)

We offer downloadable guides that include direct links to free audio content.

  1. “30 Days of Mindfulness in the Classroom”

    • What it is: A structured guide with age-specific lesson plans for PreK-12 students.

    • What’s included: Daily mindfulness exercises and free access to select Calm Kids content (marked with asterisks in the guide).

    • Download the 30-Day Guide Here

  2. “Self-Care Guide for Teachers”

    • What it is: A resource specifically for you, the educator. Includes tips for managing classroom stress and preventing burnout.

    • Download the Teacher Guide Here

How to Use These Materials

  • In Class: Play the free audio tracks directly from the guide during morning meetings or quiet time.

  • At Home: Because these resources don’t require a password, you can email the PDF (or specific links) to parents for students to use at home.

Paid Options for Schools

  • Individual Teachers: If you wish to unlock the full Calm library (Daily Calm, full Sleep Stories, etc.), you will need a personal Calm Premium subscription.

  • Districts & Organizations: For school-wide access or bulk subscriptions for staff, please check out Calm for Business.

Calm Sleep Stories: The Ultimate GuideWhat are Sleep Stories? Sleep Stories are soothing tales that mix music, sound effects, and incredible voice acting to help you drift off. They are designed to distract your racing mind and gently guide you into deep sleep.

  • Length: Typically 20–40 minutes.

How to Find & Play Stories

    • Celebrated Voices (Harry Styles, Matthew McConaughey, etc.)

Features: Playlists & Offline Listening

    • You can now stack stories to play back-to-back!

    • Start a story > Open the Player > Tap the Playlist Icon > Create new playlist.

    • How to Create and Manage Custom Playlists

  • Download for Offline:

    • Tap the Cloud Icon on any story to save it to your phone for flights or camping.

  • Curated Playlists:

    • Scroll down in the Sleep tab to find “Curated Playlists” (e.g., Remix: Rain, Sleepy Voyages), which are pre-made collections lasting 3–4 hours.
Sleep Stories for Kids

We have a dedicated section for children featuring beloved characters:

  • Disney: Disney Princesses, Cars, etc.
