Daily Calm Calendar

Calendar Sync Feature Ended (2026)

As of early 2026, we have sunset the feature that automatically synced Daily Calm prompts to your personal Google/Outlook/iCal calendar.

  • Why? We are focusing on creating more interactive resources on our blog and within the app.
  • What now? You can still access the calendar! You just need to download the PDF version manually each month (instructions below).
  • How to remove old events: If you still see old Calm events cluttering your personal calendar, please see How to Remove Daily Calm Calendar Events from Personal Calendar

How to Download the Current Monthly Calendar 

The calendar is now hosted on the Calm Blog.

  1. Visit the Calm Blog.
  2. Search for “Calendar” or look for the “Daily Calm Calendar” post at the start of the month.
  3. Download: You can save the image to your phone or download the PDF to print it out.

What is the Daily Calm Calendar? 

It is a companion tool to our Daily Calm sessions. Each day offers a prompt to meet your life with presence and curiosity.

  • Use it for: Journaling, dinner table conversation starters, or a daily moment of reflection.
  • Format: Printable visual guide with short, mindfulness-based themes.

Daily Quotes

Looking for a collection of Daily Calm quotes? You can access some of our past Daily Calm quote images and other shareable content through our social media pages: www.facebook.com/calm www.instagram.com/calm www.twitter.com/calm www.pinterest.com/calm/daily-calm/

Getting Started With Calm: A Beginner’s Guide to Meditation & Sleep

Welcome to Calm, and congratulations on taking this step toward a healthier you! When you open your Calm app, the most important thing to bring is your curiosity, a non-judgmental mind, and an open heart 💙

Depending on your personal goals, we recommend exploring the following features to get the most out of your new app:

Goal 1: Learning to Meditate

If you have come to Calm to learn mindfulness meditation, we recommend starting with our structured beginner programs before exploring the broader library.

  • The Daily Calm: This is our most popular feature. Thousands of people around the world start their day with these 10-minute sessions. Each session explores a fresh topic or theme, making it a great way to develop a regular daily practice.

    • Where to find it: You can find our Daily content right on the Home page of the app, located in the very first carousel.

  • 7 Days of Calm: Start here! This is our introductory program that teaches you the basics of meditation.

  • 21 Days of Calm: Once you finish the 7 Days of Calm, move on to this program to continue building your foundation.

  • How to Meditate: For listeners looking for a different experience, guest instructor Jeff Warren has contributed an excellent 30-day introduction to meditation.

    • Where to find courses: To find specific programs like the 7 Days of Calm, tap the Discover tab at the bottom of your screen and type the title into the search bar at the top. You can also use the quick navigation menu in the center of the Discover page (with icons for Meditation, Music, Mindful Tools, Reflections, etc.) to browse by category.

Goal 2: Improving Your Sleep

If you have come to Calm to help you drift off to a restful sleep, we offer a dedicated library of soothing content.

  • Sleep Stories: We have a collection of over 100 bedtime stories for adults and kids. Themes range from classic literature to nature essays, children’s tales, and scientific articles. We update our Sleep Stories selection with new options every week.

  • Sleep Music: We also have a large collection of soothing Sleep Music designed to help you relax before bed.

    • Where to find Sleep content: Tap the Sleep tab at the bottom of your screen. From there, you can use the sub-tabs at the top of the page to filter by Sleep Stories, Meditations, Tools, Music, Soundscapes, Playlists, and Downloads.
Tips for a Distraction-Free Experience

To get the best possible experience during your sessions, we suggest cutting down on potential distractions:

  • Use a pair of headphones.

  • Switch your phone to Airplane mode or Do Not Disturb mode before pressing ‘play’ to prevent calls or text alerts from interrupting your peace.

Additional Resources You can find more meditation and mindfulness tips, as well as a complete guide on how to start meditating with Calm, on our blog here.

How to Find and Use Calm Music (Focus, Sleep & Relaxation)

Whether you need background noise to help you study, uplifting tracks to start your day, or soothing melodies to fall asleep, Calm Music has you covered. We offer hundreds of specially curated tracks of varying lengths designed to fit your exact mood.

How to Find the Music Library

  • In the Mobile App: Tap the Discover tab at the bottom of your home screen, then tap the Music button at the top of the menu.

  • On the Web: If you are using a computer, you can access our full music library directly at www.calm.com/music.

Browse by Category

Once you are in the Music section, you can swipe through our dedicated category tabs to find the perfect track for your day. Our categories include:

  • Focus and Flow: Rhythmic, distraction-free tracks perfect for working or studying.

  • Uplift: Bright, positive music to boost your mood.

  • Piano & Classical: Soothing strings and traditional piano compositions.

  • Ambient and Atmospheric: Gentle, continuous tones to help your mind wander.

  • Electronic: Modern, chill beats to help you zone in.

  • Soundscapes: Immersive nature sounds like rain, ocean waves, and forest ambience.

Exclusive Artist Collaborations

Did you know Calm frequently releases exclusive music from world-renowned artists? As you browse the library, keep an eye out for special relaxation remixes, instrumental sleep tracks, and exclusive releases from massive artists like Moby, San Holo, Keith Urban, and even soothing Disney piano covers!

Customize Your Listening Experience (Loop & Autoplay)

If you find a track you absolutely love, you can customize your music player settings to put that single track on repeat, or use the Autoplay feature to have the app automatically play similar tracks once your current song ends.

  • For step-by-step instructions on how to use Loop, Shuffle, and Autoplay, please click here: Calm Music Settings: How to Loop, Shuffle & Autoplay Songs

Pro Tip: Download Music for Offline Listening

Want to listen to focus music on an airplane or during your daily commute without using cellular data? You can download your favorite Calm Music tracks for offline use!

  • Just tap the Download icon (a cloud with a downward arrow) on any track or playlist page.

  • You can easily access all of your saved offline music by navigating to the Sleep tab (or Discover tab) and tapping Downloads from the menu at the top.

How to Use the Timed & Open-Ended Meditation Timers

If you prefer to practice in silence or without a guided voice, Calm has you covered! We offer unguided tools that allow you to set a specific duration for your practice or simply meditate for as long as you’d like.

Here is how to find and customize your timer:

How to Start an Unguided Meditation in the App

  1. Tap the Discover tab at the bottom of your home screen.

  2. Tap the Meditation button.

  3. Scroll to the right through the category menu at the top of the screen until you see Less Guidance, and tap it.

  4. Select either Timed Meditation (to choose a specific duration) or Open-Ended Meditation (to meditate freely without a strict time limit).

  5. If using the Timed Meditation, select the amount of time you would like to meditate for (ranging from a few minutes up to several hours) and the timer will begin.

Using Calm on your computer? You can access the meditation timer directly on our website here.

How to Turn Off the Ending Bell Chime

By default, a gentle bell will chime when your Timed Meditation has run out to let you know the session is over. If you would prefer a completely silent experience without the bell, you can easily disable it:

  1. Start your Timed Meditation.

  2. Tap the Settings icon on the audio player screen.

  3. Tap the Bell Chimes option to toggle the sound OFF.

What are the Calm Dailies? (Daily Meditations & Movement)

Calm Dailies are one of our most popular features! Every single day, we release short, original, 10-ish minute sessions designed to help you build a consistent mindfulness habit.

Whether you are a beginner or an advanced meditation student, these daily sessions focus on different aspects of mindfulness and introduce fresh concepts to help you navigate your day.

Meet the Daily Programs

We currently offer four unique Daily programs led by our world-class guides:

  • The Daily Calm: A classic, soothing daily meditation with Tamara Levitt. (Available in English, German, Spanish, French, Japanese, Korean, and Portuguese).

  • The Daily Jay: Wisdom, storytelling, and actionable insights with Jay Shetty. (English Only).

  • The Daily Trip: A dynamic, quirky, and highly accessible meditation with Jeff Warren. (English Only).

  • The Daily Move: Quick, fun, and accessible mindful movement routines with Mel Mah. (Select episodes available in German and French).

How to Find the Dailies

Finding today’s daily session is easy!

  1. Open the Calm app.

  2. From your home screen, you will see a quick navigation menu near the top.

  3. Simply swipe through the top menu icons to select Daily Calm, Daily Jay, Daily Trip, or Daily Move.

Can I Listen to Past Daily Sessions?

Because a brand new session is released every 24 hours, the session featured on the home screen will change each day. However, if you missed yesterday’s session or want to relisten to an old favorite, you absolutely can! For instructions on how to search for and listen to past daily sessions, please click here: How to Listen to Past Daily Sessions

Set a Daily Reminder

The hardest part of building a new habit is remembering to do it! You can set a custom push notification on your phone to remind you to take 10 minutes for your Daily session.

  • To set this up, go to Profile > Settings (gear icon) > Mindful Reminders and toggle it on.

Build Your Mindfulness Streak!

Listening to a Daily session every day is the perfect way to build your Calm Mindfulness Streak. Every consecutive day you complete a session, your streak grows. You can track your current streak, total mindful minutes, and listening history at any time by tapping the Profile tab.

Subscription Requirements

Please note that you will need an active Calm Premium subscription to access our Calm Dailies.

What Free Content is Available on the Calm App?

You do not need a Premium subscription to enjoy Calm! We offer a variety of free mindfulness tools, meditations, and Sleep Stories for our users.

How to Spot Free vs. Premium Content

As you browse the app, you may notice a small padlock icon next to certain meditations, stories, and music tracks. The padlock indicates that the content is Premium and requires a paid subscription to access. If a program does not have a padlock, it is free for you to listen to!

Free Content Included in the App

If you are using the free version of the Calm app, you have access to the following without needing to pay:

  • Timed Meditations: You can practice unguided, timed meditations. To find these, tap the Discover tab, select Meditation, and scroll to the ‘Less Guidance’ category.

  • Day 1 of All Programs: You can listen to the first session (‘Day 1’) of any of our multi-day meditation programs (such as 7 Days of Focus or 21 Days of Calm).

  • Breathing Exercises: You have access to one of our guided Breathing Exercises.

  • Sleep Stories: You can listen to our popular Sleep Story, Blue Gold, at any time.

  • Background Scenes: You have access to a large number of free background Scenes (nature sounds and ambient music) to play while you meditate or sleep. To switch your Scene, tap the Mountain icon in the top left corner of the Home screen to preview and download free options.

What happens to my free content after a free trial?

If you complete a 7-day free trial and decide not to continue into a paid subscription, don’t worry! You will not lose your free options. In fact, completing a free trial actually unlocks additional free content on your account, including the full 7 Days of Calm meditation series and a wider selection of Sleep Stories.

What is a Calm Masterclass? (Expert Mindfulness Lessons)

A Calm Masterclass is an exclusive series of life-changing lessons taught by world-renowned experts, authors, and doctors. Designed to help you apply mindfulness to your daily life, these multi-part audio courses dive deep into specific topics like the science of sleep, breaking bad habits, radical compassion, and finding happiness.

Learn from World-Renowned Experts

Our Masterclasses feature engaging curriculum from highly sought-after thought leaders, including:

  • Dr. Judson Brewer on breaking bad habits and unwinding anxiety.

  • Shawn Achor on the science of happiness and positive psychology.

  • Elizabeth Gilbert on creative living beyond fear.

  • …and many more!

How to Find Calm Masterclasses

  • In the Mobile App: Tap the Discover tab at the bottom of your home screen, then tap the Wisdom button at the top of the menu. Scroll through the options to explore our Masterclass library.

  • On the Web: If you are using a computer, you can access our Masterclasses directly in your web browser here: www.calm.com/masterclass.

Pro Tip: Download Lessons for Offline Listening

Masterclasses are perfect for listening to on an airplane, during your commute, or while out for a walk. To listen without using cellular data, you can easily download individual lessons!

  • Just tap the Download icon (a cloud with a downward arrow) next to any lesson. You can access your saved offline audio anytime by navigating to your Profile, tapping the Settings (gear icon), and selecting Downloads.

Subscription Requirements & Free Previews

Not sure if a specific Masterclass is right for you? Every Masterclass includes a free introductory video that anyone can watch to learn what the course is about!

However, please note that you will need an active Calm Premium subscription to unlock and listen to the full set of lessons for each program.

What is Calm Body? (Mindful Movement & Stretching)

Calm Body is a collection of 10-minute video sessions that guide you through mindful movement and gentle stretching exercises. Designed for all body types and fitness levels, these short sessions help release physical tension and recharge your mind.

What Sessions Are Available?

We currently offer eight dedicated Calm Body routines tailored to different parts of your day:

  • Morning: Morning Wake Up and Morning Stretch to gently start your day.

  • Mid-Day & Activity: Afternoon Reset, Mindful Warm Up, and Mindful Cool Down to center yourself during work or around a workout.

  • Evening & Recovery: Evening Wind Down, Evening Stretch, and Back Care to release tension before bed.

Looking for a Daily Routine? Try The Daily Move!

If you enjoy the mindful stretching of Calm Body and want to build a daily habit, be sure to check out The Daily Move! Hosted by Mel Mah, these quick, daily movement videos offer fun, accessible routines to boost your mobility and mood every single day.

How to Find Calm Body & Movement Content

  • In the Mobile App: To explore Calm Body, The Daily Move, and other physical wellness content, tap the Discover tab at the bottom of your home screen, then tap the Movement button at the top of the menu. You can also simply tap the Search icon and type “Calm Body.”

  • On the Web: If you are using a computer, you can access the Calm Body page directly in your web browser here.

Pro Tip: Watch on a Bigger Screen!

Prefer to follow your stretching routine without hunching over your phone? You can easily cast or screen share Calm Body videos to your TV if you have a compatible device! Just start the video on your mobile app and use your device’s built-in screen mirroring feature (such as Apple AirPlay or Google Chromecast) to send the video directly to your television.

Subscription Requirements

Please note that you will need an active Calm Premium subscription to access the full set of Calm Body videos. However, free users can check out our free introductory video directly on the Calm Body page to learn the basics before subscribing!

What is Calm Kids? (Meditations & Sleep Stories for Children)

The Calm Kids library offers a wonderful collection of original Sleep Stories, guided meditations, and soothing lullabies perfectly tailored for kids, teens, and “kids at heart.”

Whether you are trying to help your little one wind down for bedtime or looking for a short meditation to help them process big emotions, the Calm Kids section has something for every family.

What is Included in Calm Kids?

Our children’s library is constantly growing, with new Kids Sleep Stories released 2 to 3 times per month! The collection features beloved characters and world-class narrators, including:

  • Favorite Characters: Listen to stories featuring Peppa Pig, Kung Fu Panda, the Minions, Thomas & Friends, and Trolls.

  • Celebrity Narrators: Enjoy tales brought to life by amazing voices like Maya Rudolph, Wanda Sykes, Randall Park, Diane Keaton, and many more.

How to Find the Calm Kids Library

All of our children’s content is housed directly within the main Calm app, so you do not need to download a separate app! To find it:

  1. Open the Calm app and tap the Discover tab at the bottom of your home screen.

  2. Tap the Calm Kids button.

  3. From here, you can browse through dedicated categories like Sleep, Meditations, and Lullabies designed specifically for younger listeners.

Using Calm on your computer? You can access the Calm Kids library directly on our website here.

Pro Tip: Download Stories for Car Rides & Bedtime

If your child listens to Calm on a Wi-Fi-only tablet, or if you want to play a Sleep Story during a long road trip without using your cellular data, you can download Calm Kids content for offline listening!

  • Just tap the Download icon (a cloud with a downward arrow) on any story or meditation page. You can access your saved stories anytime by navigating to the Sleep tab and tapping Downloads at the top of the screen.

Share Calm with the Whole Family!

Looking to share the gift of mindfulness with your household? The Calm Premium Family Plan covers up to 6 people under one discounted yearly price, giving everyone their own private account to access Calm Kids and our full adult library. Learn more about purchasing a Family Plan here.

What is The Spark? (Bite-Sized Interviews & Wisdom)

The Spark is a collection of bite-sized conversations meant to engage your mind and inspire fresh perspectives.

Hosted by Steve Goldbloom, these quick audio interviews feature fascinating guests—ranging from world-renowned athletes and entrepreneurs to thought leaders. Whether you are on your morning commute or taking a quick afternoon break, The Spark offers a perfect dose of motivation, humor, comfort, and wisdom.

How to Find The Spark

At this time, The Spark is available exclusively on the Calm mobile app (it is not currently available on the Calm website). To listen:

  • Quick Access: You can open The Spark directly in your Calm app by tapping right here.

  • Manual Search: Open the Calm app and tap the Discover tab at the bottom of your home screen. Tap the Wisdom button at the top of the menu, then scroll through the options to find The Spark.

Pro Tip: Download Episodes for Your Commute

Want to listen to these podcast-style interviews while commuting or flying without using your cellular data? You can download episodes of The Spark for offline listening!

  • Just tap the Download icon (a cloud with a downward arrow) on the episode page. You can easily access all of your saved offline audio by navigating to your Profile, tapping the Settings (gear icon), and selecting Downloads (or checking the Downloads section under the Discover tab).

Troubleshooting: Don’t see it?

If you navigate to the Wisdom menu and do not see The Spark listed, your app may be out of date. Please visit the App Store or Google Play Store to ensure you have updated Calm to the latest version. For instructions on how to update your app, please click here:How to Update the Calm App (iOS & Android)

Privacy

2023 Calm Terms of Service and Privacy Policy Updates

Calm released worldwide updates to its Terms of Service and Privacy Policy in 2023, which we encourage you to review in full. These updates are designed to provide more details and make them easier to read. Your continued use of our Services confirms your agreement to these updated terms.

Deleting or Accessing your Personal Information

As provided in Calm’s privacy policies, users have the right to request access to and deletion of their personal information. Follow the instructions below to delete or access your personal information, depending on the Calm product(s) you use.

Please note: Account deletions are permanent and cannot be reversed. When an account is deleted, the complete session history and all saved sessions are deleted.

Calm and Calm Sleep App

Accessing your Personal Information To access your personal information in a portable format, please email us at support@calm.com or submit a request on our website at calm.com/contact (click on the box that says “Ask a question” and type your request into the pop-up window).

Deleting your Personal Information You have the option to delete your account yourself through the “Account Settings” page in the Calm app. Simply follow these steps to delete your account:

  1. Open the Calm or Calm Sleep app and tap the Profile button on the bottom right of the screen
  2. Tap the Settings gear icon on the top left of the screen
  3. Tap “Account Settings”
  4. Tap “Delete Account” and follow the prompts to complete the delete process

If you no longer have access to your account, please submit a “delete account” request here and include the email address of the account you would like to have deleted from our system.

Please note:

  • Deleting your personal information in either the Calm or Calm Sleep account will delete your personal information across BOTH applications. 
  • Refunds cannot be issued by Calm after an account has been deleted and deleting an account yourself will not automatically generate a refund. If you would like to request a refund, please contact us and do not delete your Calm account.
  • Deleting your account will not resolve login, premium access, or other technical issues. Please contact us if you are having trouble with your Calm App.
  • You must cancel an auto-renewing subscription before deleting your Calm account.

Calm Health App

Accessing your Personal Information

To access your personal information in a portable format, please email us at support@calmhealth.com.

Deleting your Personal Information

Please see the instructions here. Questions may be directed to support@calmhealth.com.

Organization Administrators

Please direct all questions to calmbusinesshelp@calm.com.

Deleting Your Calm Health Account

Deleting your Calm Health account is irreversible. Once deleted, you will no longer have access to your Calm Health data or associated records. 

Have an expired sponsorship? Before deleting your account, you may be able to convert to an unsponsored experience instead and keep your data. See Calm Health Access: What Happens When Your Sponsorship Ends.

How to delete your account

  1. Log in and navigate to your Profile settings.

    • App: Tap the Profile tab in the bottom right corner, then the Settings icon in the upper right corner.
    • Website: Log in to the Calm Health website, click the Profile tab in the top navigation bar, then click Settings.

  2. Scroll to the bottom and tap or click Delete Account. 

  3. You’ll see an option to download your Health Summary data first. 

  4. Confirm your deletion request by clicking Delete Account again at the bottom of the screen.

    • Note: To cancel your deletion request at this point, click the X in the top right corner of your screen.

For more information, see Deleting or Accessing your Personal Information.

Questions About GDPR-Related Privacy Practices

We take privacy seriously here at Calm and are happy to provide a specific avenue for our EEA residents to ask questions about this topic.

For inquiries by EEA residents and supervisory authorities related to Calm’s privacy practices or your data subject rights in relation to The General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR), please email ****.

Your Privacy Choices: Opting Out of Targeted Advertising

Calm uses cookies and similar tracking technologies to operate our services. We also allow our advertising partners to use such technologies, which enables us to promote Calm through ads on other websites and services. In some cases, we may share your email address in a de-identified format for similar advertising purposes.

Under certain privacy laws, these activities may be considered “sales,” “sharing,” or “targeted advertising” — and you may have the right to opt out.

How to opt out

You can exercise your opt-out right through either of the following methods:

  • Web browser: Visit calm.com/optout and follow the prompts.
  • Mobile app: Go to Settings > Your Privacy Choices and follow the prompts.
Still have questions?

If you have additional questions about your privacy choices or how Calm uses your data, please reach out to our support team — we’re happy to help.

  5. Congrats! Your premium access to Calm will automatically activate for the Calm account you used during Step 4.

Calm through Apple Podcasts

For a limited time, Calm is offering a 7-day free trial to Calm Premium for Apple Podcasts users. 

I am a new Calm userwanting to subscribe to Calm via Apple Podcasts.

You can sign up for a Calm Premium subscription via Apple Podcasts.

New users are able to redeem a 7-day free trial by following the steps below:

  1. Search for “Calm” in the Apple Podcasts app and select the Calm Channel or one of our shows.
  2. At the top-center of the channel, there will be a “Get Calm Premium” button and text which says “Already a subscriber?”. You will also be able to find the button if you click on any of our exclusive episodes.
  3. Once you click on “Get Calm Premium”, you will be prompted to the App Store page for Calm. Upon downloading and opening the app,
    • You can create an account using your First Name, Email, and by creating a password.
    • You also have the option to select from creating an account with Facebook, Apple, or Google Sign in.
  4. Once your account is created, confirm which subscription type you are interested in. You have the option to select a Yearly or Monthly subscription. If on a mobile device, select Continue when you’ve selected a subscription type.
  5. Select your payment method. Paypal, Apple Pay, Google Pay are available to choose from. You can also enter your credit or debit card information. When you’re ready, select Start Your Trial (if eligible) or Subscribe.
  6. Welcome to Calm Premium! Exclusive episodes on Apple Podcasts will now be available to play and the Calm channel will be automatically added to your Apple Podcasts Library.

I am an existing Calm user wanting to link my Calm account to Apple Podcasts.Existing Calm Premium users have the ability to link Calm to Apple Podcasts. To link your accounts, follow the steps below: 

Existing Calm subscribers via App Store

  1. Upon opening the Apple Podcasts app, your subscription will be automatically connected. 
  2. Congrats! Your Calm Premium subscription will be automatically recognized and linked to the exclusive content within the Apple Podcasts app, unlocking the Subscriber Edition.

Existing Calm subscribers via Web or Android

  1. Search for “Calm” in the Apple Podcasts app and select the Calm Channel or one of our shows.
  2. At the top-center of the channel, there will be a “Get Calm Premium” button and text which says “Already a subscriber?”. You will also be able to find the button if you click on any of our exclusive episodes.
  3. Once you click “Already a subscriber?”, it will open a web view within the Apple Podcasts app. 
  4. Log into your Calm account,
    • If you created your account in the past using the email and password option, please input your credentials.
    • Otherwise, select one of the 3rd party sign in options you used when you first created your Calm account. Facebook, Apple, or Google sign in options are available.
  5. Congrats! Exclusive episodes within the show page on Apple Podcasts will now be available to play and the Calm channel will be automatically added to your Apple Podcasts Library.

Any subscriber to Calm Premium, whether they subscribe as an individual or via their employee benefits, can link their account on Apple Podcasts and listen to all the content available on that app. 

What content will be available on Apple Podcasts? What’s the difference between Calm content on Apple Podcasts and on the Calm app?

A wide selection of Calm’s Sleep Stories, meditations and mindfulness tools on Apple Podcasts are available through a variety of unique shows. We offer a large selection of free content to encourage sampling across our main content categories, though any Calm content marked “Subscription”will require a Calm subscription to gain access.

By downloading the Calm app, users can access Calm’s full range of premium content, features, and benefits, including 50,000+ hours of unique content, interactive tools like our Breathe Bubbles, personalized recommendations plus motivating features like reminders and streaks to help you maintain your progress.

I can’t find certain pieces of content on Apple Podcasts that are available on the Calm app.

For access to Calm’s entire content library, you’ll need to download the Calm mobile app or visit calm.com/app, while having an active Calm Premium subscription. 

I’ve linked my existing Calm account to Apple Podcasts but my content is still locked.

The first thing to do is make sure you are signed in to your Calm account using the same login credentials you used to activate your Calm account.

  1. In the Calm app, tap Profile in the bottom right corner, select Settings in the upper left corner, and swipe up to confirm what email was used to create your account on the bottom of the screen.
  2. Once you’ve confirmed you’ve located the account associated with your Calm Premium subscription, 
    • Existing Calm subscribers via App Store: Calm subscription must be Active subscription under your iOS settings. 
      • Apple users may have a masked email, or a Private Relay used during setup. Be sure to select the Apple sign in option when presented
    • Existing Calm subscribers via Web or Android: Make sure to link the account associated with your Calm Premium subscription

For more information on how to log in to your Calm account, visit: How to Log In and Log Out of Your Calm Account

Please note, your iOS device must be updated to the latest version. Please see 0

If you have any further trouble accessing Calm’s Premium content, please write to us here and provide a screenshot of the locked content as well as the confirmation page showing you purchased a Calm Premium subscription, along with the email address on your Calm account.

Can I link my Calm account with more than one Apple ID?

No, Calm subscribers can only link their Calm account to a single Apple ID.

How does my billing work for a Calm subscription redeemed via Apple Podcasts?

Your billing will be managed by Apple, as the purchases will be made through their App Store. 

What happens if I don’t have a Calm Premium subscription anymore?

You will no longer have access to exclusive content on Apple Podcasts, but will still be able to listen to the free Calm content.

Calm through Spotify

Calm has partnered with Spotify to provide a 1-month free trial to Calm Premium for eligible Free and Premium Tier Spotify users. Note, this offer is only available to users that haven’t redeemed a free trial in the past.

I am a new Calm user wanting to subscribe to Calm via Spotify

You can sign up for a Calm Premium subscription via Spotify’s platform if you are either a Free or Premium Tier Spotify user.

To redeem, follow the steps below or visit calm.com/spotify: 

  1. After confirming you are logged in to your Spotify account, navigate to Calm content by searching for Calm in the search bar and filtering by “podcasts & shows”.
  2. Locate a piece of content that is locked. You can identify that content by looking for the padlock icon in place of the play button.
  3. A small popup may appear, select the option Get Access
  4. Select Link Account to allow permission to link Spotify and Calm
  5. You’ll be redirected to Calm’s website where you will have the option to create an account. 
    • You can create an account using your First Name, Email, and creating a password.
    • You also have the option to select from creating an account with Facebook, Apple, or Google Sign in.
  6. Once your account is created, confirm which subscription type you are interested in. You have the option to select a Yearly or Monthly subscription. If on a mobile device, select Continue when you’ve selected a subscription type.
    • Please note: Non-US users will only have access to the Yearly subscription at this time.
  7. Select your payment method. Paypal, Apple Pay, Google Pay are available to choose from. You can also enter your credit or debit card information. When you’re ready, select Start Your Trial (if eligible) or Subscribe.
  8. Welcome to Calm Premium! Tap Download Calm to install the app. Or, select Listen on Spotify to be redirected to Spotify.

I am an existing Calm user wanting to link my Calm account to Spotify

Existing Calm Premium users have the ability to link Calm to Spotify. 

To link your accounts, follow the steps below: 

  1. After confirming you are logged in to your Spotify account, navigate to Calm content by searching for Calm in the search bar and filtering by “podcasts & shows”.
  2. Locate a piece of content that is locked. You can identify that content by looking for the padlock icon in place of the play button.
  3. A small popup may appear, select the option Get Access
  4. Select Link Account to allow permission to link Spotify and Calm. You’ll be redirected to Calm’s website where you will see the option to create an account. 
  5. Since you are already a Calm Premium user, select Log In located above the First Name form field. You’ll be redirected to the Login screen.
    • If you created your account in the past using the email and password option, please input your credentials.
    • Otherwise, select one of the 3rd party sign in options you used when you first created your Calm account. Facebook, Apple, or Google sign in options are available.
  6. If you have an active Calm subscription, you will be automatically redirected to the Spotify app where your Calm content will now be unlocked. 

What content will be available on Spotify? What’s the difference between Calm content on Spotify and on the Calm app?

A wide selection of Calm’s Sleep Stories, meditations and mindfulness tools on Spotify are available through a variety of unique shows. We offer a large selection of free content to encourage sampling across our main content categories, though any Calm content marked with a lock icon will require a Calm subscription to gain access.

By downloading the Calm app, users can access Calm’s full range of premium content, features, and benefits, including 50,000+ hours of unique content, interactive tools like our Breathe Bubbles, personalized recommendations plus motivating features like reminders and streaks to help you maintain your progress.

I can’t find certain pieces of content on Spotify that are available on the Calm app

For access to Calm’s entire content library, you’ll need to download the Calm mobile app or visit calm.com/app, while having an active Calm Premium subscription. 

I’ve linked Calm to my Spotify account but my content is still locked

The first thing to do is make sure you are signed in to your Calm account using the same login credentials you used to activate your Calm account.

  1. Start by unlinking Calm from Spotify. For more information on how to unlink Calm, visit:
  2. In the Calm app, tap Profile in the bottom right corner, select Settings in the upper left corner, and swipe up to confirm what email was used to create your account on the bottom of the screen.
  3. Once you’ve confirmed you’ve located the account associated with your Calm Premium subscription, select a locked piece of content by navigating to Calm content by searching for Calm in the search bar and filtering by “podcasts & shows”.
    • Apple users may have a masked email, or a Private Relay used during setup. Be sure to select the Apple sign in option when presented
    • For more information on how to log in to your Calm account, visit: How to Log In and Log Out of Your Calm Account

If you have any further trouble accessing Calm’s Premium content, please write to us here and provide a screenshot of the locked content as well as the confirmation page showing you purchased a Calm Premium subscription, along with the email address on your Calm account.

Lincoln retail clients in the U.S., Canada, and Mexico who own or lease a model-year 2000 or newer vehicle are eligible can redeem one-year complimentary membership to Calm Premium, with no credit card required.  Lincoln vehicles registered for commercial use and other types of Ford vehicles are not eligible for this offer.

How do I sign up for Calm as a Lincoln client?

Lincoln retail clients may sign up for a complimentary year of Calm Premium by following the steps below: 

  1. Download the Lincoln Way App to your mobile device and add your Lincoln vehicle to your garage in the Lincoln Way app.
  2. Check your Message Center in the Lincoln Way app for an invitation to Calm (this can take up to 4 business days to appear).
  3. Follow the instructions in this message and the subsequent prompts to activate your Calm Premium membership. 
  4. Using the same Calm login credentials you’ve just used to redeem your complimentary subscription, log in to the Calm app on your mobile device to access the full content of Calm.

The app is only offering me a 7-day trial. How do I get my complimentary year?

The offer for Lincoln clients must be redeemed via the invitation in your Lincoln Way app. This invitation will generate an enrollment page unique to Lincoln clients and only available by following the message within the Lincoln Way app. If you have any trouble accessing your invitation to Calm in your Lincoln Way app, please contact Lincoln Concierge by pressing the “Concierge” button within your Lincoln Way app (for iPhone users, tap “More” to find the Concierge). From here, select “Chat” or “Call” from the menu to speak with Lincoln Concierge directly.

I am supposed to receive a one-year Calm membership, but I don’t see the offer in my Lincoln Way app. How do I access it?

Lincoln clients with at least one vehicle added to their Lincoln Way app account will receive an invitation to enroll in the complimentary Calm membership.  From the Lincoln Way app, navigate to Account > Message Center to access the offer.  Please note that the Calm offer may not appear in Message Center immediately upon registering your Lincoln vehicle in the app. Your invitation should arrive within 2-4 business days of registering your vehicle in the app. If you have any trouble accessing your invitation to Calm in your Lincoln Way app, please contact Lincoln Concierge by pressing the “Concierge” button within your Lincoln Way app (for iPhone users, tap “More” to find the Concierge). From here, select “Chat” or “Call” from the menu to speak with Lincoln Concierge directly.

Will I be charged after the one-year complimentary Calm membership from Lincoln expires?

No. In the spirit of effortless experiences, Lincoln is pleased to provide access to Calm with no credit card required and no obligation to continue membership after your complimentary year expires. If you’d like to continue your Calm subscription after the free period ends, you can do so by purchasing a subscription from the offerings listed in the app or on our website here. Whether or not you choose to continue with a subscription to Calm Premium, you will be able to keep your Calm account.

I’m still having issues accessing the offer or have more questions about the Lincoln offer. What are my next steps?

Below are some frequently asked questions for those who have purchased a FitBit Sense, to help you get started with Calm.

How to redeem your Calm Premium offer:

  1. Locate your Calm offer code in your email inbox from . The subject of that email is Calm subscription redemption code.
  2. Navigate to 0
  3. Log in to your existing Calm account or sign up for a free Calm account if you don’t already have one.
  4. Enter your offer code found in your email from Fitbit.

Congrats! Your premium access to Calm will automatically activate for the Calm account you used during Step 3. Download the Calm app and log in to your Calm account.

For more information on how to download the Calm app and on what type of devices Calm can be accessed, click here.  

Will I be charged when my subscription through Fitbit ends?

No. When your 6-month Calm Premium membership expires, the premium content will become locked. At this time, you will be able to continue your access to Calm Premium by purchasing a subscription.

I can’t find my offer code from Fitbit. What do I do? 

Please note, only those who purchased their Fitbit Sense and enabled the 6-month free subscription of Fitbit Premium directly from fitbit.com, will qualify for this offer.

In order to locate your offer code for Calm, please search your email inbox for emails from . The subject of the email that contains your code is Calm subscription redemption code.

If you are unable to find this email, please locate your Fitbit Sense Order Confirmation email and confirm your eligibility for this offer. Only those whose Order Confirmation email includes both a purchase of the Fitbit Sense smartwatch and the 6-month free subscription of Fitbit Premium will receive the Calm Premium offer. 

After confirming your eligibility, if you are still unable to locate your offer code, please reach out to Fitbit’s Support team here. 

Will my mindfulness sessions on Calm sync with my Fitbit profile? 

Mindfulness sessions completed through your Calm account will not integrate with your Fitbit account. If this is something you are interested in, please let our support teams know and we’ll pass your request on to the rest of our team!

Where can I get more help? 

If you would like assistance locating your offer code for Calm, using Fitbit smartwatch, accessing your Fitbit account, please contact Fitbit’s Support team here. 

If you have any trouble activating your Calm account or using Calm’s content, please contact our support team here.

How to Redeem & Troubleshoot Calm Partner Offers (FAQ)

Calm partners with several organizations and brands to offer a variety of discounted and free trial offers.

What partner offers are currently available? To see a list of our active partnerships and discounts, please visit our guide: Promotional Offers through Calm Partners

Eligibility & Existing SubscribersCan I redeem a partner offer if I already have a paid subscription or active trial? 

  • These offers are typically available only to users who do not already have an active subscription or free trial.

What happens if I try to redeem it anyway? 

  • Ineligible users who attempt to redeem a promotional discount will receive an error message. You will not be able to redeem the offer until your current subscription or trial expires, or until you cancel it. How to Cancel Your Subscription or Free Trial

Important Note on Cancellations: 

  • When you “cancel” a subscription (such as through Apple or Google), your Premium access does not end instantly. Instead, it runs until the end of your current paid billing cycle. You must wait for that cycle to fully expire and for your account to revert to the free version before the partner link will work.

If I redeem the partner offer, will it just add time to my current subscription? 

  • No. Please be aware that the new partner subscription will begin separately at the exact time of the purchase or activation. It will NOT extend your current subscription’s expiration date.

How to Redeem Podcast Discounts

  • If you heard about a promotional discount for Calm on a podcast, you do not need a special code. To redeem a podcast discount, simply use a web browser to navigate directly to the promotional web page announced on that podcast. The web address will usually be in a format similar to: calm.com/podcastname

Troubleshooting: Locked ContentI’ve redeemed my discounted partner subscription, but the Calm Premium content is still showing up with a locked symbol. What are my next steps? 

  • If you successfully redeemed your partner subscription on the web but are still seeing locked Premium content (padlock icons) in the Calm app, it is highly likely you are signed into the wrong account. Please see this help center article to learn how to check your account and unlock your subscription: Paid For a Calm Subscription But Premium Content is Still Locked

Managing Your Subscription: Cancellations & RefundsHow do I cancel the auto-renewal of my partner free trial or subscription? 

  • For instructions on canceling your auto-renewal so you are not charged in the future, please view our help center article: How to Cancel Your Subscription or Free Trial

I was charged after my free trial ended. How do I get a refund? 

  • If you forgot to cancel and were charged, you can submit a request for a refund here.

Important Warning: If you request and receive a refund, you will lose access to the promotion. You will not be able to redeem the offer again to purchase a yearly subscription at that same discounted price in the future.

How to Redeem a Calm Partner Subscription via a Landing Page

If you receive a subscription to Calm through a partner (such as an employer or affiliated brand), you must activate it using their specific web link before using the mobile app.

What to Know Before You Begin

  • Do NOT download the app first: You will not be able to access your partner offer if you start by downloading the Calm app. The offer is only redeemable via the partner’s unique web link.

  • Need your link? Please reach out here if you are not sure what your partner link is.

  • Supported Devices: Once unlocked, you can use your subscription on iOS, Android, iPad, Apple TV, or a web browser on a computer.

Step 1: Sign Up on the Partner Landing Page

You must complete this step on a web browser before opening the Calm app.

  1. Follow the unique landing page link provided by the partner.

  2. Click the green button labeled ‘Click Here to Get Started’.

  3. You will be redirected to an account creation form.

    • New Users: Create a Calm account by entering your name, email, and a password.

    • Existing Users: Click ‘Login’ to sign in if you already have a Calm account.

  4. Submit the form. You will be taken to a confirmation screen verifying that you have successfully unlocked subscription access on your account.

Step 2: Download and Log In to the App

After you have successfully signed up through the landing page, you are ready to use the Calm app.

  1. Download the Calm app from the iTunes App Store or Google Play Store.

  2. Open the app. You will be prompted to select your goals for using the app. Select your goals and click ‘Continue’ (or tap the ‘X’ in the corner to exit this screen if you do not wish to answer).

  3. Important Warning: If at any point you see an offer for a 7-day free trial, exit the free trial page by tapping the ‘X’ in the corner. Do not click ‘Continue’ to start a free trial.

  4. Click the ‘Log in’ button.

  5. Log in using the exact same email address and password you just used in Step 1. You should now see all of the Premium content in the app unlocked.

TroubleshootingIf your content is still locked after logging in: 

Check to ensure you are using the correct account.

  1. Go to your Profile.

  2. Tap the Settings (Gear icon) in the upper left corner.

  3. Tap Account Info.

  4. Verify that you are logged in with the exact same email address you used to sign up on the partner landing page.

If you accidentally started an in-app free trial: 

If you accidentally start a 7-day free trial in the app, please be sure to cancel it right away to avoid being charged. You can read more about how to cancel based on your device here:

  • How to cancel an iOS in-app trial

  • How to cancel an Android in-app trial

What is My Pet Guru?

My Pet Guru is an interactive quiz competition that helps pet owners identify the unique ways their pets support their emotional and mental well-being.

Who is Eligible?

  • This offer is strictly available to My Pet Guru participants who are officially selected as winners of the competition.

What is Included in the Offer? Depending on the specific prize awarded, winners will receive either Annual (1-year) or Lifetime free access to Calm Premium.

How to Redeem Your Offer

If you are selected as a winner, the Mars Petcare team will contact you directly.

  1. Check your Instagram account. Winners will be contacted via Direct Message (DM) on Instagram with their official prize details.

  2. Open the DM and click the unique Calm landing page link provided to you by the Mars Petcare team.

  3. On the landing page, create a new Calm account or log in to your existing free Calm account to claim your subscription.

  4. Download the Calm app on your mobile device and log in using the exact same account details to begin listening!

Already have a paid subscription? If you currently have an active, paid Calm Premium subscription (such as through Apple or Google Play), you cannot stack your prize link on top of it. Please turn off your auto-renew, wait for your current subscription to completely expire, and then click your winning link to apply your new Annual or Lifetime access to your account!

Frequently Asked QuestionsWill I be charged when my access ends?

  • Annual Access: No. Your free access will simply expire at the end of the 1-year period, and you will not be automatically charged.

  • Lifetime Access: No. Lifetime access gives you permanent Premium entry. It will never expire and you will never be charged.

How to Redeem Calm Premium via SaskTel or Lüm Mobile

Calm has partnered with SaskTel Wireless and Lüm Mobile to provide free access to Calm Premium for eligible customers.

Who is eligible?

You are eligible to redeem this offer if you meet both of the following requirements:

  • You do not currently have an active Calm Premium subscription.

  • You are a Lüm Mobile customer, OR you are a SaskTel Wireless customer on an active postpaid or noSTRINGS plan.

Note: If you already have a paid Calm Premium subscription, you must wait for it to expire before redeeming this offer. Calm does not provide refunds for previously purchased subscriptions.

How to Redeem: SaskTel Wireless Customers

Eligible customers will receive an email or text message containing a direct sign-up link. If you did not receive the link, you can manually activate your offer by following these steps:

  1. Text the word CALM to 42256 (4CALM).

  2. You will receive an automated message asking you to confirm that you want to activate the subscription.

  3. Reply YES to the confirmation message.

  4. You will then receive a final message containing your unique sign-up link. Click the link to go to the Calm website.

  5. Create a new Calm account using a personal email address, or log in to your existing free Calm account.

  6. Download the Calm app onto your mobile device and log in using the exact same email address and password you just used to redeem the offer!

How to Redeem: Lüm Mobile Customers

Eligible customers will receive an email or text message containing a direct sign-up link. Alternatively, you can activate your offer through your Lüm account:

  1. Launch the Lüm Mobile app or log in to your myLüm online dashboard.

  2. Scroll down and click the Calm App tile.

  3. On the next screen, click Get Calm. You will be redirected to the Calm website.

  4. Create a new Calm account using a personal email address, or log in to your existing free Calm account.

  5. Download the Calm app onto your mobile device and log in using the exact same email address and password you just used to redeem the offer!

Frequently Asked QuestionsHow long does my free access last? 

  • SaskTel Wireless and Lüm Mobile customers are eligible for free access to Calm for as long as their wireless membership remains active. If you cancel or terminate your cellular service, your free access to Calm Premium will automatically end.

Purchasing Calm for a group

How to Integrate Calm for Your Organization: SSO, API, and SFTP Setup

⚠️ Important: Setting up an organization-wide integration requires administrative access to your company’s IT infrastructure. Please coordinate with your internal IT or Security team before initiating these setups to prevent deployment delays.

Welcome to Calm for Organizations! To seamlessly bring Calm’s mental wellness resources to your employees, you can choose from three secure enterprise integration methods.

Review the options below and select the technical documentation that best fits your company’s IT infrastructure:

Option 1: Partner SSO Integration

Single Sign-On (SSO) allows your employees to securely access Calm using their existing corporate credentials, eliminating the need for separate passwords.

  • Best for: Companies wanting an automated, highly secure, and friction-free login experience.

  • Access the technical guide: View Partner SSO Integration Documentation

Option 2: Partner API Integration

Our custom API allows you to build a direct, real-time connection between your internal HR systems and Calm to automate user provisioning and management.

  • Best for: Organizations with dedicated developer resources who want customized, real-time data syncing.

  • Access the technical guide: View Partner API Integration Documentation

Option 3: Partner SFTP Integration

Secure File Transfer Protocol (SFTP) allows you to securely upload recurring CSV batch files to manage employee eligibility and access.

  • Best for: Companies looking for a straightforward, automated file-drop method without complex API coding.

  • Access the technical guide: View Partner SFTP Integration Documentation

⚠️ Important Billing Note: Discounted group subscriptions are billed annually. Individual subscriptions cannot be retroactively merged into a group billing account; members must activate their group access using a unique link provided by their administrator.

Calm offers discounted group subscriptions for teams, communities, and organizations of 5 or more people. Group members receive full, unlimited access to Calm’s entire library of premium content, while administrators gain access to tools to manage the subscription seamlessly.

If your organization has between 5 and 300 members, you can purchase a group subscription directly online.

What’s Included:

How to Purchase and Learn More:

  2. Want to see how management works first? Take an Interactive Tour of the Calm Partner Portal.

  3. For specific inquiries, review our Group Subscriptions FAQ.

For Groups of 301+ People (Enterprise)

How to Request a Quote and Demo:

  1. Fill out our brief Enterprise Contact Form to schedule a live demo with a member of our Sales team.

  2. Our team typically responds to all inquiries within 48 hours.

💡 Tip: If you haven’t heard from us within 48 hours, please search your inbox and spam folder for an email ending in @calm.com.

Subscription Management

How to Cancel your Calm Sleep Subscription

This article explains how to cancel your Calm Sleep subscription, by turning off the auto-renewal feature on the same platform where you purchased (Apple App Store or Calm.com).

 

To Cancel your Calm Sleep Auto-Renewal through Apple

Calm is not able to cancel free trials or subscriptions purchased directly through Apple on your behalf. To manage your subscription through Apple, please follow the steps below:

  1. On your Apple device, open the Settings App
  2. Tap on your name located below the Search bar to access your Apple Account and iCloud
  3. Select Subscriptions
  4. Locate and select the Calm Sleep subscription listed
  5. Tap Cancel SubscriptionPlease note: Free trials activated through Apple must be cancelled at least 24 hours before the end of the trial period to avoid being charged. For more information, please visit: Cancel a subscription from Apple

 

To Cancel your Calm Sleep Auto-Renewal through Calm.com

Please note: To manage your Calm Sleep trial or subscription purchased through Calm.com, you must update your app to version 2.0.6 from the App Store. For step-by-step instructions on how to manually update an app on your iOS device, please see this guide from Apple. 

  1. Launch the Calm Sleep app
  2. Go to My Library > Settings > Manage Subscription
  3. Click “To make changes to your subscription, please visit Stripe”.
  4. Input your email address to receive a sign-in link for the Stripe Customer Portal
  5. View your Calm Sleep subscription and tap Cancel Subscription

Requesting a Refund for your Calm Sleep Subscription

Refunds are handled by the platform where you purchased your Calm Sleep subscription. This article explains how to request refund from the Apple App Store or Calm.com. 

Please note: This article details the refund request process for our latest offering, Calm Sleep. For regular Calm app refunds, please see: Receiving a Refund.

Requesting a Refund from Apple

Calm Sleep subscription purchases made through your Apple account will show on your bank statement from apple.com/bill. To request a refund, follow the steps below:

  1. Sign in to reportaproblem.apple.com
  2. Select I’d like to, then choose Request a refund
  3. Choose the reason why you want a refund, then choose Next
  4. Choose the Calm Sleep app or subscription shown, then choose Submit
  5. Be sure to cancel your subscription. Please see: How to Cancel your Calm Sleep Subscription
Apple Rejected my Refund Request

In the case Apple rejects your refund request, we advise you to contact your bank for further assistance. Apple has restricted access to manage subscriptions or refunds. Calm is unable to provide any support on these request types.

 

Requesting a Refund from Calm.com

Calm Sleep subscription purchases made through the Calm.com website will show on your bank statement from Calm.com. Please reach out to our support team here to submit your refund request.

We are usually able to accommodate refund requests made within 30 days of your purchase. Full Terms & Conditions for Calm subscription purchases can be found here.

Understanding the Calm App and Calm Sleep App Subscriptions

Please note that subscriptions for the Calm App and Calm Sleep App are completely separate.

A subscription purchased in one app cannot be applied to or restored in the other. Each app operates independently with its own subscription model and features.

For more information about both apps and what each subscription includes, please visit Available Calm Apps.

Technical Support

Calm Health Login Issues After Registering: How to Access Your Sponsored Account

This is a troubleshooting guide for login issues with the invitation-only Calm Health app.

If you’ve previously registered for Calm Health through your employer, health plan, or organization but are having login issues, are unable to access the full experience, or you’re being asked for an access code again, you’re likely logged into a different account than the one that has your sponsorship attached. This guide will help you identify the correct account and get back into Calm Health.

See also: Calm Health Redemption Errors: What They Mean and How to Resolve Them.

Looking for support for the regular Calm app? See here! 

Why This Happens

This usually occurs when:

  • You’re logged into a different account than the one used to register for Calm Health (for example, a work email instead of a personal one, or vice versa).
  • You used a different login method (such as Google, Apple, Facebook, or email) than the one you originally selected.
  • Your sponsorship has already been redeemed, and you’re trying to go through the registration process again.

Step 1: Confirm the login method you used

Search your inboxes (personal, work, or school) for a welcome or confirmation email from Calm Health. This email will confirm the email address associated with your account.

Apple users: If you signed in with Apple, you may have used the “Hide My Email” feature, which routes Calm Health emails to a randomized address forwarding to your Apple ID inbox. See Apple’s guide on managing Hide My Email addresses for more details. 

Step 2: Log into the correct account

  • Log out of the Calm Health app or website.
  • Log back in using the email address confirmed above (or social login method)
  • Once logged in with the correct account, you should see sponsored content and your personalized Plan immediately.

Step 3: If you’re still unsure which account is correct

  • Search all inboxes (personal, work, school) for emails from Calm Health.
  • Try logging in with any email addresses you commonly use.
  • Try each login option (email, Apple, Google) on the Calm Health app to see which one unlocks your Plan.

 

Still need help?

Please include your account email(s), a description of the issue, and any relevant screenshots.

  • General support: Submit a request here
  • UHC/Optum member support: Submit a request here

Calm Health Redemption Errors: What They Mean and How to Resolve Them

This article covers the most common errors you may encounter while redeeming your Calm Health sponsorship, what they mean, and how to resolve them.

“Your Eligibility Has Not Been Confirmed”

This error appears at the eligibility screen and means there may be an issue with the details you entered.

Steps to resolve:

  1. Double-check your eligibility details for any typos, including your name, date of birth, or member ID.
  2. Make sure you are entering the details exactly as they appear on your insurance member card or invitation email.
  3. Tap Submit to try again.

If you continue to receive this error, reach out to your HR or benefits administrator, or your health plan provider, to confirm you are eligible for this benefit.

 

“Unable to Verify Sponsorship”

This error means your eligibility details are already associated with an existing Calm Health account.

Steps to resolve:

  1. Check your email inbox for a message from Calm Health with details about your existing account, including the login method you originally used to register.
  2. Return to the Calm Health app or website and log in using the sign-in method shown in that email.

 

“We Couldn’t Send the SMS. Please Try Again Later”

This error appears during Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA) setup and means there may be an issue with the phone number you entered. Make sure you are entering a valid phone number for your region and try again.

 

“App Not Available in Your Country or Region”

This error appears when downloading the Calm Health app and means the app may not be available in your country or region. Calm Health is not currently available in all countries.

 

 

Still need help?

Please include your account email, a description of the error message, and any relevant screenshots.

  • General support: Submit a request here
  • UHC/Optum member support: Submit a request here

Please include your account email, a description of the issue, and any relevant screenshots.

Calm Health Technical Issues: How to Resolve App or Website Crashes, Freezing, and Slow Loading

If you’re experiencing crashes, freezing, or loading issues with the Calm Health app or website, follow these steps in order to get back to your practice.

Having trouble redeeming your Calm Health sponsorship? See Calm Health Redemption Errors: What They Mean and How to Resolve Them instead.

Calm Health App

Step 1: Check YourConnection& VPNs

  • Switch networks: Try switching from WiFi to cellular data (or vice versa). If the app suddenly works on cellular, the issue is likely your WiFi network. Resetting your home router may help Calm Health reconnect to our servers. 

  • Test other apps: Open another app that requires internet (like a browser or YouTube) to check if your device has lost connection entirely. 

  • Disable VPNs: Some VPNs can interfere with the connections required to load Calm Health’s servers. Temporarily turn these off and try again.

Step 2: Force Quit & Refresh

Sometimes the app just needs a fresh start to clear a temporary glitch.

  • Force quit: Close the Calm Health app completely and relaunch it.
  • Log out & log in: Log out of your account, then log back in using the original method you signed up with. This refreshes your account and subscription data.
Step 3: Check for App Updates

Bugs and loading issues are often fixed in our newest app versions.

  • Open the App Store (iOS) or Google Play Store (Android).
  • Search for “Calm Health” and check for an available Update. Make sure you’re running the latest version.
Step 4: Reinstall the Calm App

If the steps above didn’t work, deleting and reinstalling the app is the best way to clear out corrupted files.

  • Before you delete: Reinstalling won’t cancel your access or delete your account data, but it will remove any downloaded offline content. You’ll need to re-download saved sessions after reinstalling.
  • How to reinstall: Delete the app, restart your device, then download Calm Health again from your device’s app store.
  • Log back in: Use your original account details to log back in.

Calm Health Website

If you’re experiencing issues with the Calm Health website, try the following:

  • Check your connection: Test another website, try switching networks, and disable any VPNs, which can interfere with Calm Health’s servers.
  • Log out and back in: This refreshes your subscription and account status, which can resolve display issues that look like a loading problem.
  • Try incognito or a different browser: This helps rule out an interfering browser extension or a browser-specific issue. We recommend the latest version of Google Chrome or Safari.
  • Clear your cache and cookies: Set the timeframe to All Time rather than the default, then reload the page.  
Still need help?

Please include your account email, a description of the issue, and any relevant screenshots.

  • General support: Submit a request here
  • UHC/Optum member support: Submit a request here

Please include your account email, a description of the issue, and any relevant screenshots.

Calm Sleep App is Not Working

How to Fix Issues with the Calm Sleep App (iOS)

Which app is this for?
  • Calm Sleep (Standalone App): Use the steps below.
  • Calm: These steps may not apply. Please see The App Isn’t Working - Troubleshooting Steps.

Step 1: Check for Updates Before reinstalling, ensure you are on the latest version. See: How to Update the Calm Sleep App

  1. Open the App Store.
  2. Tap your profile icon (top right).
  3. Scroll down to Calm Sleep. If it says “Update,” tap it.

Step 2: Restart Your Device A quick restart can clear temporary glitches.

  • Power off your iPhone/iPad completely for 1-2 minutes.
  • Turn it back on and open the app.

Step 3: Delete & Reinstall (Crucial) Note: You must DELETE the app, not just “Offload” it. See Delete apps on your iOS device

  1. Long-press the Calm Sleep app icon on your home screen.
  2. Tap Remove App.
  3. Tap Delete App (Do NOT choose “Remove from Home Screen”).
  4. Go to the App Store, search for Calm Sleep, and tap the cloud icon to reinstall.

Step 4: Check Your Connection

  • Disable VPNs: Active VPNs can block media streaming. Turn off your VPN and try again.
  • Switch Networks: If you are on Wi-Fi, try switching to Mobile Data (or vice versa) to see if the issue is with your internet router.

How to Find Your Calm App Version

When troubleshooting a technical issue or contacting our support team, you may be asked which version of the Calm app you are currently running. You can find this information directly inside your app settings, or via your device’s app store if the app will not open.

Method 1: Inside the Calm App Settings

If you can successfully open the app, this is the easiest way to find your version:

  1. Open the Calm app.

  2. Tap Profile from the main menu.

  3. Tap the Settings (gear icon) in the upper left corner of the screen.

  4. Scroll down to the very bottom of the Settings menu.

  5. Your current app version will be displayed at the bottom of the screen (for example, it will look something like “Version 6.95 (36748)”).

Method 2: Via Your Device’s App Store (If the App is Crashing)

If your app is crashing, freezing, or stuck on a loading screen, you may not be able to access your Profile settings. Instead, you can check your version using your app store:

  • Apple App Store (iOS): Open the App Store and search for “Calm.” Tap the Calm app icon to view the main app page. Scroll down to the What’s New section, which will display the most recent version number. (Note: If the blue button at the top of the page says “Update,” you are currently running an older version).

  • Google Play Store (Android): Open the Google Play Store and search for “Calm.” Tap the Calm app icon to view the details page. Scroll down and tap About this app, then scroll down to the bottom of the page to find the Version number listed under the “App info” section.

Need to update your app?

If you discover your app is out of date, or if you simply want to make sure you have the newest features and bug fixes, you can easily update it. Please follow the instructions in our update guide here How to Update the Calm App (iOS & Android).

How to Fix Calm App Crashes, Freezing, or Loading Issues (Troubleshooting)

If you are experiencing crashes, freezing, or endless loading screens with the Calm app, follow these steps in order to resolve the issue and get back to your practice.

Step 1: Check Your Connection & VPNs

  • Switch networks: Try switching your phone from WiFi to Cellular data (or vice versa). If the app suddenly works on Cellular, the issue is likely your WiFi network. Resetting your home router may help Calm reconnect to our servers.

  • Test other apps: Try opening another app on your device that requires internet (like YouTube or your web browser) to narrow down if your phone has lost connection entirely.

  • Disable VPNs or Ad Blockers: Some VPNs and mobile ad blockers block the specific connections required to load Calm’s media servers. Temporarily turn these off in your phone’s settings and try playing your content again.

Step 2: Force Quit & Refresh

Sometimes the app just needs a fresh start to clear a temporary glitch.

  • Force Quit: Close the Calm app completely (by swiping up and away from your screen) and relaunch it.

  • Log Out & Log In: Tap Profile > Settings (Gear Icon) > Log Out, and then log back in to refresh your subscription status. For a step-by-step guide on logging back in, please see our login guide here: How to Log In and Log Out of Your Calm Account

Step 3: Check for App Updates

Bugs and crashing issues are often fixed in our newest app versions.

  • Open the App Store (iOS) or Google Play Store (Android) on your device.

  • Search for “Calm” and see if there is an Update button available. Ensure you are running the latest version.

Step 4: Reinstall the Calm App

If the steps above did not work, deleting and reinstalling the app is the best way to clear out corrupted files.

  • ⚠️ Important Warning Before Deleting: Deleting the app will not cancel your subscription or delete your account data, but it will delete any offline content you have downloaded. You will need to re-download your saved offline stories after reinstalling.

  • How to Reinstall: Delete the app from your home screen, restart your phone, and download Calm again from your device’s app store.

  • Log back in: Log in with your existing account details. If you see a promotional payment screen upon logging back in, simply look for an ‘X’ or ‘Restore Purchase’ to dismiss it and return to your home screen.

Still having trouble?

1. Check for Known Outages

Check our Recently Resolved Technical Issues article. If you are experiencing a known, widespread issue listed there, we may already have a fix available! Ensure your app is updated to the version specified in the article.

2. Contact Calm Support

If you have tried all the steps above and your app is still crashing or freezing, please reach out to our support team here.

To help us solve your issue as quickly as possible, please include the following in your message:

  • Your Account Email: The exact email address associated with your Calm account.

  • A Description of the Issue: A brief explanation of exactly what is happening (e.g., “The app crashes as soon as I tap a Sleep Story”).

  • Screenshots/Screen Recordings: If you are seeing a specific error message, please attach a screenshot!

How to Fix Calm Audio Stopping or Pausing During a Session

If the Calm app unexpectedly stops playing during a session, it is usually due to a brief internet interruption, a conflicting notification, or your device’s battery settings restricting the app from running in the background when your screen is locked.

To ensure the Calm app plays your content without interruption, please follow the general troubleshooting and device-specific steps below.

Step 1: Device-Specific Battery & Background Settings

If your audio only stops when your phone screen turns off or locks, your device is likely forcing the app to “sleep” to save battery. Here is how to fix it:

For Apple/iOS Devices (iPhone & iPad):

  1. Open your device Settings > General > Background App Refresh.

  2. Ensure the main toggle at the top is ON.

  3. Scroll down the list of apps to Calm and ensure its individual toggle is also switched ON.

For Android Devices:

Android devices frequently aggressively restrict battery usage for background apps.

  1. Open your device Settings > Apps > Calm.

  2. Tap Battery (or Battery usage) and select Unrestricted (or Not optimized). This ensures the system doesn’t stop Calm while it’s running in the background.

  3. Go back to the Calm app info menu and tap Mobile data.

  4. Ensure Background data usage is toggled ON, and toggle Unrestricted data usage (or Allow data usage while Data saver is on) to ON.

  5. Finally, scroll down to Manage app if unused (or Pause app activity if unused) and toggle this OFF.

Step 2: Check for Conflicting Apps & Notifications

Sometimes Calm stops playing because your phone receives a notification (like a text message chime, alarm, or social media auto-play video) that temporarily “steals” the audio focus and pauses your session.

  • Pro Tip: Use Do Not Disturb. We highly recommend putting your phone on Do Not Disturb or a specific Sleep Focus mode while listening to Sleep Stories or Meditations. This prevents background notifications from interrupting or pausing your audio.

Step 3: Check for Corrupted Downloads

If the issue happens specifically with sessions you have downloaded for offline listening (like a Sleep Story stopping in the middle of the night), the download file may be corrupt.

  1. In the Calm app, tap Profile > Settings (gear icon) > Downloads.

  2. Select the content type and tap to Delete the downloaded sessions.

  3. Try playing the session again via streaming, or re-download it while connected to a strong, stable Wi-Fi signal.

Step 4: Check Your Network & Refresh the App

  • Switch networks: A stable connection is required to stream smoothly. Try switching from WiFi to Cellular data to see if the pausing stops. If it does, resetting your home WiFi router may help.

  • Disable VPNs or ad blockers: These can interfere with Calm’s servers. Temporarily turn these off and try playing content again.

  • Update: Check the App Store or Google Play Store to ensure you are on the latest version of Calm. Note: This will remove your downloaded sessions, but your stats and history are safely saved to your account).

  • Force Quit & Reinstall: Close the app completely and reopen it. If the issue persists, delete the app, restart your phone, and redownload Calm. (Note: Deleting the app will remove your offline downloads, but your history and subscription will remain safe).

Troubleshooting for AirPods Users

If you are using newer Apple AirPods (such as AirPods 4 or Pro 2) running iOS 26+, your device may be automatically pausing the audio because it detects that your head has stopped moving and assumes you have fallen asleep.

  • To disable this: With your AirPods connected, go to your phone’s Settings > Bluetooth. Tap the (i) icon next to your AirPods name. Scroll down to Pause Media When Falling Asleep and toggle it OFF.

Still having trouble?

If you have tried all the steps above and your audio is still cutting out, please reach out to our support team here.

To help us solve your issue as quickly as possible, please include:

  • Account Email: The email address associated with your Calm account.

  • Description of the issue: Exactly when the audio stops (e.g., “It stops 5 minutes into every Sleep Story” or “It stops as soon as my screen locks”).

  • Troubleshooting steps taken: Let us know what steps you have already attempted from this list so we don’t ask you to repeat them!
