Troubleshooting: What to do if the Calm website isn’t working

If you’re experiencing issues with loading, playback, or crashing while using the Calm website, follow these steps in order to get back to your practice.

1. Check your internet connection

  • Test other websites: Try opening another website in your browser to help narrow down the issue to a network or Calm-specific issue.

  • Switch connections: If possible, try switching from WiFi to a mobile hotspot (or vice versa).

  • Reset your router: If you discover the issue occurs only while you are on WiFi, resetting your router may help Calm reconnect to the server.

  • Disable VPNs: Some VPNs can interfere with Calm’s media servers. Temporarily turn these off and try playing content again.

2. Refresh your browser

  • Refresh the page: Use the refresh button or 0{path-to-node=“7,0,0” index-in-node=“44”} (Mac) / 1{path-to-node=“7,0,0” index-in-node=“60”} (Windows) to reload the site.

  • Clear Cache and Cookies: Refresh your browser cache and clear your browser cookies. Make sure you update the timeframe to All-Time instead of the preselected amount of time.

  • Try Incognito mode: Open an Incognito or Private window to see if a browser extension is interfering with the site.

3. Check for browser updates

Ensure you are using the most recent version of your web browser for the best experience.

  • Update your browser: You can use this guide for instructions on how to check for updates based on the browser you are using.

  • Try a different browser: We generally recommend using the latest versions of Google Chrome or Safari.

4. Log out and back in

  • Refresh your session: Click on your Profile icon and select Log out. Log back in with your account details to refresh your subscription status and account data.

    • For step-by-step instructions, please see: How to Log In and Log Out of Your Calm Account.

Using the Calm Sleep App

Calm Sleep FAQ

This article provides answers to frequently asked questions about Calm Sleep, including how it differs from Calm, how to access the app and features, supported devices, and subscription management options.

What is Calm Sleep?

Calm Sleep is a new standalone app for deeper, more personalized sleep support. The app delivers daily plans with expert-backed tools and world-class content built to help people wind down, sleep deeply, and wake up refreshed. 

How is the Calm Sleep app different from the Calm app?

Calm and Calm Sleep are designed with different goals in mind:

  • Calm is our all-in-one wellness app. It’s designed to be your complete toolkit for mental health and well-being, with resources that support stress management, focus, mindfulness, and sleep. 
  • Calm Sleep is our app designed specifically for sleep. The entire app experience, from the way the app is structured to the tools it highlights and the journey it guides you through, is focused on helping you get better sleep.

Calm is built to support a more holistic wellness experience. Calm Sleep is for those who want a dedicated sleep companion. For more information on Calm’s available apps, please see: Available Calm Apps.

I am a Calm Premium subscriber. Can I use Calm Sleep, too?

As a Calm Premium subscriber, you’ll need to purchase Calm Sleep Premium separately to access all features.

How can I download Calm Sleep?

The Calm Sleep app can be downloaded for free on Apple devices (iPhone or iPad) from the App Store.

Please note: Calm Sleep is currently only supported on Apple devices running iOS 16 or later.

If I already have a Calm account, do I need a separate username and password to access Calm Sleep?

No, you do not need two separate login credentials (username and password) to access your Calm and Calm Sleep accounts. You can use the same Calm username and password to access Calm Sleep.

How do I share my feedback or suggest a feature?

We would love to hear from you! Email us with feature enhancements or app feedback by filling out this form.

What languages is Calm Sleep available in?

Calm Sleep is available in English only.

Will my stats, favorites, and streaks in the Calm app sync with my Calm Sleep app?

The Calm app and the Calm Sleep app are separate offerings, and as such, you will not be able to share your existing Calm app data with the Calm Sleep app. Additionally, streaks, favorites, and session history from Calm will not carry over to the Calm Sleep app and vice versa.

How do I receive a refund?

Please refer to our Receiving a Refund article.

Connecting Health Data to Calm Sleep

To connect your Apple Health Data to Calm Sleep:

  1. Open the Health app on your Apple device
  2. Tap your Profile in the top right corner
  3. Locate the Privacy section, and select Apps
  4. Locate and select the Calm Sleep option
  5. Select Turn On All

Find Recently Played Content in the Calm Sleep app

To find your Recently Played content:

  1. Tap My Library located on the lower navigation bar of the app
  2. On the My Library screen, you will see two sections: My Favorites and Recently Played
  3. Next to Recently Played, tap See All to view all of your Recently Played content.

How to Download the Calm Sleep app

The Calm Sleep app can be downloaded for free on your Apple devices (iPhone, iPad) from the App Store by searching for “Calm Sleep.”

You can also download the app directly through the following link:

iPhone: 0 

 

Please note: Calm Sleep is only supported on Apple devices running iOS 18+ and above. Android devices are not currently supported.

Managing Calm Sleep Notifications

To enable/disable Calm Sleep notifications:

  1. Tap My Library located on the lower navigation bar of the app
  2. Tap the Settings ⚙️ icon in the top right corner
  3. Next, select Notifications located under Preferences
  4. Tap the toggle to enable/disable Notifications

Managing Haptics and Vibrations in the Calm Sleep app

To manage the Haptics and Vibrations:

  1. Tap My Library located on the lower navigation bar of the app
  2. Tap the Settings ⚙️ icon in the top right corner
  3. Next, select Haptics and Vibrations located under Preferences
  4. Tap the toggle to enable/disable Haptics

Setting a Sleep Schedule in the Calm Sleep app

To set your Sleep Schedule:

  1. Tap My Library located on the lower navigation bar of the app
  2. Tap the Settings ⚙️ icon in the top right corner
  3. Next, select Sleep Schedule located under Preferences
  4. To set your schedule:
    • Drag the moon 🌙 icon to set your Bedtime
    • Drag the sun ☀️ icon to set your Wake Uptime
  5. Tap Update when you’re done

Using the Discover tab in the Calm Sleep app

In the Calm Sleep app, the Discover tab helps you quickly explore our content library. This article shows you how to search for sessions or topics, and browse categories such as Sleep Stories, Meditations, Soundscapes, and Music.

To search and locate content:

  1. Tap Discover located on the lower navigation bar of the app
  2. Tap on the Search bar and type in the topic of your choice, and press Search on your keyboard
  3. A list of relevant programs will appear for you to browse through and select

To browse content by category:

  1. Tap Discover located on the lower navigation bar of the app
  2. Tap on the category button below the search bar to browse Sleep Stories, Meditations, Soundscapes, and Music.
  3. A list of relevant programs will appear for you to browse through and select

Using the Home tab in the Calm Sleep app

The Hometab displays your Sleep Readiness bar, along with Your Day and Your Evening tasks.

As you check off your expert-backed tasks, your Sleep Readiness bar will progress, showing how ready your mind and body are for rest. It’s a simple way to see how the little things you do during the day add up to easier, deeper sleep at night.

Once it’s full, you’ll see personalized recommendations in the ‘Ready to Sleep?’ section at the bottom of the Home tab.

Each morning, your bar resets, giving you a fresh start and an easy way to track your progress toward better sleep.

Using the Insights tab in the Calm Sleep app

The Calm Sleep app allows you to track your nightly rest through Duration, Quality, and Check-in data. This article explains how sleep data is imported from Apple Health and how to use daily Check-ins to record and reflect on your sleep patterns.

Duration & Quality

Duration and Quality data are imported via Apple Health. A wearable device, such as an Apple Watch, must be worn while sleeping to capture additional sleep insights.

 

 

For information on how to connect the Calm Sleep app to Apple Health, please see: Connecting Health Data to Calm Sleep

Check-In

You can view the current week’s Check-in data in the Insights tab. Check-ins appear once per day and provide a record of how well you slept the night before. You’ll be able to rate your sleep and note what helped you fall asleep or what made it difficult to get a good night’s rest.

View and Manage Favorites in the Calm Sleep app

How to favorite content:

  1. Locate the session you’d like to favorite.
  2. Tap the session to open it.
  3. Tap the heart icon to add it to your Favorites.

 

How to remove content from your Favorites:

  1. Find the favorited session
  2. Tap the heart icon again.
  3. The heart will no longer be filled, indicating it has been removed.

 

How to find your Favorites:

  1. Tap My Library, located on the lower navigation bar of the app.
  2. On the My Library screen, you will see two sections: My Favorites and Recently Played
  3. Next to My Favorites, tap See All to view all of your favorited content.

How to Join and Register for “Getting Started with Calm” Webinars

Important Note for Individual Users: These webinars are exclusively available to employees, students, or members who have access to Calm Workshops through an organization’s Calm benefit. They are not available for standard individual accounts.

Overview of Calm Webinars

The Calm Workshops team hosts live online sessions designed to help you get the most out of your Calm benefit.

  • “Getting Started with Calm” (GSC) Webinars: Held every Monday, these sessions walk you through how to navigate the Calm app and highlight key content to kickstart your wellness journey.

  • Monthly Themed Workshops: Special sessions focused on specific workplace and lifestyle topics, such as navigating change, improving work-life balance, and managing stress.

Eligibility

You can attend these webinars if you are an employee, student, or member who has access to Calm Workshops through your organization’s enterprise Calm benefit.

How to Register

Because these sessions are tailored to specific organizations, registration is managed through your internal Calm representative.

  1. Reach out to your organization’s internal HR or benefits administrator, or contact your designated Calm Customer Success Manager directly.

  2. Request the registration link for the upcoming GSC or themed webinar schedule.

  3. Once registered, check your email for the calendar invite and link to join.

How to purchase, join, or troubleshoot Calm Workshops

Important Note for Employees: Calm Workshops are organizational events. If you are an individual employee looking to join, your company must first purchase a workshop package. Please contact your HR/People Team directly to see if this benefit is available to you.

What are Calm Workshops?

Calm Workshops are tailored, interactive sessions designed to support an organization’s mental health strategy. These workshops focus on delivering high-quality mental health education and actionable skill-building for your group or workforce.

How to purchase Calm Workshops for your organization

If you are an HR leader or manager looking to bring Calm Workshops to your team, please follow these steps:

  1. Contact our sales team directly at sales@calm.com to inquire about package options.

  2. Await a response from a sales representative, who will typically follow up within 48 hours.

Missing our reply? If you haven’t seen an email from us after 48 hours, please search your inbox and your Spam/Junk folder for any messages ending in @calm.com.
